Method: 提出Mass-Aware Attention（MAA），将标准注意力的L1归一化推广到Lp族。在重复模式下，MAA使分子和分母以不同速率缩放，从而在表征幅度中保留有效贡献输入的数量信息。p=1时退化为标准注意力，无需额外监督、参数、隐藏维度或显式计数特征。

Result: 在4个连续时间动态图模型和3个数据集上，MAA在11/12个模型-数据集组合中提升了未来链接AUC；线性恢复能力平均提升4.49%；偏好依附恢复在族级校正后12/12组合均有提升。在MTPP、TKG、RAG、STPP四个领域中观察到一致证据。NLL在MTPP中改善，TKG中排序基本保持，RAG中额外信息未改善诊断头，STPP中下游LayerNorm可消除信号。

Conclusion: MAA作为一种通用的归一化原则，通过控制标准注意力中的重复不变性，有效提升了面向预测器的表征信息丰富度。信息可访问性与任务效用仍然存在区别，MAA在多个领域展现出广泛适用性。

Original Abstract: High task performance does not show whether a model retains prediction-relevant structural information in its internal representation. Temporal graph models, for example, can achieve high future-link AUC while basic graph statistics remain difficult to recover from the same representation. We identify one source of this gap in the weighted averaging used by standard attention: when an evidence pattern is repeated, the numerator and denominator grow at the same rate, so inputs with different amounts of accumulated evidence can produce the same aggregate.
  We propose Mass-Aware Attention (MAA), which generalizes standard L1 normalization to an Lp family. Under repetition, MAA makes the numerator and denominator scale at different rates, retaining the effective number of contributing inputs in the representation magnitude. It adds no supervision, parameters, hidden dimensions, or explicit count features, and recovers standard attention at p=1.
  Across four continuous-time dynamic graph models and three datasets, MAA improves future-link AUC in 11 of 12 model-dataset cells. Linear recovery from the same hidden representation increases by 4.49% on average, and preferential-attachment recovery improves in all 12 cells after family-wise correction. We also observe consistent evidence in marked temporal point processes, temporal knowledge graphs, retrieval-augmented generation, and spatio-temporal point processes. Information accessibility and task utility remain distinct: NLL improves in MTPP, ranking is largely preserved in TKG, additional information in RAG does not improve the diagnostic head, and downstream LayerNorm can erase the signal in STPP. These results position MAA as a general normalization principle for improving predictor-facing representation informativeness by controlling repetition invariance in standard attention.

中文摘要翻译: 高任务性能并不能表明模型在其内部表征中保留了与预测相关的结构信息。例如，时序图模型可以在未来链接预测上取得高AUC，但从同一表征中仍难以恢复基本的图统计量。我们识别出这一问题的一个来源在于标准注意力使用的加权平均：当某种证据模式重复出现时，分子和分母以相同速率增长，因此累积证据量不同的输入可能产生相同的聚合结果。我们提出质量感知注意力（Mass-Aware Attention, MAA），将标准L1归一化推广到Lp族。在重复模式下，MAA使分子和分母以不同速率缩放，从而在表征幅度中保留有效贡献输入的数量信息。该方法无需额外监督、参数、隐藏维度或显式计数特征，且在p=1时退化为标准注意力。在四个连续时间动态图模型和三个数据集上，MAA在11/12个模型-数据集组合中提升了未来链接预测AUC。从同一隐藏表征的线性恢复能力平均提升4.49%，且在族级校正后偏好依附恢复在全部12个组合中均有所改善。我们还在标记时序点过程、时序知识图谱、检索增强生成和时空点过程中观察到一致的证据。信息可访问性与任务效用仍然存在区别：MTPP中负对数似然改善，TKG中排序基本保持，RAG中额外信息未改善诊断头，STPP中下游LayerNorm可能消除该信号。这些结果将MAA定位为一种通用的归一化原则，通过控制标准注意力中的重复不变性来提升面向预测器的表征信息丰富度。

</details>


### [203] [Optimizing Transformer Neural Network for Real-Time Outlier Detection on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22786)
*Ilia Sobakinskikh,Paul Alexander Bilokon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了如何高效优化Transformer神经网络的推理时间，并将其应用于金融时间序列的实时异常检测。通过将不同Transformer架构部署在FPGA板（PYNQ-Z2）上，实现了低延迟的异常检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 金融时间序列数据常含有错误或异常值，影响下游任务的有效性和稳定性；同时数据量激增，需要在准确性和处理速度上更好的数据清洗方法。此外，Transformer在时序建模中表现出色，但推理效率有待提升。

Method: 探索不同Transformer架构用于时间序列建模，并研究如何在FPGA板（PYNQ-Z2）上高效实现这些架构以最小化延迟，用于金融时间序列的异常检测。

Result: 将不同Transformer架构高效部署在FPGA上，实现了低延迟的金融时间序列实时异常检测。

Conclusion: FPGA可以高效加速Transformer推理，适用于金融时间序列的实时异常检测场景。

Original Abstract: In this work, we explore how the inference time of a Transformer Neural Network can be efficiently optimized with applications to real-time anomaly detection in financial time series. The financial time series are price series such as asset prices. Unfortunately, the data is often with errors or outliers that make the downstream data processing tasks useless, unstable or even harmful. Moreover, the amount of financial time-series data has been significantly increasing. Hence, there is a need for better data-cleaning methods in terms of accuracy and in terms of processing speed.
  Transformers as a neural network architecture have achieved superior performances in many tasks such as Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. Time series modelling and especially anomaly detection tasks can benefit from the features of transformers architecture in multiple ways, including the capacity to capture long-range dependencies and interactions.
  Increasingly powerful hardware, such as field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), have seen increasing usage in recent years due to their reconfigurability and high performance. They can be efficiently utilized to speed up the computations of the Transformer architecture.
  We explore different Transformer architectures for time series modelling and how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board (PYNQ-Z2). In particular, we examine the application of Transformers to detect anomalies in time series and we show how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board to minimize latency.
  The code is available at https://github.com/thxi/icl_thesis

中文摘要翻译: 在这项工作中，我们探索了如何高效优化Transformer神经网络的推理时间，并将其应用于金融时间序列的实时异常检测。金融时间序列指的是资产价格等价格序列。不幸的是，这类数据常常含有错误或异常值，使得下游数据处理任务变得无用、不稳定甚至有害。此外，金融时间序列数据的量级正在显著增长。因此，在准确性和处理速度方面，需要更好的数据清洗方法。
  Transformer作为一种神经网络架构，在自然语言处理和计算机视觉等许多任务中取得了优越的性能。时间序列建模，尤其是异常检测任务，可以从Transformer架构的多个特性中受益，包括捕获长距离依赖和交互的能力。
  FPGA等日益强大的硬件，因其可重构性和高性能而在近年来得到越来越多的应用。它们可以高效地用于加速Transformer架构的计算。
  我们探索了用于时间序列建模的不同Transformer架构，以及如何在FPGA板（PYNQ-Z2）上高效实现这些架构。具体而言，我们研究了Transformer在检测时间序列异常方面的应用，并展示了如何将它们高效地部署在FPGA板上以最小化延迟。
  代码可在 https://github.com/thxi/icl_thesis 获取。

</details>


### [204] [FMOPF: Latent Flow Matching with Constraint-Aware Interaction Priors for AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22788)
*Zhilin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMOPF框架，通过解耦压缩与生成（使用潜在流匹配）以及显式建模负荷-状态耦合（使用约束感知交互先验网络），来解决扩散模型在生成可行近优OPF解时存在的质量问题和大规模可扩展性不足。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着可再生能源渗透率加深，电力市场运营不仅需要单一最优调度解，还需要可行近优解的分布特征用于风险量化、灵敏度分析和多目标权衡评估。现有监督神经网络只能提供点预测，扩散模型在原始状态空间中生成质量差且无法扩展到中型以上系统。

Method: 提出FMOPF框架，包含两个关键设计：(1) 通过潜在流匹配（latent flow matching）将高维OPF解流形的压缩与条件生成解耦；(2) 设计约束感知交互先验网络（Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network）显式建模负荷与状态之间的耦合关系。

Result: 在四个IEEE测试系统上，FMOPF提供了最有效的牛顿-拉夫逊热启动，在生成方法中实现了最低的尾部风险，并且是首个能扩展到数百节点系统同时保持完全可行性的生成方法。消融实验证实潜在生成流程是物理可行性的必要条件，交互先验作为后期尾部风险控制器。

Conclusion: 将高维解流形压缩与条件生成解耦，并显式建模负荷-状态耦合，是生成高质量可行OPF解的关键。

Original Abstract: AC optimal power flow determines the minimum-cost generation dispatch under nonlinear power balance constraints and is solved thousands of times daily in electricity market operations. Learning a direct mapping from load conditions to OPF solutions can accelerate this computation, yet with deepening renewable penetration, a single optimal dispatch is no longer sufficient. Operators require a characterization of the distribution of feasible near-optimal solutions for risk quantification, sensitivity analysis, and multi-objective trade-off assessment. Supervised neural networks provide fast point predictions but cannot capture this conditional distribution. Diffusion-based generative models can sample diverse solutions in principle, yet existing methods operating in the raw state space exhibit degraded solution quality and fail to scale beyond medium-sized systems. We identify the root cause as the conflation of two distinct tasks within a single model. Compressing the high-dimensional OPF solution manifold is one task, and learning the conditional mapping from loads to that manifold is another. This paper presents FMOPF, a framework that resolves this conflation by decoupling compression from generation through latent flow matching and by explicitly modeling load-state coupling through a Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network. Experiments on four IEEE test systems demonstrate that FMOPF provides the most effective Newton-Raphson warm starts, achieves the lowest tail risk among generative methods, and is the first such method to scale to systems with several hundred buses while preserving full feasibility. Ablation studies confirm that the latent generation pipeline is a necessary condition for physical feasibility and that the interaction prior functions as a late-stage tail-risk controller.

中文摘要翻译: 交流最优潮流在非线性功率平衡约束下确定最低成本的发电调度，在电力市场运营中每天被求解数千次。学习从负荷条件到最优潮流解的直接映射可以加速这一计算，但随着可再生能源渗透率的加深，单一的最优调度已不再足够。运营商需要可行近优解分布的刻画，用于风险量化、灵敏度分析和多目标权衡评估。监督神经网络提供快速点预测，但无法捕捉这种条件分布。基于扩散的生成模型原则上可以采样多样化的解，但现有在原始状态空间中操作的方法存在解质量下降的问题，且无法扩展到中型以上系统。我们识别出根本原因在于单个模型中混淆了两个不同的任务：压缩高维最优潮流解流形是一个任务，学习从负荷到该流形的条件映射是另一个任务。本文提出了FMOPF，一个通过潜在流匹配解耦压缩与生成，并通过约束感知交互先验网络显式建模负荷-状态耦合来解决这一混淆的框架。在四个IEEE测试系统上的实验表明，FMOPF提供了最有效的牛顿-拉夫逊热启动，在生成方法中实现了最低的尾部风险，并且是首个能扩展到数百节点系统同时保持完全可行性的此类方法。消融研究证实，潜在生成流程是物理可行性的必要条件，交互先验作为后期尾部风险控制器发挥作用。

</details>


### [205] [Multimodal Domain Generalization for Depression Detection: An Attention-Based BiLSTM Network with Domain-Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22794)
*Ali Tabaraei,Federico Simonetta,Stavros Ntalampiras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出首个不依赖患者身份的多模态抑郁症检测框架，融合领域泛化技术，通过对抗性学习去除说话者个体偏差，在Androids-Corpus数据集上达到93.2%准确率和94.2% F1分数，超越现有基准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的自动抑郁症检测方法因说话者间差异导致的领域偏移（domain shift）而泛化能力有限，缺乏不依赖患者身份的鲁棒检测方法。

Method: 提出首个患者无关的多模态抑郁症检测框架，结合双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）与模态内及跨模态注意力机制，采用片段级融合进行决策；通过引入基于域对抗训练神经网络（DANN）的梯度反转层，以对抗性方式限制模型识别个体说话者的能力，促进域不变表征学习，实现领域泛化。

Result: 在Androids-Corpus数据集上进行5折交叉验证，以MelSpec和ItalianBERT为最优基线（30秒片段），加入领域泛化后准确率提升2.5%、F1提升3.3%，最终达到93.2%准确率、93.2%精确率、96.2%召回率和94.2% F1分数，超越所有现有基准。消融实验验证了多模态融合、深度架构选择和领域泛化的贡献。

Conclusion: 多模态融合与领域泛化的结合能有效提升抑郁症检测的鲁棒性和泛化能力，患者无关框架可显著减少个体特异性偏差。

Original Abstract: Automatic depression detection with deep learning has shown promise but often suffers from limited generalization due to domain shift arising from inter-speaker variability. To address this critical issue, we present the first patient-independent multimodal depression detection framework that incorporates domain generalization (DG), jointly leveraging both acoustic and textual modalities. The proposed model integrates bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) with intra- and cross-modal attention mechanisms, accompanied by segment-level fusion for decision-making. Generalization is further enhanced by applying a gradient reversal layer inspired by Domain-Adversarial Training of Neural Networks (DANN), which promotes domain-invariant representations by adversarially limiting the model's ability to identify individual speakers, effectively reducing patient-specific bias. Conducting experiments on the Androids-Corpus dataset with a 5-fold cross-validation (CV) protocol, various pairings of audio and text feature extractors were evaluated over different segment durations, determining MelSpec and ItalianBERT as the optimal baseline at a 30-second segment duration. The addition of DG to this baseline yields a 2.5% increase in accuracy and 3.3% in F1-score, achieving 93.2% accuracy, 93.2% precision, 96.2% recall, and 94.2% F1-score, surpassing all existing benchmarks. Extensive ablation studies assess the impact of multimodal fusion, deep architectural choices, and DG, highlighting their combined contribution to robust and generalizable depression detection.

中文摘要翻译: 基于深度学习的自动抑郁症检测已展现出潜力，但由于说话者间变异导致的领域偏移，其泛化能力往往受限。为解决这一关键问题，我们提出了首个不依赖患者身份的多模态抑郁症检测框架，该框架融合了领域泛化（DG）技术，并联合利用了声学和文本两种模态。所提出的模型集成了双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）与模态内及跨模态注意力机制，并配合片段级融合进行决策。通过引入受域对抗训练神经网络（DANN）启发的梯度反转层，以对抗性方式限制模型识别个体说话者的能力，从而促进域不变表征学习，有效减少患者特异性偏差，进一步增强了泛化能力。在Androids-Corpus数据集上采用5折交叉验证（CV）协议，评估了不同音频和文本特征提取器在各种片段时长下的组合，确定MelSpec和ItalianBERT在30秒片段时长下为最优基线。在该基线基础上加入领域泛化（DG）后，准确率提升2.5%，F1分数提升3.3%，最终达到93.2%的准确率、93.2%的精确率、96.2%的召回率和94.2%的F1分数，超越了所有现有基准。广泛的消融研究评估了多模态融合、深度架构选择以及领域泛化的影响，突出了它们对实现鲁棒且可泛化的抑郁症检测的综合贡献。

</details>


### [206] [Physically Verifiable Evidence and LLM-Based Reporting for Bearing Fault Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22797)
*Yuntong Chen,Jianyu Liu,Guobin Zhao,Ziang Wang,Chao Chen,Ju Huang,Xitian Tian,Lijiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对基于AI的机械系统故障诊断中缺乏可校验物理证据的问题，本文提出诊断证据网络（DENet），在输出中增加可对照理论值的特征频率和可观测的瞬态脉冲定位，并约束语言模型仅翻译不生成诊断内容，从而提供无需标注的推理时验证信号。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前智能故障诊断器输出仅为类别标签和softmax置信度，这是分类器的内部统计量，无法与独立的物理知识进行对照校验；同时，生成式语言模型在维护报告中的使用带来了幻觉内容进入决策报告的风险。因此亟需一种可校验、可验证的诊断输出范式。

Method: 提出诊断证据网络（DENet），一种编码器无关的多任务框架，将输出扩展为结构化证据记录：包含分类结果、可对照轴承几何与转速理论值的预测特征频率、以及可在原始波形上检查的瞬态脉冲时间定位。使用QLoRA适配的语言模型仅翻译不生成诊断内容。

Result: 在四个编码器和三个公开数据集上，DENet在无统计显著的精度损失下，于1024点频谱估计结构不可用的片段上实现约6 Hz的频率误差；预测与理论频率的偏差作为无标签推理时验证信号，检测误分类的AUROC达0.970与0.871，在高置信度区间仍具判别力；语言模型将无依据主张率从10-12%降至2%，消除虚构数据。

Conclusion: 通过输出可对照物理理论的结构化证据，DENet在保持诊断精度的同时提供了可靠的推理时验证机制，结合约束语言模型有效减少了报告中的幻觉内容，为安全关键机械系统中AI诊断的可信部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Trustworthy deployment of AI-based diagnosis in safety-critical mechanical systems hinges on validation: whether a prediction can be checked against physical reality before it is acted upon. Current intelligent fault diagnosers fail this standard in two ways. Their standard output, a class label with a softmax confidence score, is an internal statistic of the classifier, offering nothing checkable against independent physical knowledge; and the growing use of generative language models in maintenance reporting adds a second risk: hallucinated content entering reports on which decisions rest. Taking bearing fault diagnosis as the testbed, this work addresses both problems from the output side. The proposed Diagnostic Evidence Network (DENet) is an encoder-agnostic multi-task framework extending the output to a structured evidence record: the classification, a predicted characteristic frequency comparable against the theoretical value determined by bearing geometry and shaft speed, and a temporal localization of transient impulses inspectable on the raw waveform. Across four encoders and three public datasets, this evidence incurs no statistically significant accuracy cost, with a frequency error of about 6 Hz on 1,024-point segments where spectral estimation is structurally inapplicable. Centrally, the deviation between predicted and theoretical frequency constitutes a label-free, inference-time validation signal: it detects misclassifications with AUROC values of 0.970 and 0.871, and remains discriminative in the high-confidence regime where confidence-derived detectors are blind. Finally, a QLoRA-adapted language model is constrained to translate, but never generate, diagnostic content, reducing unsupported-claim rates from 10-12% to 2% and eliminating fabricated quantities.

中文摘要翻译: 基于AI的诊断在安全关键机械系统中的可信部署取决于验证：即在依据预测采取行动之前，能否将预测与物理现实进行对照检查。当前的智能故障诊断器在两个方面未能达到这一标准。它们的标准输出——带有softmax置信度分数的类别标签——是分类器的内部统计量，无法提供能与独立物理知识对照检查的内容；此外，生成式语言模型在维护报告中的日益使用带来了第二个风险：幻觉内容进入决策所依赖的报告。本文以轴承故障诊断为试验平台，从输出端同时解决这两个问题。所提出的诊断证据网络（DENet）是一种编码器无关的多任务框架，将输出扩展为结构化证据记录：包括分类结果、可与轴承几何形状和轴速决定的理论值进行对照的预测特征频率，以及可在原始波形上检查的瞬态脉冲时间定位。在四个编码器和三个公开数据集上，该证据未带来统计显著的精度损失，在频谱估计在结构上不适用的1024点片段上实现了约6 Hz的频率误差。关键是，预测频率与理论频率之间的偏差构成了一种无需标注的推理时验证信号：它检测误分类的AUROC值为0.970和0.871，并在置信度衍生检测器失效的高置信度区间仍保持判别能力。最后，QLoRA适配的语言模型被约束为仅翻译而从不生成诊断内容，将无依据主张率从10-12%降至2%，并消除了虚构的数据。

</details>


### [207] [LithoFormer: A Robust Framework for Stratigraphic Inference via Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22804)
*Shwetha Salimath,Francesca Bugiotti,Sylvain Wlodarczyk,Sohaib Ouzineb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LithoFormer是一个基于Seq2Seq Transformer的地层推断框架，通过一次性处理整个多变量测井数据，实现了高精度、无违例的地质分层，将边界误差中位数降低90%，人工劳动减少80%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于滑动窗口分类的自动化地质表征技术难以理解更广泛的地质背景，常导致地层错位，因此需要一种能够捕捉全局地质依赖关系的方法。

Method: 采用Seq2Seq Transformer模型，以通道独立的PatchTST为主干网络，结合旋转位置编码（RoPE）捕获长程地质依赖；使用解耦的多任务头联合预测地质分带和边界概率，并引入地质知识驱动的损失函数强制执行地层层序律等物理约束。

Result: 在三个真实数据集上，相比传统滑动窗口基线方法，边界误差中位数降低90%，消除地层顺序违例；人工专家工作量减少80%，消除地层不一致性。

Conclusion: LithoFormer为大规模地下建模提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案，显著提升了地质表征的准确性和效率。

Original Abstract: Accurate geological characterization of subsurface reservoirs from well log data is essential to support projects such as carbon capture and storage (CCS), geothermal development, and extraction of natural resources. Existing automated techniques for geological characterization primarily use sliding-window classification, which limits their ability to understand broader geological contexts, often leading to misaligned formation layers. To overcome these limitations, we introduce LithoFormer, a robust framework for stratigraphic inference using a Seq2Seq transformer model that ingests entire multivariate well logs in a single pass. The framework utilizes a channel-independent PatchTST backbone enhanced with rotary positional embeddings (RoPE) to capture long-range geological dependencies across entire multivariate well logs. A decoupled multi-task head is employed to jointly predict geological zonation and precise boundary probabilities, while a geology-informed loss function enforces physical constraints such as the Law of Superposition. Validated and deployed on three real-world datasets, LithoFormer demonstrates a 90% reduction in median boundary error and eliminates stratigraphic order violations compared to traditional sliding-window baselines. It also achieves a 80% reduction in manual expert labor and eliminates stratigraphic inconsistencies, providing a scalable and reliable solution for large-scale subsurface modeling.

中文摘要翻译: 基于测井数据对地下储层进行精确的地质表征，对于支持碳捕集与封存（CCS）、地热开发以及自然资源开采等项目至关重要。现有的自动化地质表征技术主要采用滑动窗口分类方法，这限制了其理解更广泛地质背景的能力，常导致地层错位。为克服这些局限，我们提出了LithoFormer——一个基于Seq2Seq Transformer的稳健地层推断框架，能够一次性处理整个多变量测井数据。该框架采用通道独立的PatchTST主干网络，并增强以旋转位置编码（RoPE），从而捕获整个多变量测井数据中的长程地质依赖关系。通过解耦的多任务头联合预测地质分带和精确的边界概率，同时引入地质知识驱动的损失函数强制执行地层层序律等物理约束。在三个真实数据集上的验证与部署表明，相比传统滑动窗口基线方法，LithoFormer将边界误差中位数降低了90%，并消除了地层顺序违例。此外，它还减少了80%的人工专家工作量，消除了地层不一致性，为大规模地下建模提供了可扩展且可靠的解决方案。

</details>


### [208] [OrchNAS: Orchestrated Neural Architecture Search Service for Personalised Federated Edge Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22805)
*Keya Patel,Sajib Mistry,Sheik Mohammad Mostakim Fattah,Aneesh Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了OrchNAS，一个面向异构边缘环境的能量感知、个性化联邦边缘智能框架，通过NAS服务自动设计服务自适应模型，并采用能量感知的全局搜索、渐进式剪枝和原对偶优化机制，在满足资源约束的同时保持全局表示。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 异构边缘环境中设备在能量、计算和内存资源上存在显著差异，现有方法难以自动为每个服务设计满足其特定资源约束的个性化模型。

Method: 1) 在服务器端部署NAS服务进行全局架构搜索；2) 采用能量感知的全局架构搜索机制学习跨异构服务的紧凑全局表示；3) 通过渐进式、贪心的能量感知剪枝策略为每个服务导出个性化的子网络；4) 提出能量高效的个性化模型优化方案，利用原对偶优化机制在架构适应过程中强制执行严格的能量预算。

Result: 在真实世界和基准数据集上的实验验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: OrchNAS能够为异构边缘环境自动设计满足资源约束的服务自适应模型，实现了能量感知的个性化联邦边缘智能。

Original Abstract: We propose OrchNAS, an energy-aware, personalised, federated edge intelligence framework that leverages a Neural Architecture Search Service to automatically design service-adaptive models for heterogeneous edge environments. The framework orchestrates the architecture search process on a server-side NAS service, enabling edge services to derive personalised architectures under device-level energy, computation, and memory constraints. We introduce an energy-aware global architecture search mechanism that learns a compact global representation across heterogeneous services. We develop an energy-efficient architecture selection mechanism that enables each service to derive a personalised subnet that satisfies its resource constraints via a progressive, greedy, energy-aware pruning strategy. We propose an energy-efficient personalised model optimisation scheme that updates service-adaptive parameters while preserving global representations, where a primal-dual optimisation mechanism enforces strict energy budgets during architecture adaptation. Experiments on real-world and benchmark datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了OrchNAS，一个能量感知、个性化、联邦式的边缘智能框架，该框架利用神经架构搜索服务自动为异构边缘环境设计服务自适应模型。该框架在服务器端的NAS服务上协调架构搜索过程，使边缘服务能够在设备级别的能量、计算和内存约束下推导出个性化架构。我们引入了一种能量感知的全局架构搜索机制，以学习跨异构服务的紧凑全局表示。我们开发了一种能量高效的架构选择机制，通过渐进式、贪心的能量感知剪枝策略，使每个服务能够推导出满足其资源约束的个性化子网络。我们提出了一种能量高效的个性化模型优化方案，在更新服务自适应参数的同时保留全局表示，其中原对偶优化机制在架构适应过程中强制执行严格的能量预算。在真实世界和基准数据集上的实验证明了该方法的有效性。

</details>


### [209] [SLA-Constrained Carbon-Aware Routing in Geo-Distributed Serverless Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22806)
*Anmol Chaudhary,Rahul Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种碳感知的无服务器路由策略，在SLA约束下通过优化地理分布式云区域的路由决策，在保持零SLA违规的同时，实现了最高46.8%的碳排放降低（平均27.4%），且路由开销极低（<0.02%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有云部署中，标准路由机制仅以延迟为优化目标，忽视了不同区域电网的碳排放强度差异，导致不必要的碳排放。当清洁能源区域在可接受延迟范围内时，仍被忽略，亟需一种碳感知的路由方案。

Method: 将碳感知无服务器路由问题建模为地理分布式云区域上的约束优化问题，并提出一种受SLA约束的碳感知路由策略，在SLA可行区域内实现最优碳减排。

Result: 基于5个主要AWS部署的真实碳强度数据，所提策略在所有评估阈值下实现最高46.8%的碳减排且零SLA违规；混合工作负载下平均碳减排27.4%；路由开销低于总请求延迟的0.02%；扩展到12个AWS区域（6大洲）后，平均碳节省从27.4%提升至47.5%。

Conclusion: 云系统可以在不牺牲用户体验的前提下实现显著的碳减排，该工作对SDG 13（气候行动）和SDG 7（可负担的清洁能源）有积极贡献。

Original Abstract: Modern cloud deployments distribute applications across multiple geographic regions, yet standard routing mechanisms prioritize latency while ignoring the fluctuating carbon intensity of local power grids. Latency-driven routing incurs avoidable carbon emissions, particularly when cleaner regions are within acceptable latency bounds. The proposed model formulates the carbon-aware serverless routing problem as a constrained optimization over geo-distributed cloud regions and introduces an SLA-constrained carbon-aware routing policy that achieves optimal carbon reduction within the SLA-feasible region, evaluated using real carbon intensity measurements across 5 primary AWS deployments. Experimental results show that the proposed policy achieves up to 46.8% carbon reduction while maintaining zero SLA violations across all evaluated thresholds. The system reduces carbon by an average of 27.4% under mixed workloads, and the routing overhead is very low (less than 0.02% of total request latency). A scalability study across 12 AWS regions spanning 6 continents demonstrates that average carbon savings increase from 27.4% to 47.5% as routing flexibility expands under mixed workloads. The proposed work contributes to SDG 13 (Climate Action) and SDG 7 (Affordable and Clean Energy) by enabling low-carbon routing decisions. These results indicate that cloud systems can achieve significant carbon savings without compromising user experience.

中文摘要翻译: 现代云部署将应用分布到多个地理区域，但标准路由机制优先考虑延迟，而忽略了本地电网波动的碳排放强度。以延迟为驱动的路由会导致可避免的碳排放，特别是当清洁区域处于可接受的延迟范围内时。本文提出的模型将碳感知无服务器路由问题建模为地理分布式云区域上的约束优化问题，并引入了一种受SLA约束的碳感知路由策略，该策略在SLA可行区域内实现最优碳减排，并使用覆盖5个主要AWS部署的真实碳排放强度数据进行了评估。实验结果表明，所提出的策略在所有评估阈值下实现了最高46.8%的碳减排，同时保持零SLA违规。在混合工作负载下，系统平均降低27.4%的碳排放，且路由开销极低（低于总请求延迟的0.02%）。一项覆盖6大洲12个AWS区域的可扩展性研究表明，在混合工作负载下，随着路由灵活性的扩展，平均碳节省从27.4%提升至47.5%。该工作通过实现低碳路由决策，对SDG 13（气候行动）和SDG 7（可负担的清洁能源）做出贡献。这些结果表明，云系统可以在不牺牲用户体验的前提下实现显著的碳减排。

</details>


### [210] [Neonatal Hypoxic-ischaemic Encephalopathy Classification from the EEG and HRV Signals Using a Conformer based Masked Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23554)
*Shuwen Yu,William P Marnane,Geraldine B. Boylan,Gordon Lightbody*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAEConformer框架，将Conformer架构与掩码自编码器（MAE）范式相结合，用于从无标注的脑电图（EEG）和心率变异性（HRV）信号中进行大规模自监督表示学习。模型通过卷积与Transformer自注意力融合局部时序和长程依赖，并引入多分辨率短时傅里叶变换（MR-STFT）损失增强重建质量。在EEG和HRV的缺氧缺血性脑病（HIE）严重程度分类任务中，分别取得了97.19%/96.56%（EEG二分类/四分类）和82.42%（HRV）的测试AUC，优于多种监督与自监督基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的生理信号（EEG/HRV）自监督学习方法在捕获局部时序模式与长程上下文依赖方面存在不足，且缺乏跨模态的通用表示学习框架，因此需要一种能够有效融合卷积与自注意力机制、并在多种生理模态上学习鲁棒且可迁移表征的方法。

Method: 提出MAEConformer框架，基于Conformer架构（卷积+Transformer自注意力）结合掩码自编码器（MAE）范式进行自监督预训练。采用多分辨率短时傅里叶变换（MR-STFT）损失作为重建目标的一部分，使模型同时学习时域和频域的多尺度特征。分别对EEG和HRV模态进行大规模预训练（6030h和4868h无标注数据），然后迁移至下游分类任务。

Result: 在EEG-HIE二分类和四分类任务上，测试AUC分别达到97.19%和96.56%，优于多种SOTA监督与自监督基线。在HRV-HIE分类任务上，测试AUC达82.42%，超越自监督Transformer基线和监督卷积基线。预训练表示展现出强迁移性和数据效率。

Conclusion: MAEConformer能够有效学习跨多种生理模态（EEG和HRV）的鲁棒且可迁移的表征，验证了结合Conformer与MAE范式在生理信号自监督学习中的有效性。

Original Abstract: In this paper, we propose the MAEConformer, a novel self-supervised learning framework that combines the Conformer architecture with the Masked Autoencoder (MAE) paradigm for large-scale representation learning from unlabelled electroencephalography (EEG) and heart rate variability (HRV) signals. By integrating convolutional operations with Transformer-based self-attention, MAEConformer effectively captures both local temporal patterns and long-range contextual dependencies in physiological time series. To enhance reconstruction fidelity and representation quality, a multi-resolution short-time Fourier transform (MR-STFT) loss is incorporated alongside the reconstruction objective, enabling the model to jointly learn temporal and spectral characteristics across multiple scales. Modality-specific EEG and HRV MAEConformer models were pretrained on 6,030h and 4,868h of unlabelled recordings, respectively, and subsequently transferred to expert-annotated downstream tasks. Experimental results demonstrate that the learned representations provide strong transferability and data efficiency. In EEG-based hypoxic ischemic encephalopathy (HIE) severity classification, the pretrained MAE-EEG model achieved test AUCs of 97.19% and 96.56% for binary and four-class classification tasks, respectively, outperforming a range of state-of-the-art supervised and self-supervised baselines. On the HRV-based HIE severity classification task, MAE-HRV achieved a test AUC of 82.42%, surpassing both self-supervised Transformer-based and supervised convolutional baselines. These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of MAEConformer for learning robust and transferable representations across multiple physiological modalities.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出MAEConformer，一种新型自监督学习框架，它将Conformer架构与掩码自编码器（MAE）范式相结合，用于从无标注的脑电图（EEG）和心率变异性（HRV）信号中进行大规模表示学习。通过融合卷积运算与基于Transformer的自注意力机制，MAEConformer能够有效捕获生理时间序列中的局部时序模式和长程上下文依赖。为了增强重建保真度和表示质量，在重建目标中引入了多分辨率短时傅里叶变换（MR-STFT）损失，使模型能够跨多个尺度联合学习时域和频谱特征。针对EEG和HRV模态的MAEConformer模型分别使用6030小时和4868小时的无标注记录进行预训练，随后迁移至专家标注的下游任务。实验结果表明，学习到的表示具有强迁移性和数据效率。在基于EEG的缺氧缺血性脑病（HIE）严重程度分类中，预训练的MAE-EEG模型在二分类和四分类任务上分别取得了97.19%和96.56%的测试AUC，优于一系列最先进的监督和自监督基线方法。在基于HRV的HIE严重程度分类任务中，MAE-HRV取得了82.42%的测试AUC，超越了自监督Transformer基线和监督卷积基线。这些发现证明了MAEConformer在跨多种生理模态学习鲁棒且可迁移表示方面的有效性。

</details>


### [211] [From Hybrid Mechanistic--Data-Driven Modeling Toward Neuro-Symbolic AI: What, Why, and How](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22811)
*Moein E. Samadi,Andreas Schuppert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N)框架，将混合机理/数据驱动模型重构成神经符号AI实例，并由此导出两个新指标（结构违反率SVR和信念离散度BD）来量化混合模型中机理部分的认知不确定性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有混合建模设计主要通过架构和训练损失来定义，缺乏跨领域比较和验证的共享语义接口，且很少关注机理部分的认知不确定性。

Method: 将混合建模重构为神经符号AI（NeSy）实例，建立H2N翻译框架：机理知识映射到语言侧，学习模块映射到信念侧，有效性域和约束映射到逻辑侧；由此推导出NeSy推理函数和逻辑-信念分解，进而提出SVR和BD两个度量指标。

Result: 在标签噪声下的二分类结构化混合模型案例中，SVR和BD越高的模型在留出精度上表现出更大变异性；在结构分布偏移下，H2N能量化模型外推时的不确定性，而测试精度只能事后反映同一偏移。

Conclusion: H2N框架为混合模型提供了统一的神经符号语义接口，并能有效量化机理部分的认知不确定性，为模型在分布偏移下的可靠性评估提供了新工具。

Original Abstract: Hybrid mechanistic/data-driven models, which combine first-principles with learned components, are increasingly used in process engineering and scientific machine learning. Common hybrid modeling designs are specified primarily through their architectures and training losses, which offers a limited basis for a shared semantic interface to compare or verify them across domains, with comparatively little attention paid to epistemic uncertainty in the mechanistic part.
  We bridge hybrid modeling and neuro-symbolic (NeSy) AI by reconstructing these designs as instances of NeSy interface. The resulting translation, Hybrid-to-NeSy (H2N), places mechanistic knowledge on the language side, learned modules on the belief side, and validity domains together with constraints on the logic side. For each design, H2N then yields an explicit NeSy inference functional and a logic-belief decomposition.
  From this decomposition we derive two metrics: structural violation rate (SVR), measuring whether the learned belief respects the mechanistic structure; and belief dispersion (BD), measuring how concentrated the learned plausibility is, serving as a hybrid model's epistemic uncertainty in its mechanistic part. We instantiate H2N on a case study of a structured hybrid model for binary classification under label noise and show that models with higher SVR and BD exhibit greater variability in held-out accuracy. Under structural distribution shift, H2N further quantifies a model's uncertainty during extrapolations, whereas test accuracy reveals the same shift only post hoc.

中文摘要翻译: 结合第一性原理与学习组件的混合机理/数据驱动模型，在过程工程和科学机器学习中应用日益广泛。常见的混合建模设计主要通过其架构和训练损失来定义，这为跨领域比较或验证提供了有限的共享语义接口基础，且对机理部分的认知不确定性关注相对较少。我们将混合建模与神经符号AI（NeSy）联系起来，将这些设计重构为NeSy接口的实例。由此产生的翻译框架Hybrid-to-NeSy（H2N）将机理知识置于语言侧，学习模块置于信念侧，有效性域和约束置于逻辑侧。对于每种设计，H2N进而导出显式的NeSy推理函数和逻辑-信念分解。从这一分解中我们推导出两个度量指标：结构违反率（SVR），用于衡量学习到的信念是否尊重机理结构；以及信念离散度（BD），用于衡量学习到的可信度的集中程度，作为混合模型在其机理部分的认知不确定性。我们在标签噪声下二分类的结构化混合模型案例上实例化H2N，并表明SVR和BD越高的模型在留出精度上表现出更大的变异性。在结构分布偏移下，H2N进一步量化了模型在外推期间的不确定性，而测试精度仅在事后揭示相同的偏移。

</details>


### [212] [MEMENTO: Memory-Guided Memetic Code-as-Policy Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22832)
*Alkis Sygkounas,Victor Aregbede,Amy Loutfi,Andreas Persson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MEMENTO提出了一种基于记忆引导的单精英模因框架，用于代码即策略的进化优化，通过演化轨迹评估器与记忆引导的爬山、宏变异和交叉操作，在长时程具身任务中优于现有基线方法，并实现了从仿真到真实机器人的策略迁移。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的大语言模型驱动的进化方法主要通过独立生成变体并选择高性能候选者来搜索策略，缺乏序列化的局部改进阶段，导致长时程具身任务中的策略优化效率不足。

Method: MEMENTO首先演化出一个轨迹评估器，将策略轨迹映射为标量适应度和结构化反馈指标；适应度用于选择接受候选和下一个精英，反馈指标则指导由记忆引导的爬山、宏变异和交叉操作生成的策略提案。

Result: 在Robosuite Franka汉诺塔操作和AI2-THOR家庭交互两个长时程具身域中，MEMENTO在任务成功率和泛化能力上均优于Eureka和REvolve等基线；消融实验表明零样本生成和未演化评估器无法解决任一域任务，去除策略搜索分支会降低性能；成功将最优演化策略部署到物理Franka机器人上。

Conclusion: MEMENTO通过引入记忆引导的局部改进阶段，有效提升了代码即策略进化框架在长时程具身任务中的性能，并验证了仿真到真实迁移的可行性。

Original Abstract: Long-horizon embodied tasks require policies that execute many dependent actions before task success can be observed. Representing policies as executable control pro- grams (code-as-policy) enables their decision logic to be inspected and revised after rollout evaluation. Revised programs can then be executed and compared by rollout performance, framing policy improvement as execution-guided program search. Evo- lutionary methods driven by large language models (LLMs) provide a natural mecha- nism for this search by generating variants and selecting high-performing candidates. However, existing approaches primarily select among independently generated vari- ants and lack a sequential local improvement phase. We introduce MEMENTO, a memory-guided single-elite memetic framework for code-as-policy evolution. ME- MENTO first evolves a rollout evaluator that maps policy rollouts to scalar fitness and structured feedback metrics. Fitness selects accepted candidates and the next elite, while feedback metrics condition policy proposals generated by memory-guided hill-climbing, macro-mutation, and crossover. We evaluate MEMENTO on two long- horizon embodied domains: Robosuite Franka Tower-of-Hanoi manipulation and AI2- THOR household interaction. MEMENTO outperforms Eureka and REvolve, adapted as code-as-policy evolutionary baselines, in task success and generalization to held- out Robosuite object configurations and unseen AI2-THOR scenes. Ablations show that zero-shot generation and unevolved evaluators fail to solve either domain, and that removing policy-search branches reduces performance. Finally, we deploy the best-evolved Robosuite policy on a physical Franka robot, demonstrating the feasibil- ity of sim-to-real transfer of the evolved code-as-policy. Code, prompts, and videos are available at: https://github.com/sygkounas/MEMENTO.

中文摘要翻译: 长时程具身任务要求策略在执行任务成功可观测之前执行大量依赖动作。将策略表示为可执行的控制程序（代码即策略）使得其决策逻辑能够在轨迹评估后被检查和修订。修订后的程序可以执行并通过轨迹性能进行比较，从而将策略改进框架化为执行引导的程序搜索。由大语言模型驱动的进化方法通过生成变体并选择高性能候选者，为这种搜索提供了自然机制。然而，现有方法主要从独立生成的变体中进行选择，缺乏序列化的局部改进阶段。本文提出MEMENTO，一种用于代码即策略进化的记忆引导单精英模因框架。MEMENTO首先演化出一个轨迹评估器，将策略轨迹映射为标量适应度和结构化反馈指标。适应度用于选择被接受的候选者和下一个精英，而反馈指标则指导由记忆引导的爬山、宏变异和交叉操作生成的策略提案。我们在两个长时程具身域上评估MEMENTO：Robosuite Franka汉诺塔操作和AI2-THOR家庭交互。MEMENTO在任务成功率和泛化能力上均优于经适配作为代码即策略进化基线的Eureka和REvolve，在Robosuite的留存物体配置和未见过的AI2-THOR场景中均表现更优。消融实验表明，零样本生成和未演化评估器无法解决任一域任务，且去除策略搜索分支会降低性能。最后，我们将最优演化策略部署到物理Franka机器人上，证明了演化代码即策略的仿真到真实迁移的可行性。代码、提示词和视频可访问：https://github.com/sygkounas/MEMENTO。

</details>


### [213] [Frustratingly Simple Black-Box Adaptation of Language Models via Logit Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22837)
*Ofek I. Cohen,Lior Shani,Aviv Rosenberg,Ankur Samanta,Tal Wagner,Yonathan Efroni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的黑盒方法，通过学习一个固定的logit偏置向量（在每个解码步添加），在不修改模型权重且无需梯度的情况下实现领域自适应，在数学和推理基准上取得性能提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多组织希望将语言模型适配到内部使用场景，以提升领域任务性能并解决敏感数据隐私问题。但现有方法（如微调开源模型或优化提示词）操作复杂、成本高。作者希望探索一种最小化替代方案，仅通过API级别的简单控制（用户自定义向量偏置logits）实现模型适配。

Method: 基于KL正则化的强化学习目标，推导了固定logit偏置向量何时能近似最优前缀依赖修正的条件；提出一种黑盒学习方法，通过从推理轨迹（rollouts）、奖励和token概率中估计闭式逆倾向性得分（inverse-propensity estimator）来学习该偏置向量，无需梯度或修改模型权重。

Result: 实验表明，这种简单的解码时干预在数学和推理基准上相比基础模型有显著提升，同时使用的可训练参数远少于传统微调方法。

Conclusion: 学习得到的logit偏置是一种轻量级的语言模型自适应机制，适用于对模型访问权限要求极低的场景。

Original Abstract: Many organizations aim to adapt language models for internal use, both to improve performance on domain-specific tasks and to address privacy concerns around sensitive data. However, such adaptation remains non-trivial: it often requires operationally challenging fine-tuning of open-source models or ad hoc prompt optimization. We study a minimal alternative based on a simple API-level control: allowing users to bias the model's logits with a user-defined vector. We develop a black-box method for learning a single context-independent logit-bias vector, added at every decoding step, without modifying model weights or requiring gradients. Starting from a KL-regularized reinforcement learning (RL) objective, we characterize when such a fixed logit-bias vector can approximate the optimal prefix-dependent correction and derive a closed-form inverse-propensity estimator from rollouts, rewards, and token probabilities. Empirically, this simple decoding-time intervention improves over base models on mathematical and reasoning benchmarks while using far fewer trainable parameters than conventional fine-tuning. Our results suggest that learned logit bias is a lightweight mechanism for adapting language models under minimal access requirements.

中文摘要翻译: 许多组织旨在将语言模型适配到内部使用场景，既为了提升领域特定任务的性能，也为了处理敏感数据带来的隐私问题。然而，这种适配仍然具有挑战性：通常需要对开源模型进行操作上困难的微调，或者进行针对性的提示词优化。我们研究了一种基于简单API级别控制的最小化替代方案：允许用户使用自定义向量偏置模型的logits。我们开发了一种黑盒方法，用于学习一个固定的、与上下文无关的logit偏置向量，在每个解码步骤中将其添加，无需修改模型权重也无需梯度信息。从KL正则化的强化学习目标出发，我们刻画了这种固定logit偏置向量在何种条件下能够近似最优的、依赖于前缀的修正，并推导出基于推理轨迹、奖励和token概率的闭式逆倾向性得分估计器。实验表明，这种简单的解码时干预在数学和推理基准上优于基础模型，同时使用的可训练参数远少于传统微调方法。我们的结果表明，学习得到的logit偏置是一种在最小访问权限要求下对语言模型进行自适应适配的轻量级机制。

</details>


### [214] [Same Predictions, Different Reasons: The Effect of Quantization on Model Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22872)
*Kazi Kamruzzaman Rabbi,Md. Zami Al Zunaed Farabe,M. Sohel Rahman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了静态训练后量化（PTQ）对五种主流CNN架构（VGG19、ResNet18、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet161、MobileNetV2）在INT8和INT4精度下的可解释性影响，发现分类精度无法反映可解释性稳定性，其中DenseNet161保持最佳特征一致性，而EfficientNet-B0在INT4下输入级归因严重退化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 已有研究证明量化能基本保持分类精度，但量化是否会改变模型内部推理机制（即可解释性/可解释性）仍是一个开放问题，亟需系统评估。

Method: 采用双视角可解释性框架（Grad-CAM空间注意力分析 + LIME输入级特征归因），结合三种互补指标（皮尔逊相关系数、结构相似性指数、Top-20% IoU）和删除/插入忠实度分析，在二分类数据集上对比全精度与量化模型。

Result: 分类精度不能可靠反映可解释性稳定性；DenseNet161在两个精度级别下均保持强特征一致性；EfficientNet-B0在INT8下空间注意力和分类精度尚可，但输入级特征归因显著退化。

Conclusion: 架构选择与量化策略同样重要，对高可解释性要求的可信部署具有直接指导意义。

Original Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) has become a practical solution for deploying deep learning models on resource-constrained edge devices by compressing high-precision floating-point weights into low-precision representations without requiring retraining. Past research has demonstrated that quantization largely preserves classification accuracy; however, whether it also preserves the model's internal reasoning remains an open question. This study presents a systematic evaluation on how static PTQ affects the interpretability / explainability of five widely used CNN architectures: VGG19, ResNet18, EfficientNet-B0, DenseNet161, and MobileNetV2 at INT8 and INT4 precision. We employ a dual interpretability framework that combines Grad-CAM for spatial attention analysis with LIME for input-level feature attribution, and systematically compare full-precision and quantized models on two binary classification datasets. Interpretability is evaluated using three complementary metrics: the Pearson correlation coefficient, structural similarity index, and top-20% IoU to capture distributional and structural variations in model explanations, supplemented by deletion/insertion faithfulness analysis. The results show that classification accuracy is not a reliable indicator of interpretability stability under reduced precision. DenseNet161 maintains strong feature consistency across both precision levels, whereas EfficientNet-B0, despite achieving competitive spatial attention and classification accuracy at INT8 precision, exhibits a substantial degradation in input-level feature attribution. These findings have direct implications for the trustworthy deployment of quantized models in applications with high interpretability requirements, demonstrating that architecture selection is as important as the quantization strategy.

中文摘要翻译: 训练后量化（PTQ）通过将高精度浮点权重压缩为低精度表示而无需重新训练，已成为在资源受限的边缘设备上部署深度学习模型的实用方案。已有研究表明，量化在很大程度上能保持分类精度；然而，量化是否也能保持模型的内部推理机制仍是一个开放问题。本研究系统评估了静态PTQ对五种广泛使用的CNN架构（VGG19、ResNet18、EfficientNet-B0、DenseNet161和MobileNetV2）在INT8和INT4精度下的可解释性影响。我们采用双视角可解释性框架，结合Grad-CAM进行空间注意力分析和LIME进行输入级特征归因，并在两个二分类数据集上系统比较全精度模型与量化模型。可解释性通过三种互补指标进行评估：皮尔逊相关系数、结构相似性指数和Top-20% IoU，以捕捉模型解释中的分布性和结构性变化，并辅以删除/插入忠实度分析。结果表明，在降低精度条件下，分类精度并非可解释性稳定性的可靠指标。DenseNet161在两个精度级别下均保持强特征一致性；而EfficientNet-B0虽然在INT8精度下具有竞争力的空间注意力和分类精度，但其输入级特征归因出现了显著退化。这些发现对高可解释性要求的应用中量化模型的可信部署具有直接启示意义，表明架构选择与量化策略同等重要。

</details>


### [215] [Spatial Prediction of Soil Microplastics and Organic Matter Using Graph Attention Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22875)
*Anik Dev Nath,Md Al Amin,Bikash Kumar Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图注意力网络（GAT）的深度学习方法，利用91个地理参考土壤样本的空间依赖关系来预测土壤微塑料和有机质含量，取得了较高拟合度（R²分别为0.87和0.91），但交叉验证显示泛化能力有限。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 准确估算土壤微塑料和有机质含量对于评估生态系统健康和支撑可持续土地利用至关重要，而传统方法难以有效建模样本间的空间依赖关系。

Method: 采用图注意力网络（GATs），构建了一个两层GAT架构，将空间坐标、土壤属性和土地利用数据纳入模型，以捕捉局部空间交互作用。

Result: 微塑料预测RMSE为625.06（R²=0.87），有机质预测RMSE为0.43（R²=0.91）；但交叉验证结果显示泛化能力有限，主要原因是样本量较小且图结构稀疏。

Conclusion: GATs在空间土壤预测中展现出潜力，但需要更密集的数据集和更好的图连接性来提升模型的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Accurate estimation of soil microplastics and organic matter is essential to assess ecosystem health and support sustainable land use. This study presents a graph-based deep learning approach using Graph Attention Networks (GATs) to model spatial dependencies among 91 georeferenced soil samples. By incorporating spatial coordinates, soil properties, and land use data, a two-layer GAT architecture was developed to capture local interactions. The final model showed strong performance, achieving RMSEs of 625.06 ($R^2 = 0.87$) for microplastics and 0.43 ($R^2 = 0.91$) for organic matter. However, cross-validation results revealed limited generalization, probably due to the small sample size and sparse graph structure. These findings demonstrate the potential of GATs for spatial soil prediction and underscore the need for dense datasets and improved graph connectivity.

中文摘要翻译: 准确估算土壤微塑料和有机质含量对于评估生态系统健康和支撑可持续土地利用至关重要。本研究提出了一种基于图的深度学习方法，利用图注意力网络（GATs）对91个地理参考土壤样本的空间依赖关系进行建模。通过整合空间坐标、土壤属性和土地利用数据，构建了一个两层GAT架构以捕获局部交互作用。最终模型表现出强劲性能，微塑料预测的RMSE为625.06（R²=0.87），有机质预测的RMSE为0.43（R²=0.91）。然而，交叉验证结果显示出有限的泛化能力，这可能是由于样本量较小和图结构稀疏所致。这些发现证明了GATs在空间土壤预测中的潜力，同时也强调了需要更密集的数据集和改进的图连接性。

</details>


### [216] [Efficient Learning of Truncated Boolean Product Distributions: Influence to the Rescue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22889)
*Rohan Chauhan,Ioannis Panageas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对截断布尔乘积分布的自然参数估计问题，提出了一种基于测度几何分析的新方法，克服了现有方法对强局部连通性（fatness）或反集中假设的依赖，将样本复杂度改进至$O(\log n / ε^2)$，匹配非截断情形下的极小化最优速率，并建立了依赖模型宽度和集合最小距离的指数级下界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法（Fotakis等人，COLT'20, Algorithmica '22）在估计截断布尔乘积分布的自然参数时，存在严重局限性：要么要求截断集$S$具有强局部连通性（fatness属性），要么要求严格的反集中假设，且截断集的总质量需为常数（相对于$n$）。若$S$的质量随$n$指数衰减，样本复杂度会达到$Ω(2^n)$。本文旨在规避这些限制。

Method: 通过分析在测度$μ_z$下集合$S$的几何结构来估计自然参数。具体包括：1) 在fatness假设下改进参数估计保证，将样本复杂度优化至$O(\log n / ε^2)$（$\ell_\infty$恢复）；2) 利用布尔函数分析中的影响（influence）概念推广fatness，给出高效推断的充分条件；3) 无需在模型任意参数化下采样。

Result: 1) 在fatness假设下，样本复杂度改进至$O(\log n / ε^2)$，匹配非截断情形下的极小化最优速率；2) 利用influence概念推广了fatness，给出了更通用的高效推断条件；3) 建立了理论下界，证明样本复杂度对模型宽度和集合中元素的最小距离存在本质的指数依赖。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法有效规避了现有方法的关键限制，在更广泛的条件下实现了截断布尔乘积分布的高效参数估计，并且样本复杂度达到了理论最优。同时，所建立的下界揭示了问题的固有难度。

Original Abstract: Learning the natural parameters $z \in \mathbb{R}^n$ of discrete distributions $μ_z$ from independent samples constrained to a subset $S \subseteq \{0,1\}^n$ is a foundational challenge in high-dimensional statistics. Existing methods for efficiently estimating truncated Boolean product distributions, notably the work of [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22], require either strong local connectivity assumptions on $S$ -- a property denoted fatness -- or stringent anti-concentration assumptions and necessitate the total mass of the truncation set to be a constant with respect to $n$. Moreover, the results in [Fotakis et al' COLT'20, Algorithmica '22] suffer from sample complexities that scale as $Ω(2^n)$ if the mass of $S$ is exponentially small in $n$.
  In this work, we circumvent these limitations by analyzing the geometry of $S$ under the measure $μ_z$. We refine the existing parameter estimation guarantees under the fatness assumption, improving the prior sample complexity to $O( \log n / ε^2)$ for $\ell_\infty$-recovery, matching the untruncated minimax rate. We further generalize fatness using the notion of influence utilized in the analysis of Boolean functions and provide sufficient conditions for efficient inference. Notably, unlike previous work, our method does not require sampling at arbitrary parameterizations of the model. Lastly, we establish a theoretical lower bound demonstrating the sample complexity exhibits an intrinsic exponential dependence on the width of the model and the minimum distance between elements in the set.

中文摘要翻译: 从受限于子集$S \subseteq \{0,1\}^n$的独立样本中学习离散分布$μ_z$的自然参数$z \in \mathbb{R}^n$是高维统计学中的一个基础性挑战。现有高效估计截断布尔乘积分布的方法，特别是[Fotakis等, COLT'20, Algorithmica '22]的工作，要么要求$S$具有强局部连通性假设（称为fatness属性），要么要求严格的反集中假设，并且截断集的总质量需相对于$n$为常数。此外，若$S$的质量随$n$呈指数衰减，[Fotakis等, COLT'20, Algorithmica '22]的结果会遭受$Ω(2^n)$量级的样本复杂度。在本工作中，我们通过分析在测度$μ_z$下$S$的几何结构来规避这些限制。我们在fatness假设下改进了现有的参数估计保证，将$\ell_\infty$恢复的样本复杂度提升至$O(\log n / ε^2)$，匹配了非截断情形下的极小化最优速率。我们进一步利用布尔函数分析中使用的influence（影响）概念推广了fatness，并给出了高效推断的充分条件。值得注意的是，与以往工作不同，我们的方法无需在模型的任意参数化下进行采样。最后，我们建立了一个理论下界，表明样本复杂度对模型宽度以及集合中元素之间的最小距离存在本质的指数依赖。

</details>


### [217] [Learning from the Descent Direction: Adaptive Gradient Descent under One-Sided Hölder Regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22906)
*Arzu Ahmadova,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对单侧 Hölder 连续可微（可能非凸）目标的自适应梯度下降方法，利用正向单侧 Hölder 曲率估计步长，并配合充分下降保障机制，在非凸优化中证明了最佳迭代驻点界，实验表明该方法在方向曲率与全梯度变化分离的基准任务上优于传统标量梯度方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 经典的 Hölder 或 Lipschitz 梯度假设控制了全梯度变化，但在梯度的大幅变化正交于或有利于更新方向时，这些假设会导致步长过于保守。为了在步长选择上更灵活，本文希望利用仅与下降不等式中的方向项相关的单侧 Hölder 正则性来设计自适应方法。

Method: 提出一种基于正向单侧 Hölder 曲率估计的自适应标量步长方法，结合一个简单的充分下降保障机制。方法不依赖预设的递减步长方案，而是根据局部下降几何自适应调整步长。

Result: 在二分类任务中，该方法取得了最低的最终交叉熵、目标值和梯度范数，以及最大的分类间隔；在非凸 Hölder 回归任务中，取得了最低的最终目标差距和梯度范数。

Conclusion: 单向 Hölder 曲率是一种有效的自适应步长信号，尤其当全梯度变化被不阻碍下降的方向所放大时。

Original Abstract: We study adaptive gradient descent for continuously differentiable, possibly nonconvex objectives under one-sided Hölder regularity. Unlike classical Hölder- or Lipschitz-gradient assumptions, which control the full gradient variation, our condition bounds only the directional term appearing in the descent inequality. This can allow less conservative step sizes when large gradient changes are orthogonal to, or favorable along, the update direction. We propose an adaptive scalar-step method based on an estimate of positive one-sided Hölder curvature, combined with a simple sufficient-decrease safeguard. For nonconvex objectives on a convex region containing the accepted update segments, we prove an explicit best-iterate stationarity bound with a rate determined by the Hölder exponent. Unlike predetermined diminishing step-size schemes, the method adapts to the local descent geometry. We evaluate the approach on two full-batch benchmarks designed to separate directional curvature from full gradient variation. On a binary classification problem, the method achieves the lowest final cross-entropy, objective value, and gradient norm, together with the largest classification margin among the compared scalar gradient methods. On a nonconvex Hölder regression problem, it attains the lowest final objective gap and gradient norm. These results indicate that one-sided Hölder curvature is an effective adaptive step-size signal when full-gradient variation is inflated by directions that do not hinder descent.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了在单侧 Hölder 正则性条件下，针对连续可微、可能非凸目标的自适应梯度下降方法。与经典的 Hölder 或 Lipschitz 梯度假设（控制全梯度变化）不同，我们的条件仅约束出现在下降不等式中的方向项。当梯度的大幅变化正交于或有利于更新方向时，这可以允许使用不那么保守的步长。我们提出了一种基于正向单侧 Hölder 曲率估计的自适应标量步长方法，并结合了一个简单的充分下降保障机制。对于包含接受更新段的凸区域上的非凸目标，我们证明了一个显式的最佳迭代驻点界，其速率由 Hölder 指数决定。与预设的递减步长方案不同，该方法自适应于局部下降几何。我们在两个旨在分离方向曲率与全梯度变化的全批处理基准任务上评估了该方法。在一个二分类问题上，该方法在比较的标量梯度方法中取得了最低的最终交叉熵、目标值和梯度范数，同时获得了最大的分类间隔。在一个非凸 Hölder 回归问题上，它取得了最低的最终目标差距和梯度范数。这些结果表明，当全梯度变化被不阻碍下降的方向所放大时，单侧 Hölder 曲率是一种有效的自适应步长信号。

</details>


### [218] [Beyond Directed Acyclic Graphs: Causal Zeros and Causal Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22910)
*Sergei V. Kalinin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出Pearl的结构因果模型（SCM）存在有向边预设和禁止反馈循环两大结构性限制，并针对对称物理/经济约束（如理想气体定律）和表观瞬时循环两类现象，提出了扩展因果模型（ECM）和因果微分方程（CDE）两种形式化框架，分别引入激活算子和时间展开机制，以容纳无固有因果方向的关系和反馈动力学。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Pearl的SCM框架要求所有关系必须预指定为有向因果边，且禁止反馈循环，但在对称物理约束（如理想气体定律）和包含反馈的动力学系统中，这种限制导致框架无法自然建模。本文旨在突破这两项结构性限制，将因果推理扩展到更广泛的领域。

Method: 1. 提出扩展因果模型（ECM），通过引入激活算子（activation operator）将对称约束形式化为因果零点（causal zeros），并给出局部可解性和图可接受性条件。2. 对有限传播状态空间系统，提出因果微分方程（CDE），将瞬态区域视为时间展开的无环因果过程，因果零点作为吸引平衡流形的定义函数，周期性/混沌吸引子则通过吸引子相关干预处理。

Result: 给出了扩展do-演算、可识别性条件、反事实语义，并指出了开放问题。

Conclusion: 通过对SCM框架的扩展，实现了对无固有因果方向的对称约束和包含反馈的动力学系统的因果建模，丰富了因果推理的形式化工具。

Original Abstract: Pearl's structural causal model (SCM) framework, built on directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) and the do-calculus, is the dominant formal language for causal reasoning. Yet it carries two structural restrictions: every relationship must be pre-specified as a directed causal edge, and feedback cycles are forbidden. This paper examines two classes of phenomena that strain these restrictions. First, symmetric physical and economic constraints, the ideal gas law being the canonical case, carry no intrinsic causal direction. Direction emerges only under intervention, and which variable is solved for must be specified as part of the intervention. We formalize such constraints as causal zeros within an Extended Causal Model by adding an activation operator, subject to local solvability and graph-admissibility conditions. Second, for the class of finite-propagation state-space systems considered here, we treat apparent instantaneous cycles as artifacts of suppressed time and ground both causal zeros and feedback in Causal Differential Equations (CDEs). In these, the transient regime is a time-unrolled acyclic causal process, and causal zeros arise as the defining functions of attracting equilibrium manifolds; periodic and chaotic attractors define further regimes of the same dynamics, treated through attractor-relative intervention. We give the extended do-calculus, identifiability conditions, counterfactual semantics, and open problems.

中文摘要翻译: Pearl的结构因果模型（SCM）框架建立在有向无环图（DAG）和do-演算之上，是因果推理的主流形式化语言。然而它带有两项结构性限制：每个关系必须预先指定为有向因果边，且不允许反馈循环。本文考察了两类挑战这些限制的现象。第一，对称的物理和经济约束——理想气体定律是典型例子——不具有内在的因果方向。方向仅在干预下出现，而求解哪个变量必须作为干预的一部分加以指定。我们通过引入激活算子，在扩展因果模型中将此类约束形式化为因果零点，并受局部可解性和图可接受性条件的约束。第二，对于本文所考虑的一类有限传播状态空间系统，我们将表观瞬时循环视为被抑制的时间的假象，并在因果微分方程（CDE）中为因果零点和反馈提供了基础。在CDE中，瞬态区域是一个时间展开的无环因果过程，因果零点作为吸引平衡流形的定义函数出现；周期吸引子和混沌吸引子定义了同一动力学的其他区域，通过吸引子相关的干预进行处理。我们给出了扩展do-演算、可识别性条件、反事实语义及开放问题。

</details>


### [219] [Hidden Boundary Motion in Transformer Optimization: Function-Space Orthogonalization of Affine Weight and Bias Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22927)
*Zhang Gongyue,Sheng Yixuan,Liu donghan,Wang Zhiyong,Ren Weihong,Liu honghai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文揭示了仿射层中权重更新存在与偏置更新功能不可区分的“边界运动”分量（ΔWμ），并发现该分量在AdamW优化中主导了实际的边界运动（与显式偏置的比值中位数达134.7）。作者提出SBO-AdamW优化器，将权重梯度分解为形状分量和边界分量分别优化，在单种子实验中验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.85%，但存在偏置坐标漂移问题，表明需要稳定的中心化仿射参数化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 权重和偏置通常被独立优化，但当仿射层的输入均值非零时，权重更新中存在一个与偏置更新功能不可区分的隐藏分量（边界运动），现有优化方法忽略了这一结构性问题。

Method: 提出形状-边界正交AdamW（SBO-AdamW），将原始权重梯度g_W分解为g_W - g_b μ^T（形状分量）和g_b μ^T（边界分量），对两者使用独立的Adam状态进行优化，并补偿权重导致的边界位移。

Result: 在IMDb上训练的4层Transformer中：边界项g_b μ^T的中位数范数为原始权重梯度范数的0.664倍；||ΔWμ||/||Δb||中位数为134.7，||ΔWμ||/||Δb+ΔWμ||中位数为0.994，表明边界运动几乎完全通过权重矩阵实现。SBO-AdamW将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，测试准确率从78.73%提升至82.73%，最佳验证点从第3000步提前至第800步。

Conclusion: 隐藏的边界运动是重要的优化机制，但SBO-AdamW存在偏置坐标漂移和边界能量下降的问题，尚未能成为通用的最终优化器。下一步需要开发稳定的中心化仿射参数化方案。

Original Abstract: Weights and biases are normally optimized as separate parameter tensors, yet they do not represent separate functions when the input to an affine layer has nonzero mean. For an affine map $z=Wx+b$ with input mean $μ$, a weight update contains a sample-independent displacement $ΔWμ$ that is functionally indistinguishable from a bias update. We call this hidden contribution \emph{boundary motion} and decompose each update into a centered, sample-varying \emph{shape} component and a shared \emph{boundary} component. On a four-layer Transformer trained from scratch on IMDb, the bias-like term $g_bμ^\top$ has a median norm equal to 0.664 of the raw weight-gradient norm across affine layers and training checkpoints. More strikingly, the median ratio $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb}$ is 134.7, while $\norm{ΔWμ}/\norm{Δb+ΔWμ}$ is 0.994. Thus, under AdamW, the observed boundary motion is almost entirely realized through the weight matrix rather than the explicit bias.
  We implement a diagnostic optimizer, Shape--Boundary Orthogonal AdamW (SBO-AdamW), that optimizes $g_W-g_bμ^\top$ and $g_b$ with independent Adam states and compensates the weight-induced boundary displacement. In a single-seed experiment, SBO-AdamW raises validation accuracy from 81.68\% to 85.81\% and validation-selected test accuracy from 78.73\% to 82.73\%, with the best validation checkpoint occurring at step 800 instead of step 3000. However, the moving-batch-center compensation produces severe bias-coordinate drift and strongly reduces boundary energy. The present evidence therefore supports hidden boundary motion as an important optimization mechanism, but it does not yet establish a final general-purpose optimizer. A stable centered-affine parameterization is identified as the required next step.

中文摘要翻译: 权重和偏置通常作为独立的参数张量进行优化，但当仿射层的输入均值非零时，它们并不代表独立的功能。对于输入均值为μ的仿射映射z=Wx+b，权重更新包含一个与样本无关的位移项ΔWμ，该位移项在功能上与偏置更新不可区分。我们将这种隐藏的贡献称为"边界运动"，并将每次更新分解为一个中心化的、随样本变化的"形状"分量和一个共享的"边界"分量。在从零开始在IMDb上训练的四层Transformer中，偏置项g_bμ^T的中位数范数等于各仿射层和训练检查点中原始权重梯度中位数范数的0.664倍。更引人注目的是，||ΔWμ||/||Δb||的中位数为134.7，而||ΔWμ||/||Δb+ΔWμ||的中位数为0.994。因此，在AdamW优化下，观察到的边界运动几乎完全通过权重矩阵而非显式偏置实现。我们实现了一个诊断性优化器——形状-边界正交AdamW（SBO-AdamW），该优化器使用独立的Adam状态优化g_W-g_bμ^T和g_b，并补偿权重引起的边界位移。在单种子实验中，SBO-AdamW将验证准确率从81.68%提升至85.81%，将基于验证集选择的测试准确率从78.73%提升至82.73%，最佳验证检查点出现在第800步而非第3000步。然而，移动批中心补偿导致了严重的偏置坐标漂移，并显著降低了边界能量。因此，现有证据支持隐藏边界运动是一种重要的优化机制，但尚未能建立最终的通用优化器。下一步需要一种稳定的中心化仿射参数化方案。

</details>


### [220] [Distribution-Specific Curvature Control with Finite-Sample Guarantees for Open-Weight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22929)
*Domenic Rosati,Ali Dadsetan,Hong Huang,Xijie Zeng,Hassan Chowdhry,Subhabrata Majumdar,Hassan Sajjad,Frank Rudzicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HarmAlign方法，通过在估计的对比激活子空间上进行保函数谱变形，在保持良性任务可训练性的同时，防止开源权重模型被恶意微调破坏安全防护。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有安全防护方法（如谱变形）因全局曲率膨胀而阻碍良性适应，缺乏既能防止有害微调又不影响良性任务可训练性的方法。

Method: HarmAlign方法：在估计的对比激活子空间上进行保函数谱变形，推导子空间能量的有限样本界和局部有害分布曲率下界，利用稳定性-进展二分法实现条件收敛速率控制。

Result: 在固定架构、有限预算的一阶威胁模型下，HarmAlign能阻止直接微调和三种数据/目标自适应攻击，同时保护良性任务的可训练性；该防护在多种一阶优化器变体、分布外有害微调场景下持续有效，并扩展到意外安全退化和突现错配等场景。

Conclusion: HarmAlign通过局部曲率约束实现了安全防护与良性适应的平衡，为开源模型的安全微调防护提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: A short fine-tuning run can undo the safety guards of an open-weight model---retraining a refusal-trained assistant to aid weapons development or produce hate speech. Preventing such harmful fine-tuning while retaining benign adaptability remains difficult: the only prior method with an explicit curvature certificate, spectral deformation, inflates curvature globally and thereby obstructs benign adaptation along with harmful adaptation. We propose HarmAlign, which applies function-preserving spectral deformation along a estimated contrastive activation subspace. We derive finite-sample bounds for the estimated subspace energy and the resulting local harmful-distribution curvature lower bound. A stability--progress dichotomy for constant-step gradient descent turns the certified curvature into conditional convergence-rate control. Empirically, within a fixed-architecture, finite-budget first-order threat model, HarmAlign blocks direct fine-tuning and three data- or objective-adaptive attacks across a hazardous-knowledge relearning setting and a harmful-assistance fine-tuning setting, while the protected benign tasks remain trainable. The block persists across the tested first-order optimizer variants over every attack checkpoint, and under out-of-distribution harmful fine-tuning, and it extends to important cases in our threat model: accidental safety degradation and emergent misalignment.

中文摘要翻译: 短时间的微调可以解除开源权重模型的安全防护——重新训练一个经过拒答训练的模型，使其协助武器研发或生成仇恨言论。在保持良性适应能力的同时防止此类有害微调仍然困难：先前唯一具有显式曲率保证的方法（谱变形）会全局性地膨胀曲率，从而同时阻碍了良性适应和有害适应。我们提出了HarmAlign，该方法在估计的对比激活子空间上应用保函数谱变形。我们推导了估计子空间能量的有限样本界以及由此产生的局部有害分布曲率下界。针对常数步长梯度下降的稳定性-进展二分法，将认证的曲率转化为条件收敛速率控制。实验上，在固定架构、有限预算的一阶威胁模型下，HarmAlign在一个危险知识重新学习设置和一个有害协助微调设置中，阻断了直接微调和三种数据或目标自适应攻击，同时受保护的良性任务仍然可训练。该阻断在测试的一阶优化器变体上的每个攻击检查点持续有效，在分布外有害微调下同样有效，并且扩展到我们威胁模型中的重要情况：意外安全退化和突现错配。

</details>


### [221] [Spectral-Aware Analytic Class-Incremental Learning for Long-Tailed Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22931)
*Quyen Tran,Hai Nguyen,Quan Dao,Zhuowei Li,Nam Le,Trung Le,Dimitris Metaxas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR)框架，通过各向异性谱滤波解决解析持续学习中长尾类别带来的谱坍塌问题，在类别增量学习场景下实现了新的最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有解析持续学习（ACL）方法在长尾分布下的类增量学习中表现不佳，原因是类别不平衡加剧了Gram矩阵的病态问题：尾部类别发生严重的谱坍塌，其特征子空间在数值上与噪声难以区分。标准Ridge回归（各向同性正则化）对所有特征值施加均匀惩罚，无法在不过度收缩头部类别的情况下稳定尾部类别。

Method: 提出Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR)框架，将长尾学习视为谱正则化问题。GSR作为一种各向异性谱滤波器，构建结构化的数据依赖谱扰动矩阵Δ，选择性地膨胀Gram矩阵中尾部类别坍塌的特征方向，从而改善Gram矩阵的稳定秩。

Result: 理论分析证明GSR能够保证Gram矩阵的稳定秩得到改善，确保数值稳定性。大量实验表明GSR在长尾设置下的解析类增量学习（CIL）中取得了新的最优结果，在计算效率与鲁棒泛化之间实现了更优的权衡。

Conclusion: GSR框架通过各向异性谱正则化有效解决了长尾分布下解析持续学习中的谱坍塌问题，为长尾类增量学习提供了一种理论上严谨且计算高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Analytic Continual Learning (ACL) offers a computationally efficient alternative to gradient-based approaches. Recent ACL methods are based on Recursive Least Squares (RLS) and have achieved the state-of-the-art results compared to other alternatives. However, they falter significantly in Class-Incremental Learning scenarios characterized by Long-Tailed distributions. While the ill-conditioning of the autocorrelation (Gram) matrix is a known limitation of RLS, we demonstrate that class imbalance exacerbates this issue into a distinct spectral pathology: "tail" classes suffer from severe spectral collapse, rendering their subspaces numerically indistinguishable from noise. Standard Ridge Regression ($L_2$) fails to address this effectively as it applies isotropic regularization - a uniform penalty that is insufficient to stabilize the tail without over-shrinking the head. To address this, we propose Geometry-Spectral Rectification (GSR), a theoretically grounded framework that treats long-tailed learning as a spectral regularization problem. Unlike standard isotropic regularization (Ridge) which uniformly penalizes all eigenvalues, GSR acts as an anisotropic spectral filter, selectively inflating the collapsed eigenvalues of tail classes. We construct a structured, data-dependent spectral perturbation matrix $Δ$ that selectively inflates collapsed tail eigen-directions of the Gram matrix. Theoretical analysis proves that GSR guarantees an improved stable rank for the Gram matrix, ensuring numerical stability. Extensive experiments show that GSR establishes a new state-of-the-art for analytic CIL, offering a superior trade-off between computational efficiency and robust generalization in long-tailed settings.

中文摘要翻译: 解析持续学习（ACL）为基于梯度的学习方法提供了一种计算高效的替代方案。最近的ACL方法基于递归最小二乘法（RLS），相比其他替代方法取得了最先进的结果。然而，在以长尾分布为特征的类增量学习场景中，这些方法表现明显不佳。尽管自相关（Gram）矩阵的病态性是RLS的一个已知局限，但我们证明类别不平衡会将这一问题加剧为一种独特的谱病理现象："尾部"类别遭受严重的谱坍塌，使其子空间在数值上与噪声难以区分。标准岭回归（$L_2$）无法有效解决此问题，因为它施加的是各向同性正则化——一种均匀的惩罚项，不足以在不过度收缩头部类别的情况下稳定尾部类别。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了几何-谱矫正（GSR）框架，这是一个理论上有充分依据的框架，将长尾学习视为一个谱正则化问题。与标准各向同性正则化（岭回归）对所有特征值施加均匀惩罚不同，GSR作为一种各向异性谱滤波器，选择性地膨胀尾部类别的坍塌特征值。我们构建了一个结构化的、数据依赖的谱扰动矩阵$Δ$，能够选择性地膨胀Gram矩阵中尾部类别坍塌的特征方向。理论分析证明，GSR能够保证Gram矩阵的稳定秩得到改善，确保数值稳定性。大量实验表明，GSR在解析CIL中建立了新的最优结果，在长尾设置下提供了计算效率与鲁棒泛化之间的优越权衡。

</details>


### [222] [Discrepancy-Rounded Fair Bandits with Static and Time-Varying Exposure Floors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22935)
*Ibne Farabi Shihab,Joyanta Jyoti Mondal,Anuj Sharma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机bandit中带有精确曝光下限（minimum-exposure constraints）的问题，提出了块状时间变化地板模型BDQ-UCB算法，通过Beck-Fiala零空间舍入处理重叠组约束，并在理论 regret 界和实验中验证了方法的可行性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在推荐系统、内容策展和资源分配中，每个提供者或组必须在周期内获得保证的曝光量，而不仅仅是累积平均。现有的bandit方法处理的是聚合约束，缺乏对周期性精确曝光下限的保证，因此需要一种新的建模和算法框架。

Method: 提出块状（blockwise）时间变化地板模型，将分数公平调度舍入为整数拉取，曝光误差视为差异向量。BDQ-UCB算法确定性满足每个块地板，regret由非强制预算R控制。对于重叠组地板，采用Beck-Fiala零空间舍入，确保每个组地板在块预算内满足，违规度低于臂度数t。还提出了MOSS残差变体、kl-UCB$^{++}$残差规则以及双账本分解和计划采样规则。

Result: BDQ-UCB达到高概率regret $O(\sqrt{KR\log(KT)})$；MOSS残差变体达到$O(\sqrt{KR})$；匹配下界给出极小化最优率$\Theta(\sqrt{KR})$。重叠组约束下，每臂舍入会导致$\Omega(s)$违规，而Beck-Fiala舍入将违规控制在臂度数t以下。实验在合成地板、MovieLens-100k类型曝光和压力测试中验证了方法的精确可行性和与调优拉格朗日基线相当的regret。

Conclusion: 本文建立的块状曝光地板模型和对应的舍入方法为精确曝光约束下的bandit问题提供了理论完备且实践有效的解决方案，尤其对重叠组约束场景具有关键优势，但条件无关的重叠下界仍为开放问题。

Original Abstract: Minimum-exposure constraints arise in recommendation, content curation, and regulated allocation when each provider, arm, or group must receive guaranteed exposure inside a period rather than only in aggregate. We study stochastic bandits with exact exposure floors and show that the right object is a rounding problem: a fractional fair schedule is realized as integral pulls, and the exposure error is exactly a discrepancy vector. The main contribution is a blockwise model with time-varying floors. BDQ-UCB satisfies every block floor deterministically and has fair regret governed by the nonmandatory budget $R$, not the horizon $T$, with high-probability regret $O(\sqrt{KR\log(KT)})$. A MOSS residual variant attains $O(\sqrt{KR})$, and a matching lower bound gives the minimax rate $Θ(\sqrt{KR})$, even with positive mandatory exposure; a kl-UCB$^{++}$ residual rule adds instance-dependent optimality. The formulation becomes essential for overlapping group floors: per-arm rounding can violate a group constraint by $Ω(s)$ in the group size, whereas Beck--Fiala null-space rounding meets every group floor within the block budget with violation below the arm degree $t$, and composes with UCB at the same $R$-parametrized regret. For learned group plans, we close disjoint systems at $\widetildeΘ(\sqrt{KT})$, give a dual-ledger decomposition explaining why naive index rules fail under overlap, and prove a plan-sampling rule that is pathwise feasible under an initial cover-slack condition and attains a conditional $\widetilde O(\sqrt{KT})$ guarantee, leaving the condition-free overlap rate open. Experiments on synthetic floors, MovieLens-100k genre exposure, and deployment stress tests show exact feasibility without penalty tuning and regret competitive with tuned Lagrangian baselines.

中文摘要翻译: 最低曝光约束出现在推荐系统、内容策展和资源配置中，要求每个提供者、臂或组在一个周期内（而非仅累积地）获得保证的曝光量。我们研究了具有精确曝光地板的随机bandit问题，并表明正确的对象是一个舍入问题：分数公平调度被实现为整数拉取，而曝光误差恰好是一个差异向量。主要贡献是一个具有时间变化地板的块状模型。BDQ-UCB算法确定性满足每个块地板，其公平regret由非强制预算$R$（而非时间$T$）控制，高概率regret为$O(\sqrt{KR\log(KT)})$。MOSS残差变体达到$O(\sqrt{KR})$，匹配下界给出极小化最优率$\Theta(\sqrt{KR})$，即使存在正强制曝光；kl-UCB$^{++}$残差规则增加了实例依赖最优性。该公式对于重叠组地板至关重要：每臂舍入会导致组约束违反量达到组大小的$\Omega(s)$，而Beck–Fiala零空间舍入在块预算内满足每个组地板，违规度低于臂度数$t$，并与UCB组合得到相同的$R$参数化regret。针对学习到的组计划，我们将不相交系统闭合在$\widetilde\Theta(\sqrt{KT})$，给出一个双账本分解来解释朴素索引规则为何在重叠下失败，并证明了一个计划采样规则，该规则在初始覆盖松弛条件下路径可行，并达到条件性$\widetilde O(\sqrt{KT})$保证，但条件无关的重叠率仍是开放问题。在合成地板、MovieLens-100k类型曝光和部署压力测试上的实验表明，该方法无需惩罚调优即可精确可行，且regret与调优的拉格朗日基线相当。

</details>


### [223] [Verbalized Particle Posterior: Bayesian Inference over Natural Language Hypotheses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22961)
*Yan Zhang,Shikan Lian,Shibo Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Verbalized Particle Posterior (VPP)，将口头化机器学习视为贝叶斯推断问题，通过粒子滤波和贝叶斯模型平均提升不确定性量化与鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: Verbalized Machine Learning (VML) 可解释但缺乏不确定性度量，且单次运行假设差异大、容易失败，需要一种能够量化不确定性并提升鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 将口头化学习建模为贝叶斯推断问题：维护一组自然语言假设作为粒子，使用 Metropolis-Hastings (VPP-MH) 或序贯蒙特卡洛 (VPP-SMC) 更新粒子，并通过贝叶斯模型平均进行预测。LLM 仅作为黑盒调用，无需访问 logits 或梯度。

Result: 在回归、分类和规则发现基准上，VPP 在每个基准上都优于单次 VML 运行，在大多数基准上匹配或超过独立 VML 运行的最优集成，并消除了 VML 偶尔产生的灾难性单次运行失败。

Conclusion: VPP 通过将口头化学习置于贝叶斯框架下，不仅提升了预测性能和鲁棒性，还因每个粒子是人类可读的假设，使得后验本身可被读者检查，清晰展示数据支持或排除的解释。

Original Abstract: Verbalized Machine Learning (VML) parameterizes a model as a natural-language prompt that an LLM evaluates as f(x; theta). The framework is interpretable, but it commits to a single hypothesis with no measure of uncertainty, and that hypothesis varies substantially across optimization runs on the same data. We propose the Verbalized Particle Posterior (VPP), which treats verbalized learning as a Bayesian inference problem: maintain a population of natural-language hypotheses as particles, update them with Metropolis-Hastings (VPP-MH) or Sequential Monte Carlo (VPP-SMC), and predict by Bayesian model averaging. Both algorithms treat the LLM as a black box, requiring no access to logits or gradients. A distinctive consequence follows. In classical Bayesian learning, model selection sits outside the posterior; in VPP both model structure and parameters share a single language space, and the posterior ranges over both. We evaluate VPP on regression, classification, and rule-discovery benchmarks. It improves over a single VML run on every benchmark and matches or exceeds an oracle-best ensemble of independent VML runs on most, while eliminating the catastrophic single-run failures that VML occasionally produces. Because each particle is a human-readable hypothesis, the posterior is itself something a reader can inspect, seeing in plain text which explanations the data supported and which it ruled out.

中文摘要翻译: 口头化机器学习（VML）将模型参数化为自然语言提示，由大语言模型作为 f(x; θ) 进行评估。该框架具有可解释性，但它致力于单一假设且缺乏不确定性度量，并且在相同数据上的多次优化运行中，该假设存在显著差异。我们提出口头化粒子后验（VPP），将口头化学习视为贝叶斯推断问题：维护一个自然语言假设的群体作为粒子，通过 Metropolis-Hastings（VPP-MH）或序贯蒙特卡洛（VPP-SMC）更新它们，并通过贝叶斯模型平均进行预测。两种算法都将 LLM 视为黑盒，无需访问 logits 或梯度。由此产生一个独特的结论。在经典贝叶斯学习中，模型选择位于后验之外；而在 VPP 中，模型结构和参数共享同一个语言空间，后验覆盖两者。我们在回归、分类和规则发现基准上评估 VPP。它在每个基准上都优于单次 VML 运行，在大多数基准上匹配或超过独立 VML 运行的最优集成，同时消除了 VML 偶尔产生的灾难性单次运行失败。由于每个粒子都是人类可读的假设，后验本身可供读者检查，以纯文本形式查看数据支持了哪些解释以及排除了哪些解释。

</details>


### [224] [Learned Interventions in Lean 4 grind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22972)
*Evan Wang,Simon Chess,Sophie Szeto,Theodore Meek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种失败触发的学习级联策略，在Lean 4的grind策略的启发式决策中引入学习模型，仅在原有策略失败后才介入，从而确保不损失原有证明能力。实验表明，成本感知的e-match过滤器略微提升求解率和速度，而lookahead步骤能证明原本超时的定理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: grind策略依赖手工调优的启发式规则来决定实例化和case-split，这些启发式规则是学习的理想目标。但由于grind的搜索是非单调的，学习到的启发式规则在帮助某个证明的同时可能破坏另一个证明，导致整体增益接近于零。因此亟需一种不损害原有证明能力的学习方法。

Method: 提出失败触发的级联策略（failure-triggered cascade）：仅在标准grind策略失败后才调用学习到的干预措施，通过构造保证不会丢失grind原本已经能完成的证明。将该方法应用于grind的两个内部决策：（1）成本感知的e-match过滤器；（2）lookahead步骤。

Result: （1）成本感知的e-match过滤器能求解更多问题，且运行速度提升约5%；（2）lookahead步骤能证明五个原本超时的定理；（3）静态预测正确的case-split不比随机好，因为分裂是否爆炸是运行时属性，特征无法捕获。

Conclusion: 在定理证明策略中，学习最有效的应用方式是作为决定何时以及如何花费有限搜索的机制，并以可靠的符号化回退作为后盾。

Original Abstract: Lean~4's \grind{} tactic combines congruence closure, \ematch{}ing, and case-splitting into a single automated solver, and like any such solver, it relies on hand-tuned heuristics to decide what to instantiate and where to case-split. These heuristics are tempting targets for learning, but there is a catch: because \grind{}'s search is non-monotone, a learned heuristic that helps one proof can break another, and an always-on replacement usually nets out near zero. We avoid this by invoking a learned intervention only after stock \grind{} has already failed: a failure-triggered cascade that, by construction, cannot lose a proof \grind{} already had. We apply it to two of \grind{}'s internal decisions. A cost-aware \ematch{} filter solves slightly more problems and runs about 5\% faster. A lookahead step, proves five theorems it otherwise times out on. We also report the negative result that motivated the design: across four feature-based models, statically predicting the correct case split is no better than random, because whether a split explodes is a runtime property that the features do not capture. Our results suggest that learning within theorem-proving tactics is most effective as a mechanism for deciding when and how to spend bounded search, backed by a reliable symbolic fallback.

中文摘要翻译: Lean 4的grind策略将同余闭包、e-match和case-split整合为单一的自动化求解器。与同类求解器一样，它依赖手工调优的启发式规则来决定实例化什么以及在哪里进行case-split。这些启发式规则是学习的理想目标，但存在一个问题：由于grind的搜索是非单调的，学习到的启发式规则在帮助某个证明的同时可能破坏另一个证明，而一个始终开启的替代方案通常净收益接近于零。我们通过仅在标准grind策略已经失败后才调用学习到的干预来避免这一问题：一种失败触发的级联策略，通过构造保证不会丢失grind原本已经能完成的证明。我们将其应用于grind的两个内部决策。一个成本感知的e-match过滤器能求解稍多的问题，且运行速度快约5%。一个lookahead步骤能证明五个原本超时的定理。我们还报告了促使这一设计的负面结果：在四个基于特征的模型上，静态预测正确的case-split不比随机好，因为分裂是否爆炸是一个运行时属性，特征无法捕获。我们的结果表明，在定理证明策略中，学习最有效的应用方式是作为决定何时以及如何花费有限搜索的机制，并以可靠的符号化回退作为后盾。

</details>


### [225] [Bayesian Complete-Pooling in Cross-Subject Classification for Motor Imagery Electroencephalogram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22980)
*Ethan Davis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大规模研究表明，贝叶斯完全池化模型在跨被试运动想象脑电解码中，虽然统计上改善了可靠性并增加了预测不确定性，但实际收益有限，且能耗约为频率派方法的13倍，部分池化可能是更有前景的方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 脑机接口长期追求免校准操作，但现有分类器仅关注判别性能，忽略了预测概率的校准质量。考虑到EEG信号的非平稳性和分布漂移下点估计分类器过度自信的风险，有必要系统评估贝叶斯方法在概率校准方面的实际收益。

Method: 在20个数据集上，对比6对频率派和贝叶斯完全池化管线（共享相同特征工程，贝叶斯管线采用MCMC后验采样）。以Brier分数（分解为可靠性和分辨率）为主要指标，辅以AUROC（判别能力）和香农熵（锐度），通过随机效应元分析（REML，Knapp-Hartung调整）和留一法影响分析进行统计验证。

Result: 贝叶斯完全池化在可靠性上产生统计显著但非实际显著的改进，增加了预测不确定性（锐度降低）；Brier分数、分辨率和判别能力无显著差异。研究间异质性低，但可靠性结果对留一法移除敏感。贝叶斯管线能耗约为频率派管线的13倍，但相对于常用家电仍算适度。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯完全池化单独用于跨被试运动想象分类的实用收益有限，跨被试和跨会话的部分池化是更具前景的未来方向。

Original Abstract: Brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) have long sought calibration-free operation, but classifiers are typically benchmarked by discrimination alone, blind to whether predicted probabilities are well calibrated - a meaningful gap given nonstationary electroencephalogram (EEG) signals and the risk of overconfident point-estimate classifiers under distribution shift. We conducted a large-scale study contrasting Bayesian complete-pooling models against frequentist baselines for cross-subject, left-hand versus right-hand motor imagery EEG classification across 20 datasets. Six frequentist pipelines were each paired with an analogous Bayesian pipeline sharing identical feature engineering, fit via Markov chain Monte Carlo posterior sampling. Our primary metric was the Brier score, decomposed into reliability and resolution, alongside AUROC for discrimination and Shannon entropy for sharpness. Each metric was analyzed via random-effects meta-analysis (REML, Knapp-Hartung adjustment), verified by leave-one-out influence analysis. Bayesian complete-pooling produced statistically but not practically significant improvements in reliability and increases in predictive uncertainty (lower sharpness); Brier score, resolution, and discrimination showed no significant differences. Between-study heterogeneity was low across all metrics, though the reliability result was sensitive to leave-one-out removal. We additionally profiled computational cost, finding that Bayesian pipelines consumed roughly thirteen times more energy than their frequentist counterparts, a cost that remains modest relative to common household appliances. These results suggest that Bayesian complete-pooling alone offers limited practical benefit for cross-subject motor imagery classification, and that partial-pooling across subjects and sessions is a more promising direction for future work.

中文摘要翻译: 脑机接口（BCI）长期以来一直追求免校准操作，但分类器通常仅通过判别能力进行基准测试，忽略了预测概率是否得到良好校准——考虑到非平稳脑电图（EEG）信号以及点估计分类器在分布漂移下过度自信的风险，这是一个有意义的差距。我们开展了一项大规模研究，在20个数据集上对比了贝叶斯完全池化模型与频率派基线方法用于跨被试左手与右手运动想象脑电解码。六个频率派管线各自与一个共享完全相同特征工程的贝叶斯管线配对，后者通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛后验采样进行拟合。我们的主要指标是Brier分数（分解为可靠性和分辨率），辅以AUROC（判别能力）和香农熵（锐度）。每个指标均通过随机效应元分析（REML，Knapp-Hartung调整）进行分析，并通过留一法影响分析验证。贝叶斯完全池化在可靠性上产生了统计显著但非实际显著的改进，并增加了预测不确定性（锐度降低）；Brier分数、分辨率和判别能力未见显著差异。所有指标的研究间异质性较低，但可靠性结果对留一法移除敏感。我们还分析了计算成本，发现贝叶斯管线的能耗约为频率派管线的13倍，这一成本相对于常见家用电器仍然适度。这些结果表明，仅使用贝叶斯完全池化对跨被试运动想象分类的实用收益有限，而跨被试和跨会话的部分池化是未来工作中更有前景的方向。

</details>


### [226] [Finite-Time Analysis of the Natural Policy Gradient in Finite-Horizon Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22982)
*Asha Barua,Sajad Khodadadian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次为有限时域且转移核依赖于时域的已知动力学马尔可夫决策过程中的精确自然策略梯度算法提供了有限时间收敛保证，证明了常数步长下的次线性收敛率和递增步长下的线性收敛率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自然策略梯度是强化学习中重要的基础算法，但其在有限时域设定下（转移核依赖于时域）的收敛性理论上尚未得到充分理解，缺乏有限时间收敛保证。

Method: 研究了精确NPG在有限时域MDP中的收敛性，考虑了常数步长和递增步长两种情形；在常数步长下证明了次线性收敛率，并将其推广到线性MDP；在递增步长下设计了仅依赖于时域长度的稳健调度方案，实现了几何收敛率。

Result: 常数步长下NPG以$\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$速率次线性收敛；递增步长下以$\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\vartheta_ρ}\right)^t\right)$速率线性收敛，其中步长调度$\eta_t=\eta_0(H/(H-1))^t$可达到该几何速率。

Conclusion: 为精确NPG在有限时域设定下建立了完整的收敛理论，填补了该算法在该场景下的理论空白，并为实际应用中的步长选择提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: Natural Policy Gradient (NPG) is a well-established Reinforcement Learning algorithm that underlies widely used methods such as Trust Region Policy Optimization and Proximal Policy Optimization, both of which have demonstrated strong empirical success. In this paper, we study exact NPG in finite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with known dynamics and horizon-dependent transition kernels. We provide the first finite-time convergence guarantees for this algorithm in this setting, for which we consider both constant and increasing step size regimes. With a constant step size $η_t=η$, we prove that NPG converges sublinearly with a rate of $\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$ after $t$ iterations, where $H$ is the horizon length. We also extend this constant step size analysis to linear MDPs in an exact population-projection oracle under a full support projection distribution, recovering the same sublinear rate as in the tabular setting. Furthermore, with increasing step sizes, we prove that this algorithm achieves a linear convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\vartheta_ρ}\right)^t\right)$ for a problem-dependent constant $\vartheta_ρ> 1$, and the horizon-only robust schedule of the form $η_t=η_0(H/(H-1))^t$ where $η_0>0$ and $H \geq 2$, attains this same geometric rate.

中文摘要翻译: 自然策略梯度（NPG）是一种成熟的强化学习算法，它支撑着信任区域策略优化和近端策略优化等广泛使用的方法，这些方法均已展现出强大的实证成功。本文研究了在动力学已知且转移核依赖于时域的有限时域马尔可夫决策过程中的精确NPG算法。我们首次为该设定下的该算法提供了有限时间收敛保证，并考虑了常数步长与递增步长两种情形。在常数步长$η_t=η$下，我们证明了经过$t$次迭代后，NPG以$\mathcal{O}(H^{2}/t)$的速率次线性收敛，其中$H$为时域长度。我们将此常数步长分析扩展到线性MDP，在支持集为全支撑的投影分布下使用精确种群投影预言机，恢复了与表格设定相同的次线性速率。此外，在递增步长下，我们证明了该算法以$\mathcal{O}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{\vartheta_ρ}\right)^t\right)$的速率实现线性收敛，其中$\vartheta_ρ>1$为依赖于问题的常数；而仅依赖于时域的鲁棒调度方案$\eta_t=\eta_0(H/(H-1))^t$（其中$\eta_0>0$且$H \geq 2$）同样达到了这一几何速率。

</details>


### [227] [Label-free Industrial Fault Detection via Adversarial Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A System for Run-to-Failure Prognostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22987)
*Dhiraj Neupane,Mohamed Reda Bouadjenek,Richard Dazeley,Sunil Aryal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出对抗性逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，将机械故障检测（MFD）作为离线逆强化学习问题处理，从观测状态转换中恢复内在的“健康”奖励，无需故障标签或人工奖励工程。在三个数据集上，AIRL是唯一实现非饱和检测后一致性的方法，优于上下文赌博机（CB）和重构基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有MFD方法严重依赖监督学习，但故障标签在实际中极为稀缺；当前“基于RL”的MFD方法退化为静态上下文赌博机（CB）公式，忽略了状态转换和时间折扣因子，本质上等同于标准监督分类。

Method: 提出对抗性逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，将MFD建模为离线逆强化学习问题。通过从观测状态转换中恢复内在的“健康”奖励，无需手工设计奖励函数和故障标签，利用状态转移的动态信息而非静态误差阈值。

Result: 在HUMS2023、IMS、XJTU-SY三个运行至失效基准数据集上，AIRL是唯一在所有数据集上实现非饱和检测后一致性的方法。CB基线无法检测渐进退化，重构模型崩溃为始终异常的误报状态。

Conclusion: AIRL通过逆强化学习有效捕获机械系统的退化动态，实现了无需故障标签的可靠故障检测，优于现有CB和重构方法。

Original Abstract: Machinery fault detection (MFD) remains heavily reliant on supervised learning, which struggles with the scarcity of fault labels in real-world settings. While reinforcement learning (RL) offers a framework to model the sequential nature of degradation, current ``RL-based'' MFD methods reduce the problem to a static contextual bandit (CB) formulation: by ignoring state transitions and discarding the temporal discount factor, they collapse to standard supervised classification. We propose an adversarial inverse reinforcement learning (AIRL) framework that treats MFD as an offline IRL problem. Unlike reconstruction-based approaches that rely on static error margins, or CBs that ignore dynamics, our method recovers an intrinsic "health" reward directly from observational state transitions, requiring neither manual reward engineering nor fault labels. On three run-to-failure benchmarks (HUMS2023, IMS, XJTU-SY), AIRL is the only method achieving non-saturated post-detection consistency across all datasets, while CB baselines fail to detect gradual degradation and reconstruction models collapse into always-anomalous states. Code and data: https://github.com/dhirajneupane/AIRL-MFD-DN.

中文摘要翻译: 机械故障检测（MFD）仍然严重依赖监督学习，但在实际场景中故障标签极度匮乏。虽然强化学习（RL）为建模退化的序列特性提供了框架，但当前"基于RL"的MFD方法将问题退化为静态上下文赌博机（CB）公式：由于忽略了状态转移并丢弃了时间折扣因子，它们沦为了标准的监督分类。我们提出一种对抗性逆强化学习（AIRL）框架，将MFD视为离线逆强化学习问题。与依赖静态误差阈值的重构方法或忽视动态特性的CB方法不同，我们的方法直接从观测状态转换中恢复一个内在的"健康"奖励，既不需要手动设计奖励，也不需要故障标签。在三个运行至失效基准数据集（HUMS2023、IMS、XJTU-SY）上，AIRL是唯一在所有数据集上实现非饱和检测后一致性的方法，而CB基线无法检测渐进退化，重构模型则崩溃为始终异常的误报状态。代码和数据：https://github.com/dhirajneupane/AIRL-MFD-DN。

</details>


### [228] [Recycling computational processes of dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems: a reservoir computing approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23009)
*Sora Todaka,Akihiro Yamamoto,Nozomi Akashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于机器学习的方法，通过复用动态规划计算中的中间结果作为特征，来辅助求解多个组合优化问题，从而减少计算开销并提高近似精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统计算中结果复用仅限于同一问题的计算，无法在多个同时求解的问题之间共享计算过程。手动设计能利用跨任务关系的算法十分困难，因此需要自动发现这类算法的方法。

Method: 基于储层计算（reservoir computing）思想，将动态规划求解组合优化问题时记录的计算结果作为线性回归的特征，利用这些特征辅助其他组合优化问题的求解。

Result: 在旅行商问题和子集和问题上的实验表明，复用动态规划过程相比使用通用特征能提升近似精度，且相较于独立求解能减少计算时间。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新型计算范式，多个计算过程可以高效地共享和回收中间结果与状态，区别于传统的计算设计方式。

Original Abstract: Reusing previously computed results is a long-standing principle for reducing computational cost, but such reuse has largely been confined to a single problem's computation. Sharing computational processes across multiple simultaneously solved problems remains possible in principle, yet designing algorithms that exploit nontrivial cross-task relationships is difficult to do manually. Here, we use machine learning to discover such algorithms automatically. Specifically, based on reservoir computing, we propose a method that uses computation results recorded by dynamic programming for combinatorial optimization problems as features for linear regression, leveraging them to assist other combinatorial optimization computations. We validate the approach on the traveling salesman and subset sum problems. Multiplexing the dynamic programming process improves approximation accuracy over generic features and reduces computation time compared with independent solutions. These results suggest a new form of computation, distinct from conventional computational design, in which multiple processes efficiently share and recycle intermediate results and states.

中文摘要翻译: 重复使用先前计算的结果是降低计算成本的长期原则，但这种复用主要局限于单一问题的计算。在多个同时求解的问题之间共享计算过程在原则上是可行的，然而手动设计能够利用非平凡跨任务关系的算法十分困难。本文利用机器学习来自动发现此类算法。具体而言，基于储层计算，我们提出了一种方法：将动态规划求解组合优化问题时记录的计算结果作为线性回归的特征，利用这些特征辅助其他组合优化计算。我们在旅行商问题和子集和问题上验证了该方法。复用动态规划过程相比通用特征提高了近似精度，且相较于独立求解减少了计算时间。这些结果表明了一种新型的计算范式，区别于传统的计算设计，其中多个计算过程能够高效地共享和回收中间结果与状态。

</details>


### [229] [Mini-batch Noise Lowers Sharpness via Dominant-Subspace Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23012)
*Junho So,Dongwook Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了SGD训练中Hessian矩阵主导子空间的作用，指出该子空间并非主要用于降低损失，而是解释小批量SGD锐度动态的关键。通过引入锐度校正项，使梯度下降的锐度演化更接近SGD。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究表明，SGD训练中梯度与Hessian矩阵top-k特征向量构成的主导子空间强对齐，但该子空间内的更新对损失降低无实质贡献，因此需要重新理解该子空间的真正作用。

Method: 理论分析了主导方向上的波动平均梯度如何产生锐度校正项，并推导了由小批量噪声在主导方向上诱导的锐度校正项。实验上将该校正项添加到GD中，观察锐度演化的变化。

Result: 实验结果表明，将推导的锐度校正项添加到梯度下降（GD）中，能使GD的锐度演化更接近小批量SGD的锐度演化。

Conclusion: Hessian矩阵的主导子空间主要作用并非降低损失，而是通过主导方向上的噪声波动产生锐度校正，从而解释SGD的锐度动态。

Original Abstract: During SGD training, the gradients often align strongly with the dominant subspace spanned by the top-$k$ eigenvectors of the Hessian of the loss. While this seems to naturally imply that loss reduction mainly occurs within this space, prior work has shown that updates within this dominant subspace make no meaningful progress in reducing the loss. In this work, we argue that the dominant subspace is better understood not as the main space for loss reduction, but as a key subspace for explaining the sharpness dynamics of mini-batch SGD. To explain the role of the dominant subspace in reducing top-$k$ sharpness, we show how the averaged gradient over fluctuations in the dominant directions produces a sharpness correction term, and derive a sharpness correction term induced by mini-batch noise in the dominant directions. Experimental results show that adding the derived correction term to GD brings the sharpness evolution of GD closer to that of SGD.

中文摘要翻译: 在SGD训练过程中，梯度通常与损失函数Hessian矩阵的top-k特征向量所构成的主导子空间强烈对齐。虽然这似乎自然意味着损失降低主要发生在这个空间内，但先前的工作表明，在该主导子空间内的更新对减少损失没有实质性进展。在这项工作中，我们认为主导子空间更适合被理解为不是损失降低的主要空间，而是解释小批量SGD锐度动态的关键子空间。为了解释主导子空间在降低top-k锐度中的作用，我们展示了主导方向上的波动平均梯度如何产生一个锐度校正项，并推导了由小批量噪声在主导方向上诱导的锐度校正项。实验结果表明，将推导的校正项添加到梯度下降（GD）中，能使GD的锐度演化更接近SGD的锐度演化。

</details>


### [230] [All in One: Generative Modeling as Mean-Field Game Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23026)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MFGLab开源库，将均值场博弈（MFG）框架统一应用于连续时间生成建模，覆盖12种经典模型；并提出DI-Flow新方法，通过可微熵函数促进模式覆盖，以及基于学习的MFG求解器，在随机动力学行上显著优于神经训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有连续时间生成模型虽可从MFG统一视角看待，但有两个维度未被探索：交互项在多数模型中为零，且丰富的MFG求解器从未被用于生成建模。

Method: 提出MFGLab开源库，以四个可组合代价函数指定所有12种模型，自动共享训练循环、log-Jacobian和反向ODE采样器；并提出DI-Flow，使用可微熵函数鼓励模式覆盖；同时提供基于学习的MFG求解器。

Result: 在2维基准上的实验表明，统一API相对于手工实现是无损的；DI-Flow和MFG求解器在随机动力学行上显著优于神经训练基线。

Conclusion: MFG统一框架可无损覆盖多种生成模型，且引入交互项和MFG求解器能进一步提升性能，为生成建模提供了新的理论工具和实用库。

Original Abstract: Mean-field games (MFGs) offer a unifying lens on continuous-time generative modeling: a cost tuple recovering twelve prominent models---Continuous Normalizing Flows, OT-Flow, Score-based Models, Schrödinger Bridges, and more---as special cases of one variational problem.
