Yet two dimensions of this space remain entirely unexplored: the interaction term $\mathcal{I}$ is set to zero in many existing models, and the rich family of MFG solvers has never been applied to generative modeling. We address both gaps with MFGLab an open-source PyTorch library whose primary API is the cost tuple: all twelve models are specified by four composable cost functions, and the training loop, log-Jacobian, and reverse-ODE sampler are shared automatically. We additionally propose DI-Flow, a novel cost design that uses a differentiable entropy functional to encourage mode coverage, and provide learning-based MFG solvers that substantially outperform neural training on stochastic-dynamics rows. Experiments on two 2-D benchmarks confirm that the unified API is lossless relative to hand-coded implementations.

中文摘要翻译: 均值场博弈（MFGs）为连续时间生成建模提供了一个统一的视角：一个代价元组可将12种著名模型——包括连续归一化流、OT-Flow、基于分数的模型、薛定谔桥等——恢复为同一变分问题的特例。然而，这一空间的两个维度至今完全未被探索：交互项ℐ在现有许多模型中被设为零，且丰富的MFG求解器从未被应用于生成建模。我们通过MFGLab——一个开源的PyTorch库——填补了这两个空白，其主要的API为代价元组：所有12种模型由四个可组合的代价函数指定，训练循环、log-Jacobian和反向ODE采样器自动共享。此外，我们提出了DI-Flow，一种新颖的代价设计，使用可微熵函数来鼓励模式覆盖，并提供了基于学习的MFG求解器，其在随机动力学行上显著优于神经训练。在2维基准上的实验证实，该统一API相对于手工实现是无损的。

</details>


### [231] [Multi-Agent Privacy Game in Federated Learning: A Unified Mean-Field View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23029)
*Kun Zhao,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将隐私保护联邦学习建模为平均场隐私博弈，通过平均场统计量实现大规模客户端场景下可处理的纳什均衡，并为每个客户端提供个性化隐私保障，同时保持指数衰减的隐私保证。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有隐私保护联邦学习方法要么通过注入校准噪声限制组合保证，要么将隐私选择建模为多智能体博弈但纳什均衡随客户端数量增加而变得难以处理。需要一种能兼顾大规模客户端、异构偏好和可处理均衡的方法。

Method: 将隐私保护联邦学习建模为平均场隐私博弈：每个客户端策略性地选择自己的隐私预算，仅通过一个平均场统计量与整体种群交互。平均场极限使得任意数量客户端下均衡可解，并通过对数索博列夫收缩继承指数衰减的隐私保证。该方法恢复熵隐私基线（同质特例）和多智能体隐私博弈（有限种群特例）。

Result: 在二次回归、逻辑回归和MNIST上的实验表明，所提框架能达到与熵基线相当的隐私-效用权衡，同时提供同质基线无法表达的个性化隐私保障。

Conclusion: 平均场隐私博弈框架成功桥接了噪声注入与博弈论两种隐私保护路线，在大规模联邦学习中实现了可处理的个性化隐私保护，同时保持强隐私保证和良好效用。

Original Abstract: Federated learning enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without centralising their data, yet privacy remains a persistent concern because the shared model updates can leak information about local datasets. Existing privacy-preserving methods either inject calibrated noise into client updates, limiting their composition guarantees, or formulate client privacy choices as a multi-agent game whose Nash equilibrium becomes intractable as the number of clients grows. We bridge these two lines of work by formulating privacy-preserving federated learning as a mean-field privacy game: each client strategically chooses its own privacy budget while interacting with the population only through a single mean-field statistic. The mean-field limit yields a tractable equilibrium for arbitrarily many clients, accommodates heterogeneous client preferences, and inherits an exponentially decaying privacy guarantee through a log-Sobolev contraction. The framework recovers the entropic privacy baseline as the homogeneous special case and the multi-agent privacy game as the finite-population case. Experiments on quadratic regression, logistic regression, and MNIST demonstrate that the proposed framework attains the privacy-utility trade-off of the entropic baseline while delivering a personalized privacy guarantee that the homogeneous baseline cannot express.

中文摘要翻译: 联邦学习能够在分布式客户端之间进行协作模型训练而无需集中其数据，但隐私仍然是一个持久性问题，因为共享的模型更新可能泄露本地数据集的信息。现有的隐私保护方法要么向客户端更新注入校准噪声（限制了其组合保证），要么将客户端隐私选择建模为多智能体博弈（其纳什均衡随客户端数量增加而变得难以处理）。我们通过将隐私保护联邦学习建模为平均场隐私博弈来桥接这两条研究路线：每个客户端策略性地选择其自身的隐私预算，同时仅通过一个平均场统计量与整体种群交互。平均场极限为任意数量的客户端提供了可处理的均衡，适应异构的客户端偏好，并通过对数索博列夫收缩继承指数衰减的隐私保证。该框架将熵隐私基线恢复为同质特例，将多智能体隐私博弈恢复为有限种群特例。在二次回归、逻辑回归和MNIST上的实验表明，所提框架能够达到熵基线的隐私-效用权衡，同时提供同质基线无法表达的个性化隐私保证。

</details>


### [232] [Online Policy Evaluation for MDPs with Dynamic UBSR Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23030)
*Weikai Wang,Erick Delage*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对风险感知强化学习中的策略评估问题，提出了在动态效用型短缺风险（UBSR）度量下、线性函数近似上的在线学习算法UBSR-TD，证明了其几乎必然收敛性，并设计了多种加速变体。数值实验和易腐库存管理应用验证了方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有风险感知策略评估方法要么限制于特定风险度量类别，要么依赖模拟器访问，难以在完全在线场景中应用。本文旨在提出一种适用于动态UBSR度量的、计算高效的在线策略评估方法。

Method: 提出UBSR-TD算法，通过在时序差分（TD）误差中引入损失函数，将风险中性MDP的策略评估方法扩展到动态UBSR设置中。建立了算法几乎必然收敛的条件，并开发了多种加速收敛的变体。

Result: 数值实验支持了理论收敛性结果；在具有货架期不确定性的易腐库存管理问题中的应用证明了方法的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 本文表明，通过将损失函数纳入时序差分误差，现有风险中性MDP的策略评估算法可以便捷地适配到动态UBSR场景，兼具计算效率与理论保证。

Original Abstract: Developing efficient function-approximation methods for policy evaluation is a fundamental challenge in risk-aware reinforcement learning. Existing approaches either focus on restrictive classes of risk measures or rely on access to a simulator, limiting their applicability in fully online settings. In this work, we propose computationally efficient online learning algorithms for policy evaluation in Markov decision processes (MDPs) with dynamic utility-based shortfall risk (UBSR) measures under linear function approximation. Specifically, we introduce the UBSR-TD algorithm, establish conditions under which it converges almost surely, and develop several variants designed to accelerate convergence. Our formulation shows that existing policy evaluation algorithms for risk-neutral MDPs can be readily adapted to dynamic UBSR settings by incorporating a loss function into the temporal-difference error. Numerical experiments support our theoretical findings, and an application to a perishable inventory management problem with shelf-life uncertainty demonstrates the practical effectiveness of the proposed methods.

中文摘要翻译: 为风险感知强化学习中的策略评估开发高效的函数逼近方法是一项基本挑战。现有方法要么局限于限制性较强的风险度量类别，要么依赖于对模拟器的访问，限制了它们在完全在线环境中的应用。本文针对具有线性函数近似的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP）中的动态效用型短缺风险（UBSR）度量，提出了计算高效的在线学习算法用于策略评估。具体而言，我们引入了UBSR-TD算法，建立了其几乎必然收敛的条件，并开发了多种旨在加速收敛的变体。我们的公式表明，通过将损失函数纳入时序差分误差中，现有的风险中性MDP策略评估算法可以便捷地适配到动态UBSR设置。数值实验支持了我们的理论发现，并将该方法应用于一个具有货架期不确定性的易腐库存管理问题，证明了所提方法的实际有效性。

</details>


### [233] [MixQuant: Adaptive Mixed-Precision Quantization for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23047)
*Ashitabh Misra,Madhav Agrawal,Arham Jain,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MixQuant提出了一种与具体技术无关的自适应量化框架，通过边际化层间量化依赖关系、在分配器自身生成的计划上校准参数以及惩罚最低比特分配，实现了单次离线校准即可服务于任意内存预算的混合精度量化，在多个大模型和量化方案上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有混合精度量化方法针对单一固定内存预算求解最优比特分配，但实际部署中预算会变化且在校准时未知。尽管自适应量化可通过一次离线校准服务任意预算，但现有方法在评估层敏感性时未考虑该层敏感性与其他层量化水平的依赖关系，导致次优分配。

Method: MixQuant包含三个核心组件：(1) 通过边际化每层在随机量化上游配置上的失真，得到与预算无关的敏感性评分；(2) 在分配器自身产生的计划上校准量化器参数；(3) 惩罚将层置于最低比特宽度的分配。部署时只需一次贪心遍历即可为任意预算生成分配方案。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-2-7B和Mistral-7B模型上，结合AWQ和GPTQ量化方案，MixQuant在所有设置下均优于自适应和混合精度基线；在最紧预算下平均准确率提升高达8个点，困惑度从12.43降至10.70，且匹配ILP求解器性能的同时部署成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MixQuant有效捕获了层间量化依赖关系，通过技术无关的自适应框架实现了任意预算下的高效混合精度量化，在精度和部署灵活性上均显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Mixed-precision quantization improves the accuracy of post-training quantization by allocating higher bitwidths to sensitive layers, but existing methods solve the allocation for a single fixed memory budget. In practice the budget varies across deployments and is unknown at calibration time. Adaptive quantization addresses this with one offline calibration that serves any budget, yet current methods score layer sensitivity in a manner that does not consider its dependency on quantization levels of other layers. We show that a layer's sensitivity depends strongly on the bitwidths of its upstream layers and that this dependence shifts the resulting preferred bit allocation. We propose MixQuant, a technique-agnostic adaptive framework that wraps any base quantizer. MixQuant marginalizes each layer's distortion over random quantized upstream configurations to obtain budget-agnostic scores, calibrates the quantizer's parameters on plans the allocator itself produces, and penalizes allocations that leave layers at the lowest bitwidths. A single greedy pass then serves any budget at deployment. Across Llama-3.2-3B, Llama-2-7B, and Mistral-7B under AWQ and GPTQ, MixQuant outperforms adaptive and mixed-precision baselines in every setting, improving average accuracy by up to 8 points and reducing perplexity from 12.43 to 10.70 at the tightest budget, while matching an ILP solver at negligible deployment cost.

中文摘要翻译: 混合精度量化通过为敏感层分配更高的比特宽度来提升训练后量化的精度，但现有方法仅针对单一固定内存预算求解分配问题。实际部署中预算会随场景变化，且在校准时未知。自适应量化通过一次离线校准即可服务任意预算来解决这一问题，但当前方法在评估层敏感性时未考虑该层对其他层量化水平的依赖关系。我们证明，层的敏感性强烈依赖于其上游层的比特宽度，且这种依赖关系会改变最优比特分配的偏好。本文提出MixQuant，一个与具体技术无关的自适应框架，可封装任意基础量化器。MixQuant通过将每层的失真在随机的量化上游配置上进行边际化，得到与预算无关的评分；在分配器自身产生的计划上校准量化器参数；并对将层置于最低比特宽度的分配施加惩罚。部署时，单次贪心遍历即可服务任意预算。在Llama-3.2-3B、Llama-2-7B和Mistral-7B上，结合AWQ和GPTQ，MixQuant在所有设置下均优于自适应和混合精度基线，在最紧预算下平均准确率提升高达8个点，困惑度从12.43降至10.70，同时以可忽略不计的部署成本匹配了ILP求解器的性能。

</details>


### [234] [The Entropic Bound for Transformers: Why Static Rank Fails and Attention-Native Rank Recovers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23050)
*Byeong Hoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer解决固定任务所需的最小模型容量，提出了基于熵界的任务内在容量度量，证明了线性注意力下秩的紧下界，并将其推广到真实注意力机制，建立了注意力原生内在秩的概念，恢复了完整的熵界结构。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 神经缩放定律回答了损失如何随模型、数据和计算增长而下降，但未解决一个更根本的问题：对于固定任务，解决它所需的最小模型容量是多少？本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文首先在线性注意力代理中证明token混合算子的内在秩$r^*$是紧下界；通过梯度下降隐式偏差恢复该秩；引入注意力原生内在秩（最小查询-键核秩）；使用能量有效秩作为估计器；通过受控插值实验定位线性注意力向真实注意力迁移失败的根源。

Result: 线性注意力中秩亏缺模型必然承担不可避免的额外风险，且$r^*$处可达最优；梯度下降可在标准低秩隐式偏差假设下恢复该秩；$r^*$可在训练前从数据中恢复。真实注意力中静态权重核无法概括输入条件化的混合算子，但引入注意力原生内在秩后，线性和softmax注意力的完整熵界结构（缺陷性、可达性、可恢复性）均得以恢复。线性QK注意力的$r^*$可在训练前精确恢复，softmax注意力仅能部分恢复。

Conclusion: 本文将熵界从事后描述器重新定义为注意力原生的容量度量，并精确刻画了其可预测性边界。

Original Abstract: Neural scaling laws describe how loss decreases as models, data, and compute grow, but they do not answer a prior question: for a fixed task, what is the minimum model capacity required to solve it? We study this through the Entropic Bound, a spectral notion of task-intrinsic capacity for Transformers. We first prove that, in a linear attention surrogate, the intrinsic rank $r^*$ of the token-mixing operator is a tight lower bound: any rank-deficient model incurs unavoidable excess risk, and the bound is achievable at $r^*$. We further show that gradient descent recovers this rank under standard low-rank implicit-bias assumptions, confirm all three properties empirically, and show $r^*$ is recoverable from data before training. We then ask whether this transfers to real attention. A naive transfer fails, and a controlled interpolation ladder localizes the cause precisely: it is not softmax and not a rank constraint, but the input-conditioned nature of attention's mixing operator, which a static weight kernel cannot summarize. Motivated by this, we introduce an attention-native intrinsic rank -- the minimum query-key kernel rank realizing the task within the attention class -- and show that under this definition the full Entropic Bound structure (deficiency, achievability, recovery) is restored for both linear and softmax attention, with the energy effective rank as the estimator robust to softmax distortion. Finally, we map the boundary of data-only predictability: $r^*$ is exactly recoverable for linear QK attention, even without the value map at scale, while softmax attention admits only partial pre-training recovery due to nonlinear inversion and kernel-value identifiability effects. Our results reframe the Entropic Bound from a post-hoc descriptor into an attention-native capacity measure with a precisely characterized predictability frontier.

中文摘要翻译: 神经缩放定律描述了损失如何随模型、数据和计算规模的增大而下降，但并未回答一个前置问题：对于固定任务，解决它所需的最小模型容量是多少？我们通过熵界（Entropic Bound）——一种Transformer任务内在容量的谱概念——来研究这一问题。我们首先证明，在线性注意力代理中，token混合算子的内在秩$r^*$是一个紧下界：任何秩亏缺模型都会承担不可避免的额外风险，且该界在$r^*$处可达。我们进一步证明，在标准低秩隐式偏差假设下，梯度下降能够恢复该秩，并通过实验验证了所有三个性质，同时表明$r^*$可在训练前从数据中恢复。随后，我们探究该结论是否能迁移到真实注意力机制。直接迁移失败，而一个受控的插值实验精确定位了原因：问题不在于softmax，也不在于秩约束，而在于注意力混合算子的输入条件化性质——这是静态权重核无法概括的。受此启发，我们引入了注意力原生的内在秩——在注意力类别内实现任务所需的最小查询-键核秩——并证明在该定义下，线性和softmax注意力均恢复了完整的熵界结构（缺陷性、可达性、可恢复性），其中能量有效秩是对softmax畸变具有鲁棒性的估计器。最后，我们刻画了纯数据可预测性的边界：对于线性QK注意力，$r^*$可在训练前精确恢复，即使没有尺度上的值映射；而由于非线性反演和核-值可辨识性效应，softmax注意力仅能实现部分训练前恢复。我们的结果将熵界从事后描述器重新定义为一种注意力原生的容量度量，并具有精确刻画的可预测性边界。

</details>


### [235] [Through the Bottleneck: How Multi-head Latent Attention Separates Content from Position in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23054)
*Dhruvil S,Fenil Sojitra,Ravirajsinh Chauhan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对DeepSeek-V2提出的多头潜注意力（MLA）进行了全面的可解释性研究，发现其cKV瓶颈主要保留内容信息（实体身份保持率达98%）而丢弃位置信息；归纳头集中在单层（第12层）；一个“语义枢纽”层（第15层）同时具有最高SVD有效秩和最强破坏-归因分数；且瓶颈全局过度配置，平均仅使用46%容量。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MLA已被大规模生产模型广泛采用，但尚无先前工作研究其bottleneck保留或丢弃了哪些信息，以及它如何重塑Transformer内部电路结构。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 训练一个1.14亿参数的Transformer（在网页/代码/数学混合数据上预训练，在TinyStories上微调），通过SVD分解、注意力头分类学分析、线性探针以及破坏-归因分析等方法对MLA的表示进行机制性可解释性研究。

Result: （1）cKV瓶颈学习到纯内容表示，保留实体身份（98%保留率）而丢弃位置信息，验证了MLA通过RoPE分离内容与位置的设计；（2）归纳头集中出现在单层（第12层），而非标准MHA中的分布式形成；（3）单个“语义枢纽”层（第15层）同时具有最高SVD有效秩和最强破坏-归因分数；（4）瓶颈全局过度配置，平均仅使用46%容量。

Conclusion: MLA不仅是被动压缩注意力，还重塑了模型组织内容、位置和电路结构的方式。本文是初步研究，局限性在第5节中详述。

Original Abstract: Multi-head Latent Attention (MLA), introduced in DeepSeek-V2, compresses key-value pairs through a shared low-rank bottleneck (cKV), achieving 81% KV-cache reduction during inference. Despite its adoption in massive production models, no prior work has studied what information this bottleneck preserves or discards, nor how it reshapes internal transformer circuits. We present the first comprehensive mechanistic interpretability study of MLA, training a 114M-parameter transformer (pretrained on a web/code/math mixture, fine-tuned on TinyStories) and analyzing its representations through SVD, attention head taxonomy, linear probing, and a disruption-attribution analysis. Our key findings are: (1) the cKV bottleneck learns a pure content representation, preserving entity identity (98% retention) while discarding positional information, validating MLA's separation of content from position via RoPE; (2) induction heads co-locate at a single layer (Layer 12), unlike their distributed formation in standard MHA; (3) a single "semantic hub" layer (Layer 15) simultaneously exhibits the highest SVD effective rank and strongest disruption-attribution score; and (4) the bottleneck is globally over-provisioned, using only 46% of its capacity on average. These findings suggest MLA does not merely compress attention passively, but reshapes how the model organizes content, position, and circuit structure. We view this as an initial data point and detail scope limitations in Section 5.

中文摘要翻译: DeepSeek-V2中引入的多头潜注意力（MLA）通过共享低秩瓶颈（cKV）压缩键值对，在推理时实现了81%的KV缓存减少。尽管已被大规模生产模型采用，但尚无先前工作研究该瓶颈保留或丢弃了哪些信息，以及它如何重塑Transformer内部电路结构。我们首次对MLA进行了全面的机制性可解释性研究，训练了一个1.14亿参数的Transformer（在网页/代码/数学混合数据上预训练，在TinyStories上微调），并通过SVD分解、注意力头分类学分析、线性探针以及破坏-归因分析来研究其表示。我们的关键发现是：（1）cKV瓶颈学习到纯内容表示，保留实体身份（98%保留率）而丢弃位置信息，验证了MLA通过RoPE分离内容与位置的设计；（2）归纳头集中出现在单层（第12层），而非标准多头注意力中分布式形成的形式；（3）一个单独的“语义枢纽”层（第15层）同时具有最高SVD有效秩和最强破坏-归因分数；（4）该瓶颈全局过度配置，平均仅使用46%的容量。这些发现表明，MLA不仅是被动压缩注意力，还重塑了模型组织内容、位置和电路结构的方式。我们将此视为初步数据点，并在第5节中详述了范围局限性。

</details>


### [236] [Self-Boosting Vision-Language Models with Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23125)
*Shuai Wang,Daoan Zhang,Zhe Tang,Hao Cheng,Jiaheng Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出NOPD（噪声学生在线自蒸馏），一种无需外部模型或标注数据即可提升视觉语言模型性能的自蒸馏方法，通过干净与噪声输入间的预测差异提供自监督信号，在多个视觉推理任务上匹配甚至超越强化学习与模型蒸馏方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的VLM后训练方法依赖人工标注数据、外部模型蒸馏、人类反馈强化学习或可验证答案等外部监督，限制了模型在无外部监督下自我提升的能力。因此，作者希望设计一种无需任何外部模型或真实答案的自蒸馏方法。

Method: NOPD方法的核心是让模型从被噪声扰动（corrupted）的输入中学习，同时使用模型自身在干净（clean）输入下的预测结果作为token级别的监督信号。利用干净与噪声输入之间的预测差异自然产生自监督信号，实现无需外部监督的在线自蒸馏。

Result: 在5个视觉推理任务上有效；在Geometry3K的2.1K样本上训练，使Qwen2.5-VL-7B在验证集上提升20个点；在分布外测试集MathVista上获得7.4个点的提升；在3个模型、12个基准上均取得一致改进。

Conclusion: NOPD是一种简单而有效的通用自蒸馏方法，能够在无需任何外部模型或真实答案的情况下显著提升视觉语言模型的性能，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Post-training enables vision-language models (VLMs) to understand human instructions and perform various downstream tasks. Current post-training methods usually rely on human-annotated data, distillation from external models, reinforcement learning with human feedback, or verifiable answers. This limits their ability to improve without external supervision. To tackle this, we propose NOPD (Noisy Student On-Policy Self-Distillation), a simple yet effective self-distillation approach that improves VLMs without any external models or ground-truth answers. Our key insight is that prediction discrepancies between clean and corrupted inputs naturally induce a self-supervision signal. In NOPD, the model learns from corrupted inputs while using its own predictions under clean inputs as token-level supervision. We show the effectiveness of NOPD on five visual reasoning tasks; it can match and even outperform reinforcement learning approaches or distillation from external models. Notably, when trained with 2.1K samples from Geometry3K, NOPD improves Qwen2.5-VL-7B by 20 points on its validation set. It also shows generalization on out-of-distribution test sets and achieves 7.4 point gains on MathVista. Furthermore, we demonstrate that NOPD is a general approach to enhance VLMs, achieving improvements across three models on 12 benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 后训练使视觉语言模型（VLM）能够理解人类指令并执行各种下游任务。当前的后训练方法通常依赖人工标注数据、外部模型蒸馏、人类反馈强化学习或可验证答案。这限制了它们在无需外部监督下进行自我提升的能力。为解决这一问题，我们提出NOPD（噪声学生在线自蒸馏），一种简单而有效的自蒸馏方法，无需任何外部模型或真实答案即可提升VLM性能。我们的关键洞察是：干净输入与噪声输入之间的预测差异自然产生自监督信号。在NOPD中，模型从噪声输入中学习，同时使用自身在干净输入下的预测作为token级别的监督。我们在五个视觉推理任务上展示了NOPD的有效性；它可以匹配甚至超越强化学习方法或外部模型蒸馏。值得注意的是，在Geometry3K的2.1K样本上训练时，NOPD使Qwen2.5-VL-7B在其验证集上提升20个点。它还在分布外测试集上展现出泛化能力，在MathVista上取得7.4个点的提升。此外，我们证明了NOPD是一种通用的VLM增强方法，在3个模型、12个基准上均实现性能提升。

</details>


### [237] [Diffusion-Guided Search via Exponential Tilting (DiffTilt): An Application to Falsification of Safety-Critical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23134)
*Tanmay Khandait,Preetom Biswas,Hideki Okamoto,Bardh Hoxha,Georgios Fainekos,Giulia Pedrielli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DiffTilt，一种基于扩散模型指数倾斜的分布框架，用于在自动驾驶和网络-物理系统中发现罕见的失效场景。通过将扩散模型的引导采样解释为联合空间中的重要性采样，实现概率质量向失效相关行为的KL最优重分配，从而克服条件采样面临的乘性稀有性效应。在ARCH-COMP基准和提出的拖挂卡车基准上，DiffTilt相比现有方法取得更好或相当的失效发现性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有失效检测方法依赖条件采样策略，对环境与系统执行轨迹的联合分布进行分解，导致乘性稀有性效应——失效触发输入和失效触发轨迹同时稀缺，使得穷举搜索成本过高。

Method: 提出DiffTilt框架，通过指数倾斜扩散模型诱导的联合分布，将引导采样解释为联合空间中的重要性采样，利用引导分数实现概率质量向失效相关行为的KL最优重分配，并仅需学习评分函数来表征场景质量，避免昂贵系统仿真的大量使用。

Result: 在ARCH-COMP基准和新增的拖挂卡车基准上，DiffTilt相比现有最先进方法取得竞争性或更优的失效发现性能，在规范定义不限于STL公式时增益更大。

Conclusion: 理论证明倾斜操作能可靠放大失效概率且严格优于受限于乘性稀有性的条件采样方法；实验验证了DiffTilt在多个基准上的有效性。

Original Abstract: Discovering rare safety-critical failures in autonomous and cyber-physical systems is a fundamental challenge in verification and validation. Existing falsification approaches rely on conditional sampling strategies that factor the joint distribution over environments and system executions, and therefore suffer from multiplicative rarity effects: the simultaneous scarcity of failure-inducing inputs and failure-inducing traces makes exhaustive search prohibitively expensive.
  This paper develops DiffTilt, a distributional framework that exponentially tilts a diffusion model-induced joint distribution over environments and executions. We show that diffusion-guided sampling admits an exact interpretation as importance sampling in the joint space, where guidance scores induce a KL-optimal reallocation of probability mass towards failure-relevant behaviors. We further show that tilting provably amplifies failure probability and strictly outperforms conditional sampling, which is limited by multiplicative rarity. In this framework, the joint generative model serves as a reusable prior over scenarios and need not faithfully represent the system under test. Expensive system simulations are instead limited to learning a scoring function that characterizes scenario quality, enabling their selective and adaptive use.
  We study DiffTilt on ARCH-COMP benchmarks, and we propose an additional tractor-trailer benchmark showing the behavior of several approaches when scenario generation is guided by a well-defined specification rather than a reward. The proposed method achieves competitive or improved falsification performance compared to state-of-the-art approaches, with larger gains when specification definition is not limited to STL formulas.

中文摘要翻译: 在自动驾驶和网络-物理系统中，发现罕见的安全关键失效是验证与确认中的基本挑战。现有的伪造方法依赖对环境和系统执行轨迹的联合分布进行分解的条件采样策略，因此受到乘性稀有性效应的影响：失效诱导输入和失效诱导轨迹的同时稀缺使得穷举搜索成本过高。本文提出了DiffTilt，一种通过指数倾斜扩散模型诱导的环境与执行轨迹联合分布的分布框架。我们证明了扩散引导采样可被精确解释为联合空间中的重要性采样，其中引导分数实现了概率质量向失效相关行为的KL最优重分配。我们进一步证明，倾斜可可靠地放大失效概率，并且严格优于受限于乘性稀有性的条件采样。在此框架中，联合生成模型作为可重用的场景先验，无需忠实表示被测系统。昂贵的系统仿真被限制于学习表征场景质量的评分函数，从而支持其选择性和自适应性使用。我们在ARCH-COMP基准上研究了DiffTilt，并提出了一个额外的拖挂卡车基准，展示了几种方法在由明确规范而非奖励引导场景生成时的行为。所提方法相比现有最先进方法取得了竞争性或更优的伪造性能，在规范定义不限于STL公式时增益更大。

</details>


### [238] [Foundation Models and Fine-Tuning: Toward a New Generation of Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23146)
*Morad Laglil,Bertrand Pracca,Emilie Devijver,Eric Gaussier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 综述了时间序列基础模型（参数规模从数千万到数亿）的架构、预训练策略和优化方法，并通过实验证明对选定模型进行微调能持续提升零样本预测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 受到大语言模型在自然语言处理中突破性进展的启发，探索基础模型作为零样本时间序列预测的通用范式，以消除对数据集特定模型设计和人工调参的需求。

Method: 综述了时间序列基础模型的主流架构、预训练策略和优化方法，并对选定的基础模型进行后预训练微调，以增强其在特定数据集上的预测性能。

Result: 实验结果表明，后预训练微调步骤能在零样本基线之上持续提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 基于基础模型的时间序列预测范式能够提供统一的解决方案，而微调是进一步提升特定数据集预测性能的有效手段。

Original Abstract: Inspired by recent breakthroughs in large language models for natural language processing, foundation models have emerged as a promising paradigm for zero-shot time series forecasting, enabling accurate predictions on datasets never seen during pre-training. Ranging from tens to hundreds of millions of parameters, these models are pre-trained on vast and diverse collections of time series, learning generalizable representations that support both point and probabilistic forecasting. This approach alleviates the need for dataset-specific model design and manual tuning, offering a unified solution across forecasting problems. In this work, we review the main architectures, pre-training strategies, and optimization methods underpinning these models. We further investigate post-pre-training fine-tuning of selected foundation models to enhance their performance on specific datasets. Our empirical results demonstrate that this step consistently improves forecasting accuracy over the zero-shot baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 受大语言模型在自然语言处理中取得突破性进展的启发，基础模型已成为零样本时间序列预测的一种有前景的范式，能够对预训练阶段从未见过的数据集进行准确预测。这些模型的参数规模从数千万到数亿不等，在海量且多样的时间序列集合上进行预训练，学习到支持点预测和概率预测的通用表示。该方法缓解了数据集特定模型设计和手动调参的需求，为各类预测问题提供了统一解决方案。本文综述了支撑这些模型的主要架构、预训练策略和优化方法。我们进一步研究了选定基础模型的后预训练微调，以提升其在特定数据集上的性能。实验结果表明，该步骤相较于零样本基线持续改善了预测精度。

</details>


### [239] [XGRVFL-MV: Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View Random Vector Functional Link Network with FleXi Guardian Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23149)
*Yogesh Kumar,Mudasir Ganaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于残差耦合与图嵌入的多视图RVFL模型（XGRVFL-MV），通过引入FleXi Guardian损失函数和Nesterov加速梯度下降优化，在多视图分类任务中取得了优于基线方法的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于随机向量函数链接网络（RVFL）的多视图方法存在三个挑战：难以保持视图特定的几何结构、难以限制大预测残差的影响、难以建模多视图之间的关系。

Method: 提出XGRVFL-MV模型：1）为每个视图构建RVFL表示；2）引入基于局部Fisher判别分析加权的图嵌入，包含内在图和惩罚图；3）采用有界非对称的FleXi Guardian（XG）损失函数进行残差学习；4）引入残差耦合项，在保持视图特定表示的同时促使视图间预测残差的一致性；5）采用基于Nesterov加速梯度下降的无逆一阶优化过程求解。

Result: 在UCI、KEEL、AwA和Corel5k基准数据集上的实验结果显示，结合统计分析和超参数敏感性分析，XGRVFL-MV在分类性能上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的XGRVFL-MV模型通过残差耦合、图嵌入和XG损失函数的有机结合，有效解决了多视图RVFL方法中的关键挑战，在多视图分类任务上达到了有竞争力的性能。

Original Abstract: Random Vector Functional Link (RVFL) networks provide an efficient randomized learning framework for classification. Existing multi-view RVFL methods utilize complementary information from multiple views. However, preserving view-specific geometric structure, limiting the influence of large prediction residuals, and modeling relationships between multiple views remain challenging. This paper proposes a Residual-Coupled Graph-Embedded Multi-View RVFL model with fleXi guardian loss (XGRVFL-MV) for multi-view classification. The proposed model constructs RVFL representation for each view, incorporates graph embedding with intrinsic and penalty graphs constructed using the Local Fisher Discriminant Analysis weighting scheme. It also uses the bounded and asymmetric FleXi Guardian (XG) loss for residual learning. A residual-coupling term is introduced to encourage consistency among view-specific prediction residuals while preserving view-specific representations. The resulting optimization problem is solved using an inversion-free first-order optimization procedure based on Nesterov accelerated gradient descent. We evaluate the proposed model on UCI, KEEL, AwA, and Corel5k benchmark datasets. Experimental results, together with statistical analyses and hyperparameter sensitivity analyses, show that XGRVFL-MV achieves competitive classification performance compared with the baseline methods across the evaluated benchmark datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 随机向量函数链接网络为分类提供了一种高效的随机学习框架。现有的多视图RVFL方法利用来自多个视图的互补信息。然而，保持视图特定的几何结构、限制大预测残差的影响以及建模多视图之间的关系仍然具有挑战性。本文提出了一种带有FleXi Guardian损失的残差耦合图嵌入多视图RVFL模型（XGRVFL-MV），用于多视图分类。该模型为每个视图构建RVFL表示，并引入使用局部Fisher判别分析加权方案构建的包含内在图和惩罚图的图嵌入。它还采用有界且非对称的FleXi Guardian损失函数进行残差学习。引入残差耦合项，在保持视图特定表示的同时，促使视图特定预测残差之间的一致性。使用基于Nesterov加速梯度下降的无逆一阶优化过程求解所得优化问题。我们在UCI、KEEL、AwA和Corel5k基准数据集上评估了该模型。实验结果、统计分析和超参数敏感性分析表明，XGRVFL-MV在评估的基准数据集上取得了与基线方法相比有竞争力的分类性能。

</details>


### [240] [In-Context Learning as Implicit Policy Gradient](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23153)
*Masahiro Kaneko,Timothy Baldwin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上证明了基于分数的上下文学习（score-conditioned ICL）与策略梯度优化（REINFORCE算法）之间存在结构对应关系，并推导了注意力更新引起的分布偏移的上界，建立了类似信任区域策略优化的理论框架，实验验证了LLM能有效利用分数信息调整输出分布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管已有经验证据表明LLM可以通过将生成的样本及其评估分数作为上下文示例来迭代改进输出，但这一现象背后的理论基础尚不清楚。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 提供构造性证明，表明自注意力机制可以在特定权重矩阵配置下实现类似REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合；2) 推导在简化隐状态模型中，有界注意力更新引起的分布偏移的精确上界，建立类似KL约束策略优化的信任区域类比；3) 通过多个LLM上的实验验证理论。

Result: 1) 证明了分数条件ICL与策略梯度优化之间的结构对应关系；2) 推导了分布偏移的上界；3) 实验表明LLM能有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高分示例偏移，且注意力权重与示例分数之间存在强相关。

Conclusion: 分数条件化的上下文学习在理论上类似于策略梯度优化，LLM能够通过注意力机制利用分数信息来调整输出分布，这一过程可被理解为一种隐式的信任区域策略优化。

Original Abstract: Recent work has shown that large language models (LLMs) can iteratively improve their outputs by incorporating generated samples and their corresponding evaluation scores as in-context examples. Despite these empirical findings, the theoretical foundations underlying this phenomenon remain poorly understood. In this paper, we show that score-conditioned In-Context Learning (ICL) admits a structural correspondence to policy gradient optimization. We first provide a constructive proof that self-attention mechanisms can implement reward-weighted aggregation analogous to the REINFORCE algorithm under specific weight matrix configurations, and discuss the relationship between this construction and the behavior of pretrained transformers. The correspondence is directional in hidden-state space and holds exactly only under the stated simplifying conditions; we quantify its strength empirically. Within our simplified hidden-state model, we furthermore derive an exact upper bound on the distribution shift induced by a bounded attention update, yielding a trust-region-like analogy to KL-constrained policy optimization. We validate our theory through extensive experiments across multiple LLMs, demonstrating that LLMs effectively utilize score information to shift output distributions toward high-scoring exemplars, and that attention weights exhibit a strong correlation with example scores.

中文摘要翻译: 近期研究表明，大语言模型（LLMs）能够通过将生成的样本及其对应评估分数作为上下文示例来迭代改进其输出。尽管已有这些经验发现，但这一现象背后的理论基础仍鲜为人知。本文证明，基于分数的上下文学习（ICL）与策略梯度优化之间存在结构对应关系。我们首先提供一个构造性证明，表明自注意力机制在特定权重矩阵配置下可以实现类似REINFORCE算法的奖励加权聚合，并讨论该构造与预训练变换器行为之间的关系。该对应关系在隐状态空间中是方向性的，且仅在所述简化条件下严格成立；我们通过实验量化了其强度。在我们的简化隐状态模型中，我们还推导了由有界注意力更新引起的分布偏移的精确上界，从而建立了与KL约束策略优化类似的信任区域类比。我们通过在多个LLM上进行的大量实验验证了我们的理论，实验结果表明LLM能够有效利用分数信息将输出分布向高分示例偏移，并且注意力权重与示例分数之间存在强相关性。

</details>


### [241] [Wrong Design Intent Is Worse Than None: A Derangement-Control Diagnosis of Header Conditioning in CAD Program Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23191)
*Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过独立于条件本身的几何度量（基于B-rep固体的可执行断言）和因果控制实验，系统验证了微调后的代码LLM在参数化CAD生成中是否真正读取设计意图头部（header）。实验发现：错误头部会显著降低模型对可生成意图的遵循度；这种损害来自学习到的header→程序映射，而非边缘分布偏移；独立度量揭示了指标循环性；错误意图并非噪声，而是主动误导生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有研究将设计意图头部（header）附加到代码上微调LLM以引导CAD生成，但模型是否真的读取了头部内容尚未得到独立于条件本身的度量和因果控制的验证，存在指标循环性问题。

Method: 在Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B上对CadQuery风格的草图-拉伸程序进行LoRA微调，在程序前添加包含五个特征的设计意图头部（CADCON）；使用与头部定义的正则表达式提取器无关的、基于生成B-rep实体的可执行几何断言作为独立度量指标；采用预注册的{0%, 40%}-前缀×{正确, 错误, 掩码}-头部的全因子实验设计，并进行洗牌控制实验（derangement control）。

Result: (i) 在40%前缀条件下，语义错误的头部将模型对可生成意图（多边形和薄壁几何）的遵循度从0.43降至0.30/0.21（文本/令牌）；圆形和高意图在该检查点处于生成基线底部。(ii) 洗牌控制实验显示，标准模型对错误头部敏感（3/3种子显著，p≤4.2×10⁻³），而洗牌模型虽仍能生成但免疫于错误头部，证明损害来自学到的header→程序映射。(iii) 独立度量将正确头部的表观收益从+0.21（正则）降至+0.02（几何），量化了指标循环性。(iv) 在0%前缀下无条件的基线完全无法生成有效CAD。

Conclusion: 错误意图不是随机噪声，而是主动误导生成；头部内容确实被模型读取并影响生成行为，但效果具有意图类型依赖性和条件依赖性；独立于条件本身的几何度量是评估此类方法的关键。

Original Abstract: Fine-tuned code LLMs can be conditioned on a lightweight design-intent header to steer parametric CAD generation, but whether the model actually reads the header's content has not been tested under a metric independent of the conditioning itself, nor with a causal control. We study CADCON, a five-feature design-intent header prepended to CadQuery-style sketch-extrude programs during LoRA fine-tuning of Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B, re-scored by executable geometric assertions on the produced B-rep solid, sharing no code with the header-defining regex extractor. Across three seeds and a pre-registered {0%, 40%}-prefix $\times$ {correct, wrong, masked}-header matrix we find: (i) in conditional completion (40% prefix), a semantically wrong header degrades adherence below the no-header baseline (0.43 $\to$ 0.30/0.21 text/token) on design intents the model can render unconditioned -- polygonal and thin geometries; circle and tall intents sit at a baseline generation floor for this checkpoint ($\approx$0 in both compared arms) and are uninformative for this contrast; (ii) a derangement control -- retrained with shuffled ground-truth headers, identical header marginal but destroyed content correlation -- remains competent yet is immune to wrong headers, while the standard model is not (text headers; interaction significant on 3/3 seeds, p $\leq$ $4.2\times10^{-3}$): the harm requires the learned header$\to$program mapping, excluding the marginal/mechanical distribution-shift confound; (iii) the independent metric deflates the apparent benefit of a correct header (token: +0.21 regex $\to$ +0.02 geometry), quantifying metric circularity; (iv) the harm is regime-specific -- at 0% prefix the unconditioned baseline cannot generate valid CAD at all. Wrong intent is not noise: it actively misdirects generation.

中文摘要翻译: 微调后的代码大语言模型可以通过轻量级设计意图头部进行条件化，从而引导参数化CAD生成，但模型是否真正读取了头部的内容，此前尚未通过独立于条件本身度量标准的指标以及因果控制进行检验。本文研究了CADCON——一个包含五个特征的设计意图头部，在Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B的LoRA微调过程中预置于CadQuery风格的草图-拉伸程序之前，并通过可执行的几何断言对生成的B-rep实体进行重新评分，这些断言与定义头部的正则表达式提取器无代码共享。跨三个随机种子和一个预注册的{0%, 40%}-前缀×{正确, 错误, 掩码}-头部矩阵，我们发现：(i) 在条件补全（40%前缀）中，语义错误的头部会降低模型对模型能够在无条件情况下生成的意图（多边形和薄壁几何）的遵循度（文本/令牌从0.43降至0.30/0.21）；圆形和高意图在该检查点的无条件生成基线处于底部（两个对比组中均≈0），对此对比无信息量；(ii) 通过使用打乱的真实头部重新训练的洗牌控制模型——保持了相同的头部边缘分布但破坏了内容相关性——仍具备生成能力但对错误头部免疫，而标准模型则不然（文本头部；3/3种子交互显著，p≤4.2×10⁻³）：损害需要学到的头部→程序映射，排除了边缘/机械分布偏移的混杂因素；(iii) 独立度量降低了正确头部的表观收益（令牌：从正则的+0.21降至几何的+0.02），量化了指标循环性；(iv) 损害具有机制特异性——在0%前缀下，无条件基线完全无法生成有效的CAD。错误意图并非噪声：它会主动误导生成。

</details>


### [242] [Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling for Anomaly Detection in Cyber-Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23197)
*Youngseok Hwang,Joonsung Kwon,Geonwoo Lee,Hyunwoo Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPR-GM，一种利用大语言模型从系统文档提取物理耦合先验知识来指导时序图结构构建的异常检测框架，在数据稀缺场景下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于图的多元传感器时序异常检测方法在小规模物理系统中面临标注异常稀少、正常数据有限的问题，易学到虚假相关并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。

Method: 利用大语言模型从系统文档中提取传感器间的有向物理耦合，编码为二值领域邻接矩阵作为结构门控；结合正常训练数据的皮尔逊相关系数进行调制；基于变异系数计算传感器级可靠性加权异常分数。所有组件在训练前固定，无可学习参数。

Result: 在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习等基线方法。

Conclusion: 在数据稀缺的信息物理系统中，引入领域结构化图先验是一种比完全学习拓扑更实用的替代方案。

Original Abstract: Anomaly detection on multivariate sensor time series is critical for industrial monitoring of cyber-physical systems (CPS), where even subtle deviations from normal behavior can indicate process disruption. Recent graph-based approaches have made significant progress, but they often struggle in small-scale physical systems with scarce labeled anomalies and limited normal data. In such settings, graph-based models tend to capture spurious correlations and produce unstable sensor topologies. We propose DPR-GM (Domain-Prior-Regularized Graph Modeling), a forecasting-based framework that incorporates system design knowledge into graph construction. DPR-GM leverages a large language model (LLM) to extract directed physical couplings between sensor pairs from system documentation, which are encoded as a binary domain adjacency matrix serving as a structural gate over sensor relations. This gate is then modulated by Pearson correlations estimated from normal training data. The anomaly score is further weighted by sensor-level reliability derived from the coefficient of variation. All graph and weighting components are fixed prior to training and add no learnable parameters. On the SKAB benchmark, DPR-GM outperforms graph-based, statistical, and deep learning baselines across F1, AUROC, and AUPRC, showing that domain-structured graph priors are a practical alternative to fully learned topologies in data-scarce CPS.

中文摘要翻译: 多元传感器时间序列上的异常检测对于信息物理系统（CPS）的工业监控至关重要，即使与正常行为的细微偏差也可能指示过程中断。近年来基于图的方法取得了显著进展，但这类方法在小规模物理系统中往往表现不佳，原因是此类系统中标注异常稀少且正常数据有限。在这种设定下，基于图的模型容易捕获虚假相关并产生不稳定的传感器拓扑结构。我们提出DPR-GM（领域先验正则化图建模），这是一种基于预测的框架，将系统设计知识融入图构建过程。DPR-GM利用大语言模型（LLM）从系统文档中提取传感器对之间的有向物理耦合，这些耦合被编码为二值领域邻接矩阵，充当传感器关系上的结构门控。该门控进一步由从正常训练数据估计的皮尔逊相关系数进行调制。异常分数额外通过从变异系数导出的传感器级可靠性进行加权。所有图和权重组件在训练前固定，且不增加可学习参数。在SKAB基准上，DPR-GM在F1、AUROC和AUPRC指标上均优于基于图、统计和深度学习的基线方法，表明在数据稀缺的信息物理系统中，领域结构化图先验是一种比完全学习拓扑更实用的替代方案。

</details>


### [243] [Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS): Greedy Feature Selection via Orthogonal Deflation in PCA Loading Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23198)
*Baran Koseoglu,Berrin Yanikoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VPOS是一个基于加权PCA载荷空间的无监督特征选择贪心框架，通过零空间去偏投影移除已选特征的方差方向，使后续选择覆盖协方差结构的正交部分。在8个基准上均取得最低重构MSE，速度比基于图的方法快10-140倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督特征选择方法在保持协方差结构完整性方面存在不足，且计算效率低（尤其是基于图的方法）。需要一种既能保留数据方差结构又能高效运行的特征选择方法。

Method: 提出Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS)框架：在加权PCA载荷空间中进行贪心特征选择，每次选择后通过零空间去偏（null-space deflation）投影掉已选特征的方差方向，迫使后续选择覆盖协方差结构的正交部分。每次选择可证明降低载荷矩阵秩1，贪心目标与单调子模最大化关联。超参数d通过最小化重构MSE的灵敏度扫描确定。

Result: 在8个基准数据集上，VPOS全部取得最低重构MSE，运行速度比基于图的方法快10-140倍。与无去偏的PCA对比（匹配d），去偏机制使MSE降低10-73%，证实了去偏是主要驱动因素。

Conclusion: VPOS通过正交选择与零空间去偏策略，在保证协方差结构完整性的同时实现了高效的无监督特征选择，显著优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: We propose Variance-Preserving Orthogonal Selection (VPOS), a greedy framework for unsupervised feature selection that operates in the weighted PCA loading space. After each selection, VPOS projects out the chosen feature's variance direction via null-space deflation, forcing subsequent selections to cover orthogonal parts of the covariance structure. Each step provably reduces the loading matrix rank by one, and the greedy objective connects to monotone submodular maximization. The single hyperparameter $d$ is selected via a reproducible rule: the value minimising reconstruction MSE in a sensitivity sweep. On eight benchmarks, VPOS achieves the lowest reconstruction MSE on all eight while running 10-140x faster than graph-based methods at scale. Comparing against PCA (no deflation) at matched $d$ confirms deflation as the primary driver, reducing MSE by 10-73%.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了方差保持正交选择（VPOS），一个在加权PCA载荷空间中运行的贪心无监督特征选择框架。每次选择后，VPOS通过零空间去偏投影移除已选特征的方差方向，迫使后续选择覆盖协方差结构的正交部分。每一步均可证明降低载荷矩阵的秩1，且贪心目标与单调子模最大化相关联。单一超参数d通过一个可复现的规则选择：在灵敏度扫描中最小化重构MSE的值。在八个基准数据集上，VPOS在所有八个数据集上均取得最低重构MSE，同时在规模上比基于图的方法快10-140倍。与相同d下不使用去偏的PCA对比，证实去偏是主要驱动因素，可将MSE降低10-73%。

</details>


### [244] [ParasGB: A Graph Benchmark Suite for Parasitic Estimation on AMS Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23225)
*Jiajun Zou,Jiawei Liu,Ao Liu,Junnong Tian,Yibin Zhang,Chengjie Liu,Yuxi Wang,Shan Shen,Wenhua Gu,Jun Yang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ParasGB是首个面向预布线阶段寄生效应的开源基准测试套件，提供大规模、异构RC网络数据集及统一评估协议，用于可复现的电路图学习与寄生感知建模研究。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随着芯片制造进入深亚微米节点，互连寄生效应日益主导模拟/混合信号电路性能并导致昂贵的版图迭代，而现有基于GNN的寄生建模研究缺乏公开、高保真的RC基准数据集来支持可复现评估。

Method: 构建ParasGB基准套件：从流片验证的设计中利用商用EDA工具提取大规模异构RC网络，包含节点级对地电容、边级电阻和边级耦合电容；设计统一的评估协议和标准化训练流程，对多种GNN架构进行基准测试。

Result: 揭示了该任务中存在的极端标签不平衡、长尾寄生分布和强结构异质性等挑战，为后续研究提供了标准化的评估平台。

Conclusion: ParasGB为早期寄生预测建立了物理可解释且标准化的基准，为电路图学习和寄生感知模型开发提供了开放的可复现研究平台。

Original Abstract: As chip manufacturing processes advance to deep submicron nodes, parasitic interconnect effects increasingly dominate the performance of analog and mixed-signal (AMS) circuits and often lead to costly layout iterations. This makes early-stage estimation of parasitic capacitance and resistance important for parasitic-aware design exploration before full physical implementation. However, progress on GNN-based parasitic modeling has been hindered by the lack of public, high-fidelity RC benchmarks that support reproducible evaluation. To address this gap, we introduce ParasGB, the first open-source benchmark suite for pre-layout parasitic parameter prediction on circuit graphs. ParasGB provides large-scale, heterogeneous RC networks extracted with commercial EDA tools from tape-out-proven designs, together with a unified evaluation protocol covering node-level ground capacitance, edge-level resistance, and edge-level coupling capacitance. Within this framework, we benchmark diverse GNN architectures using a standardized training pipeline and expose challenges such as extreme label imbalance, long-tailed parasitic distributions, and strong structural heterogeneity. By establishing a physically grounded and standardized benchmark for early-stage parasitic prediction, ParasGB provides an open platform for reproducible research on circuit graph learning and parasitic-aware model development. All datasets, preprocessing scripts, and configurations are publicly available in our code repository https://github.com/ShenShan123/ParasGB.git.

中文摘要翻译: 随着芯片制造工艺推进到深亚微米节点，互连寄生效应日益主导模拟与混合信号（AMS）电路的性能，并常常导致昂贵的版图迭代。这使得在全物理实现之前，对寄生电容和电阻进行早期阶段估计对于寄生感知的设计探索至关重要。然而，基于图神经网络（GNN）的寄生建模研究因缺乏公开、高保真且支持可复现评估的RC基准数据集而进展缓慢。为弥补这一空白，我们提出了ParasGB，这是首个面向预布线阶段寄生参数预测的开源基准测试套件。ParasGB提供从流片验证的设计中利用商用EDA工具提取的大规模、异构RC网络，并附有统一的评估协议，涵盖节点级对地电容、边级电阻和边级耦合电容。在该框架内，我们采用标准化的训练流程对多种GNN架构进行基准测试，揭示了极端标签不平衡、长尾寄生分布和强结构异质性等挑战。通过为早期寄生预测建立物理可解释且标准化的基准，ParasGB为电路图学习和寄生感知模型开发的可复现研究提供了一个开放平台。所有数据集、预处理脚本和配置均已公开在我们的代码仓库中：https://github.com/ShenShan123/ParasGB.git。

</details>


### [245] [From Score Learning to Discretized Sampling: An End-to-End Generalization Analysis of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23226)
*Jinshu Huang,Yiming Jiang,Chunlin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为基于分数的扩散模型建立了一个统一的理论分析框架，从有限样本学习、网络参数化和数值离散化三个维度刻画了生成质量的联合影响，并将总生成误差分解为四个可解释的组成部分。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有扩散模型的理论分析往往依赖于理想化的假设（如预言机分数或预设误差阈值），缺乏对有限样本学习、实际网络结构和离散化策略如何共同影响生成质量的完整理论理解。

Method: 提出一个统一的收敛性与泛化性分析框架，针对实用ResNet类架构参数化的分数扩散模型，从有限样本、离散时间的实际分数函数学习问题出发，分析其到理想连续时间、总体水平目标的泛化与收敛性质，并基于学习到的分数函数分析采样过程，给出端到端的全变差距离估计。

Result: 将生成总误差显式分解为四个可解释分量：前向过程的截断误差、反向时间离散化误差、结合有限数据和前向时间离散化的泛化误差，以及训练优化差距。定量刻画了训练样本量、时间离散化网格和优化精度对最终样本保真度的联合控制作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为扩散模型的生成质量提供了系统性的理论保障，揭示了样本量、离散化步长和优化误差等因素如何共同决定生成性能，为实际应用中的超参数选择提供了理论指导。

Original Abstract: Despite the empirical success of score-based diffusion models, a complete theoretical understanding of how finite-sample learning, network parameterization, and numerical discretization jointly dictate generative quality remains underdeveloped. Existing sampling analyses often evaluate the generative performance conditional on an oracle score or a pre-specified error threshold. In this work, we establish a unified convergence and generalization framework for score-based diffusion models parameterized by practical ResNet-type architectures. We analyze the generalization and convergence properties from the practical finite-sample, discrete-time learning problem of the score function to the ideal continuous-time, population-level objective. Based on the generalization result of the learning problem of score function, we analyze the sampling process induced by the learned score function and provide an end-to-end total variation distance estimate for the generated terminal distribution. This estimate explicitly decomposes the overall generative error into four interpretable components: the truncation error of the forward process, the reverse-time discretization error, the generalization error incorporating both finite data and forward-time discretization, and the training optimization gap. Our results quantitatively characterize how the training sample size, temporal discretization grids, and optimization accuracy jointly control the final fidelity of samples generated by diffusion models.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于分数的扩散模型在经验上取得了成功，但对于有限样本学习、网络参数化和数值离散化如何共同决定生成质量，目前仍缺乏完整的理论理解。现有的采样分析通常依赖于预言机分数或预设误差阈值来评估生成性能。在这项工作中，我们为实用ResNet类架构参数化的分数扩散模型建立了一个统一的收敛性与泛化性分析框架。我们从分数函数学习问题的实际有限样本、离散时间设置出发，分析其到理想连续时间、总体水平目标的泛化与收敛性质。基于分数函数学习问题的泛化结果，我们分析了由学习得到的分数函数所驱动的采样过程，并为生成的目标分布提供了端到端的全变差距离估计。该估计将生成总误差显式分解为四个可解释的部分：前向过程的截断误差、反向时间离散化误差、包含有限数据和前向时间离散化的泛化误差，以及训练优化差距。我们的结果定量刻画了训练样本量、时间离散化网格和优化精度如何共同控制扩散模型生成样本的最终保真度。

</details>


### [246] [Context-Aware Concept Distillation for Trustworthy Flood Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23237)
*Eli Levinkopf,Efrat Morin,Claudia V. Goldman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出上下文感知概念蒸馏（CACD）框架，将不透明的LSTM模型蒸馏为可解释的水文学代理模型，在5203个全球流域上实现高保真度（中位数NSE 0.70），平衡了AI精度与可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在洪水风险预测中虽精度高，但“黑箱”特性阻碍了高风险公共安全决策中的信任与问责；现有可解释AI方法仅提供局部归因，无法提供决策所需的可验证、有操作意义的因果叙事。

Method: 提出上下文感知概念蒸馏（CACD）框架：1）无监督流水线发现“水文语言”（可解释的水文概念）；2）残差超网络（Residual Hypernetwork）根据静态流域特征动态调制这些概念；3）将不透明LSTM蒸馏为可解释的水文学代理模型。

Result: 在5203个全球流域上评估，模型达到高保真度（中位数NSE 0.70），在未见过的未来数据上显著优于黑箱基线模型（如多层感知机）。

Conclusion: 证明了人类可解释的概念足以重构洪水动态，实现了AI精度与负责任环境决策所需透明度之间的平衡。

Original Abstract: Effective flood risk management relies on accurate forecasting, yet the "black box" nature of stateof-the-art Deep Learning models creates a barrier to trust and accountability in high-stakes public safety decisions. While existing Explainable AI (XAI) methods offer local attributions, they fail to provide the verifiable, operationally meaningful causal narratives required by disaster response authorities. To address this societal challenge, we propose Context-Aware Concept Distillation (CACD), a framework developed in collaboration with domain experts to distill opaque LSTMs into interpretable, hydrology-aware surrogate models. We introduce an unsupervised pipeline to discover a "Hydrological Language" and a Residual Hypernetwork that dynamically modulates these concepts based on static basin characteristics. Evaluated on 5,203 basins globally, our model achieves high fidelity (Median NSE 0.70), significantly outperforming black-box baselines (e.g., Multi Layer Perceptrons) on unseen future data. By demonstrating that human-interpretable concepts are sufficient to reconstruct flood dynamics, this work balances AI accuracy with the transparency required for responsible environmental decision-making.

中文摘要翻译: 有效的洪水风险管理依赖于准确的预测，然而最先进的深度学习模型的"黑箱"特性在高风险公共安全决策中造成了信任和问责障碍。虽然现有的可解释人工智能方法提供了局部归因，但它们无法提供灾害响应部门所需的可验证、具有操作意义的因果叙事。为应对这一社会挑战，我们提出上下文感知概念蒸馏（CACD）——一个与领域专家合作开发的框架，旨在将不透明的LSTM蒸馏为可解释的、具有水文学意识的代理模型。我们引入了一个无监督流水线来发现"水文语言"，以及一个残差超网络，该网络根据静态流域特征动态调制这些概念。在5203个全球流域上的评估表明，我们的模型实现了高保真度（中位数纳什效率系数0.70），在未见过的未来数据上显著优于黑箱基线模型（如多层感知机）。通过证明人类可解释的概念足以重构洪水动态，本工作平衡了AI精度与负责任环境决策所需的透明度。

</details>


### [247] [StageGuard: Physiologically Constrained Sleep Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23284)
*Juntang Wang,Yihan Wang,Hao Wu,Jiayu Gao,Shixin Xu,Dongmian Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StageGuard提出了一种即插即用的结构化推理框架，通过可微软过渡惩罚和半马尔可夫约束解码，在保持分类精度的同时，显著降低睡眠分期输出中的生理学违规（如罕见转换和过度碎片化），从而更准确地导出下游睡眠架构指标。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习睡眠分期模型虽能达到接近人工判读的逐帧准确率，但其输出的睡眠图谱常违反生理学不变性，如出现罕见转换（直接觉醒→REM）或过度碎片化序列，这些违规会偏倚下游睡眠指标（如总睡眠时间、REM潜伏期等），无论整体准确率如何。

Method: StageGuard是一个即插即用、骨干无关的结构化推理框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 可微软过渡惩罚（differentiable soft transition penalty），在训练时抑制生理学罕见的类别转换；(2) 半马尔可夫约束解码器（semi-Markov constrained decoder），结合持续时间增强的状态空间，在推理阶段联合施加过渡惩罚和最小持续时间约束。该方法不硬性禁止罕见转换，在发射证据充分时允许其出现。

Result: 在6种骨干网络和4个数据集上，StageGuard将过渡违规率（TVR）降至生理学合理水平，将碎片化指数（FI）降低56-62%，同时保持或略微提升分类准确率；约束满足的改进转化为下游睡眠架构统计量误差降低59-79%，并能比无约束基线更准确地恢复专家定义的亚组差异（OSA严重程度、年龄）的方向和效应量。

Conclusion: 通过向深度学习睡眠分期模型中注入生理学先验约束，可以显著提升睡眠图谱的生理学合理性，进而大幅提高下游睡眠架构指标的估计精度，且能更忠实地反映临床亚组差异。

Original Abstract: Automated sleep staging is increasingly used in large-scale studies to derive sleep-architecture endpoints: total sleep time, REM latency, sleep efficiency, and bout-duration statistics. Deep learning models achieve epoch-level accuracy approaching inter-rater agreement, yet often produce hypnograms that violate physiological invariants, such as rare transitions (e.g., direct Wake -> REM) or excessively fragmented sequences. Such violations can bias downstream sleep metrics, regardless of overall accuracy. We propose StageGuard, a plug-and-play, backbone-agnostic structured-inference framework that wraps any neural sleep-staging backbone with physiology-informed priors. StageGuard combines (1) a differentiable soft transition penalty that discourages physiologically rare transitions during training, and (2) a semi-Markov constrained decoder with a duration-augmented state space that jointly enforces transition penalties and minimum bout durations at inference. Unlike hard-prohibition methods, it admits rare transitions when emission evidence is overwhelming, leaving informative pathological events recoverable rather than blocked. StageGuard constrains staging outputs to satisfy known physiological priors rather than modeling sleep generatively. We quantify the validity gap using transition-violation rate (TVR) and fragmentation index (FI) and demonstrate that, across six backbones and four datasets, StageGuard reduces TVR to physiologically plausible levels and lowers FI by 56-62%, while maintaining or slightly improving classification accuracy. Crucially, improved constraint satisfaction translates into 59-79% lower error on derived sleep-architecture statistics not directly optimized by the method, and recovers the direction and effect size of expert-defined subgroup differences (OSA severity, age) more faithfully than the unconstrained baseline.

中文摘要翻译: 自动睡眠分期越来越多地被用于大规模研究以推导睡眠架构终点指标：总睡眠时间、REM潜伏期、睡眠效率和睡眠片段持续时间统计量。深度学习模型能够达到接近评估者间一致性的逐帧准确率，但其输出的睡眠图谱常常违反生理学不变性，例如罕见的转换（如直接从觉醒到REM）或过度碎片化的序列。这类违规无论整体准确率如何，都可能偏倚下游睡眠指标。我们提出StageGuard，一个即插即用、骨干网络无关的结构化推理框架，为任何神经睡眠分期骨干模型包裹上生理学先验知识。StageGuard结合了(1)一个可微的软过渡惩罚项，在训练期间抑制生理学上罕见的转换；以及(2)一个具有持续时间增强状态空间的半马尔可夫约束解码器，在推理时联合施加转换惩罚和最小持续时间约束。与硬性禁止方法不同，当发射证据足够充分时，它允许罕见转换发生，使得有信息价值的病理事件能够被恢复而非被阻断。StageGuard将分期输出约束在已知的生理学先验范围内，而非生成式地建模睡眠。我们使用过渡违规率（TVR）和碎片化指数（FI）来量化有效性差距，并证明在六个骨干网络和四个数据集上，StageGuard将TVR降至生理学合理水平，将FI降低56-62%，同时保持或略微提升分类准确率。关键的是，约束满足的改进使得方法未直接优化的下游睡眠架构统计量误差降低了59-79%，并且比无约束基线更忠实地恢复了专家定义的亚组差异（OSA严重程度、年龄）的方向和效应量。

</details>


### [248] [FILLER: Feature Imputation via Latent Location Exploration and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23295)
*Santu Mondal,Chayan Maitra,Rajat K. De*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为FILLER的特征填补方法，通过在生成模型的二维潜空间中进行迭代搜索来填补缺失值，使用G-NeuroDAVIS作为生成模型，在多个图像数据集上验证了其优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有缺失值填补方法在可扩展性和结构一致性之间难以取得平衡，亟需一种既能保持数据底层结构又能高效处理大规模数据的填补方法。

Method: FILLER方法利用在完整观测数据上训练的生成模型（G-NeuroDAVIS）构建二维潜空间，通过迭代搜索在该潜空间中寻找与不完整测试样本最匹配的潜编码，从而生成合理的填补值；同时提供了迭代搜索收敛性的数学证明。

Result: 在多个图像数据集上，分别在随机缺失和结构化缺失模式下评估，与现有最优方法相比，FILLER在RMSE、PSNR和SSIM指标上表现更优；Wilcoxon符号秩检验验证了统计显著性；下游分类和聚类任务也证实了填补质量。

Conclusion: FILLER通过生成模型潜空间搜索的范式，有效平衡了可扩展性与结构一致性，为缺失值填补提供了一种新的高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: In real-world machine learning applications, incomplete observations create a fundamental challenge. Researchers have come up with several ideas to address this crucial problem. However, current models still face challenges in balancing scalability and structural consistency. This study proposes a feature imputation method, called FILLER, that deliberately searches the two-dimensional latent space produced by a generative model and fills the missing values with appropriate entries. The generative model is trained on fully observed data to generate samples from the latent space, and FILLER uses this trained model to impute the values missing in the corrupted test samples. In this study, G-NeuroDAVIS serves the purpose of the generative model. This work also presents a mathematical proof on the convergence of the iterative search. Finally, FILLER has been evaluated on several image datasets under random and structured missingness patterns with varying levels of imputation complexities. In order to justify the efficacy of FILLER, it has been compared against existing state-of-the-art solution strategies in terms of RMSE, PSNR, and SSIM. In addition, Wilcoxon signed-rank test has been carried out to validate statistical significance. Moreover, downstream analyses (classification and clustering) have also established the quality of imputation in terms of standard metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 在现实世界的机器学习应用中，不完整观测数据带来了一个根本性挑战。研究人员提出了多种思路来解决这一关键问题。然而，现有模型在平衡可扩展性和结构一致性方面仍然面临挑战。本研究提出了一种名为FILLER的特征填补方法，它通过刻意搜索生成模型产生的二维潜空间，并用合适的条目填补缺失值。该生成模型在完整观测数据上训练，以从潜空间中生成样本，而FILLER利用该训练模型来填补受损测试样本中缺失的值。在本研究中，G-NeuroDAVIS充当生成模型。本文还给出了迭代搜索收敛性的数学证明。最后，FILLER在多个图像数据集上进行了评估，涵盖了随机和结构化缺失模式以及不同复杂程度的填补场景。为了验证FILLER的有效性，将其与现有最优解决方案在RMSE、PSNR和SSIM指标上进行了比较。此外，还进行了Wilcoxon符号秩检验以验证统计显著性。同时，下游分析任务（分类和聚类）也通过标准指标验证了填补质量。

</details>


### [249] [AlloBench: Measuring Online Tool Allocation Capability in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23332)
*Daniel Wang,Andrew Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现前沿LLM代理在抽象文本任务中能近乎最优地分配工具创建预算（少量高复用工具），但在代码构建任务中无法迁移该能力，揭示了在线工具分配是当前模型的一个显著能力边界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 可复用工具创建需要代理在当前固定成本与未来潜在复用收益之间进行权衡，理想的代理应创建少量高复用工具而非大量一次性工具。作者希望测试LLM代理是否具备这种有意识的分配行为，以及在抽象框架与代码构建两个场景下的表现差异。

Method: 设计了一个配对基准测试，包含两种上下文：抽象文本公式化任务和代码构建任务。在固定预算下测试多个前沿模型（Claude Haiku, Claude Opus, GPT-5.4-mini, GPT-5.6 Sol）的分配行为。进一步通过消融实验（如不评估脚本、仅部分构建）识别各模型的失败模式，并测试了经策略训练的Qwen模型的泛化能力。

Result: 所有前沿模型在抽象框架中表现近乎最优，但无法将能力迁移到脚本编写任务。前三个模型即使在脚本不被评估时也会失败；GPT-5.6 Sol在较弱操纵下保持选择性，仅在完整构建时崩溃。经策略训练的Qwen模型能泛化抽象分配能力到未见过的词汇变体，但在脚本分配任务上无改进。

Conclusion: 在线工具分配能力是当前前沿LLM代理的一个显著能力边界，即使是最先进的模型也无法将抽象场景中的最优分配行为迁移到实际的代码构建任务中。

Original Abstract: Creating a reusable tool is an investment: an agent pays a fixed cost now in exchange for the potential of future reuse. Therefore, a user should prefer an agent that creates a small number of highly reusable tools, rather than many one-offs. We introduce a paired benchmark that tests whether LLM agents exhibit conscious allocation behavior under a fixed budget in two contexts: an abstract text-based formulation and a code-construction task. We find that every frontier model we test---Claude Haiku, Claude Opus, GPT-5.4-mini, and GPT-5.6 Sol---acts near-optimally in the abstract framing but fails to transfer this ability to script-writing. Through further experiments, we identify the particular failure modes for each model. Notably, the first three models fail even when the scripts are not evaluated, while GPT-5.6 Sol stays selective under that weaker manipulation and collapses only at full construction. Furthermore, an open-source Qwen model policy-trained for abstract allocation generalizes this ability across held-out lexical variations, but sees no improvement at script allocation. Together, these results establish online tool allocation as a significant capability boundary, even for modern frontier models.

中文摘要翻译: 创建可复用工具是一项投资：代理现在支付固定成本，以换取未来复用的潜力。因此，用户应偏好创建少量高复用工具而非大量一次性工具的代理。我们引入了一个配对基准测试，用于检验LLM代理在固定预算下是否在两种情境中展现出有意识的分配行为：抽象的基于文本的公式化任务和代码构建任务。我们发现，我们测试的每个前沿模型——Claude Haiku、Claude Opus、GPT-5.4-mini和GPT-5.6 Sol——在抽象框架中表现接近最优，但未能将此能力迁移到脚本编写任务。通过进一步的实验，我们识别了每个模型的特定失败模式。值得注意的是，前三个模型即使在脚本不被评估时也会失败，而GPT-5.6 Sol在较弱的操纵下仍保持选择性，仅在完整构建时崩溃。此外，一个经过策略训练的开源Qwen模型能够将抽象分配能力泛化到未见过的词汇变体，但在脚本分配任务上未见改进。综合这些结果，在线工具分配被确认为一个显著的能力边界，即使对现代前沿模型也是如此。

</details>


### [250] [Training with (Swap) Regret Loss in a Single-Layer Self-Attention Model: A Case Study on the Probability Simplex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23333)
*Chanwoo Park,Asuman Ozdaglar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了基于遗憾（regret）的损失函数框架，证明单层自注意力模型在遗憾损失下训练时，其前向传播等价于平滑虚拟博弈（smoothed fictitious play）更新，并新提出交换遗憾（swap-regret）损失函数，使得模型前向传播实现Blum-Mansour无遗憾算法。遗憾驱动的注意力机制能够实现博弈论意义上的均衡行为，无需监督学习即可获得在线学习动态。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 将决策理论中的遗憾作为直接损失函数训练模型以做出更优决策，并探索该框架在概率单纯形策略下的理论性质，同时将遗憾损失从外部遗憾扩展到交换遗憾，以增强模型对交换偏离（swap deviation）的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 在概率单纯形策略下重新分析Park et al. (2025)的遗憾损失框架；2) 证明单层自注意力模型在遗憾损失下的驻点与平滑虚拟博弈更新一致；3) 新引入交换遗憾损失函数，并证明其驻点对应Blum-Mansour算法，每个注意力头实现外部遗憾更新（通过平滑虚拟博弈）。

Result: 1) 单层自注意力模型在外部遗憾损失下，驻点前向传播精确匹配平滑虚拟博弈更新；2) 交换遗憾损失下，模型前向传播实现Blum-Mansour无遗憾算法；3) 外部遗憾动态导致粗相关均衡（CCE），交换遗憾动态导致相关均衡（CE）。

Conclusion: 基于遗憾的目标函数能够引导最小化的注意力架构获得具有博弈论保证的在线学习动态，无需这些算法的监督轨迹。这为将博弈均衡求解与深度学习模型结合提供了理论基础。

Original Abstract: We revisit the regret loss framework introduced in Park et al. (2025), which uses decision-theoretic regret as a direct loss function for training models to make better decisions, through the lens of probability-simplex policies. Our first result shows that a single-layer self-attention model trained with regret loss admits a stationary point whose forward-pass exactly matches smoothed fictitious play with the appropriate stepsize that ensures no-regret behavior-i.e., for any given policy input, the model outputs the same update that smoothed fictitious play would produce. In parallel, we also newly introduce a swap-regret loss function, which extends the regret-loss framework beyond external regret and enables models to directly optimize for swap-deviation robustness. We further show that this swap-regret loss admits a stationary point whose forward pass implements the corresponding swap-regret update induced by classical Blum-Mansour no-pass implementation algorithm, with each head implementing an external-regret update via smoothed fictitious play. Together, these results show that regret-trained attention can realize differentiable mechanisms whose deployment induces equilibrium behavior in games: external-regret dynamics lead to coarse correlated equilibrium, while swap-regret dynamics lead to correlated equilibrium. Thus, regret-based objectives steer minimal attention architectures toward online-learning dynamics with game-theoretic guarantees, without supervised traces of those algorithms.

中文摘要翻译: 我们重新审视了Park等人(2025)引入的遗憾损失框架——该框架将决策理论中的遗憾作为直接损失函数来训练模型以做出更优决策——并从概率单纯形策略的视角进行分析。我们的第一个结果表明，使用遗憾损失训练的单层自注意力模型存在一个驻点，其前向传播精确匹配具有适当步长的平滑虚拟博弈（该步长确保了无遗憾行为），即对于任意给定的策略输入，模型输出与平滑虚拟博弈所产生的更新完全相同。同时，我们新引入了一种交换遗憾损失函数，它将遗憾损失框架从外部遗憾扩展至更广的范畴，使模型能够直接优化对交换偏离的鲁棒性。我们进一步证明，该交换遗憾损失存在一个驻点，其前向传播实现了经典Blum-Mansour无遗憾实现算法所对应的交换遗憾更新，其中每个注意力头通过平滑虚拟博弈实现外部遗憾更新。综合这些结果，我们发现经过遗憾训练的注意力机制可以实现可微的机制，这些机制的部署能够在博弈中引发均衡行为：外部遗憾动态导致粗相关均衡，而交换遗憾动态导致相关均衡。因此，基于遗憾的目标函数能够引导最小化的注意力架构获得具有博弈论保证的在线学习动态，而无需这些算法的监督轨迹。

</details>


### [251] [Neural operator discovery from heterogeneous trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23337)
*Zituo Chen,Qiaofeng Li,Jiaxin Hu,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经算子发现（NOD）框架，通过因子化潜在条件公式，从无标签的异质轨迹中同时学习共享的神经算子和系统特异性潜在表征，实现了无需显式因子监督的算子学习与泛化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有神经算子方法需要显式的条件变量（如物理参数、几何形状、边界条件）来扩展到系统族，但实际场景中这些变量往往无法观测。本文旨在解决无显式因子监督下的算子学习问题。

Method: 提出因子化潜在条件公式，联合学习神经算子和低维潜在表征，包含三个关键组件：因子化预测（factorized prediction）、轨迹解耦采样（trajectory-decoupled sampling）和维度选择（dimension selection）。

Result: 在多种系统中，所学潜在表征捕捉了系统变化的本质维度，以平滑且近似可逆的方式组织系统实例，潜在结构与底层控制因子对齐。实现了对未见系统实例的泛化，包括跨模态的零样本外推和稳定的长时域预测。

Conclusion: 建立了无需显式因子监督的可解释算子学习范式，为在缺乏因子标注的真实场景中进行算子学习提供了新框架。

Original Abstract: Neural operators provide data-driven mappings for modeling dynamical systems. Extending them to families of systems typically requires explicit conditioning variables such as physical parameters, geometries, or boundary conditions. In many real-world settings, these quantities are unobserved. Here, we formulate neural operator discovery (NOD) as the problem of learning both shared solution operators and system-specific variation directly from heterogeneous trajectories without access to labeled governing factors. We introduce a factorized latent-conditioning formulation that jointly learns a neural operator and a low-dimensional latent representation through factorized prediction, trajectory-decoupled sampling, and dimension selection. Across diverse systems, the learned latent representation captures the intrinsic dimensionality of system variation and organizes system instances in a smooth and approximately invertible latent structure aligned with the underlying governing factors. This organization enables generalization to previously unseen system instances, including zero-shot extrapolation across regimes and stable long-horizon prediction. These results establish an interpretable paradigm for operator learning in the absence of explicit factor supervision.

中文摘要翻译: 神经算子为建模动力学系统提供了数据驱动的映射。将神经算子扩展到系统族通常需要显式的条件变量，如物理参数、几何形状或边界条件。在诸多实际场景中，这些量是无法观测的。本文提出神经算子发现（NOD）问题，即直接从异质轨迹中同时学习共享的求解算子和系统特异性变化，无需访问标注的控制因子。我们引入了一种因子化潜在条件公式，通过因子化预测、轨迹解耦采样和维度选择，联合学习神经算子和低维潜在表征。在多种系统中，所学潜在表征捕捉了系统变化的本质维度，并以平滑且近似可逆的潜在结构组织系统实例，该结构与底层控制因子对齐。这种组织方式使得模型能够泛化到未见过的系统实例，包括跨模态的零样本外推和稳定的长时域预测。这些结果建立了一种在缺乏显式因子监督情况下的可解释算子学习范式。

</details>


### [252] [Does Graph Compression Preserve Signal Propagation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23338)
*Kawshik Banerjee,Khaled Mohammed Saifuddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图压缩（粗化与稀疏化）对信号传播行为的影响，发现两者在保留信号多样性与传播保真度之间存在根本性权衡：稀疏化保留更高信号多样性但传播轨迹偏离原始图，粗化更忠实于原始传播但导致更强的平滑与秩坍缩。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有图压缩评估仅关注下游任务性能或结构保持，未能直接刻画压缩后传播动力学的变化，因此需要系统研究压缩范式对信号传播行为的影响。

Method: 在五个数据集上，对粗化与稀疏化两种压缩范式，在不同压缩率和传播深度下，使用三种互补指标衡量信号行为（信号多样性、传播保真度等）。

Result: 稀疏化保留更高信号多样性并缓解过平滑，但其传播轨迹逐渐偏离原始图；粗化更忠实地保留传播行为，但代价是更强的平滑和秩坍缩。两种传播目标（保留信号多样性与保留传播保真度）在压缩下存在显著冲突。

Conclusion: 图压缩中存在信号多样性与传播保真度之间的固有权衡，评估协议需要同时考虑这两个维度。

Original Abstract: Graph compression reduces the computational cost of graph learning, but its effect on signal propagation remains largely underexplored. Existing work evaluates compression through downstream task performance or structural preservation, neither of which directly captures how propagation dynamics change after compression. We study two fundamental compression paradigms, coarsening and sparsification, and ask whether they preserve the propagation behavior of the original graph. Across five datasets, varying compression rates, and propagation depths, we measure signal behavior through three complementary metrics. Our results reveal a consistent tension between the two compression families. Sparsification retains higher signal diversity and mitigates oversmoothing, but its propagation trajectory progressively diverges from that of the original graph. Coarsening more faithfully preserves propagation behavior, but at the cost of stronger smoothing and rank collapse. These findings demonstrate that two propagation-centric objectives, preserving signal diversity and preserving propagation fidelity, are distinct and empirically at odds under graph compression, highlighting the need for evaluation protocols that jointly consider both dimensions. The code and results are available at: https://github.com/KawshikBanerjee/Compression-Propagation-Duality

中文摘要翻译: 图压缩降低了图学习的计算成本，但其对信号传播的影响在很大程度上仍未被充分探索。现有工作通过下游任务性能或结构保持来评估压缩，这两种方法均不能直接刻画压缩后传播动态的变化。我们研究了两种基本的压缩范式——粗化和稀疏化，并探究它们是否保留了原始图的传播行为。在五个数据集、不同压缩率和传播深度下，我们通过三种互补指标衡量信号行为。我们的结果揭示了两类压缩之间的一致张力：稀疏化保留了更高的信号多样性并缓解了过平滑，但其传播轨迹逐渐偏离原始图；粗化更忠实地保留了传播行为，但代价是更强的平滑和秩坍缩。这些发现表明，两个以传播为中心的目标——保留信号多样性和保留传播保真度——在图压缩下是不同的且经验上存在冲突，凸显了需要联合考虑这两个维度的评估协议。代码和结果见：https://github.com/KawshikBanerjee/Compression-Propagation-Duality

</details>


### [253] [SPRKD: Effective Knowledge Distillation for Deep Neural Networks via Saddle Region Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23346)
*Aditya Dewan,Arjun Yogeswaran,Benjamin Fedoruk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPRKD方法，将知识蒸馏从教师输出复制重构为利用教师作为优化曲率和领域代理，通过鞍点招募策略让学生重新探索低损失鞍区域，在极低参数量CNN（6,430参数）上达到94.8%准确率，超越传统KD方法24.7个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏方法（如Response KD）在低计算场景下表现不佳：学生模型仅模仿教师输出logits，导致任务性能低下、收敛困难，且仅起到正则化作用而非实质性知识迁移。需要一种新的蒸馏范式，使轻量模型能从弱教师中有效学习。

Method: 提出SPRKD方法：利用Hessian特征值谱密度（ESD）识别低损失鞍点区域；将弱教师集成为一个近似的鞍区域（ASR）；通过注入式迁移学习（Transfer Learning by Injection）将ASR重新参数化到学生网络中；采用指数衰减欧几里得变换、负Hessian特征步长和高斯扰动逼近鞍区域。

Result: 在疟疾血涂片分类任务中，6,430参数CNN从25,546参数弱教师蒸馏达到94.8%验证准确率，比Response KD高24.70个百分点（McNemar p=6.3e-87），与同架构从头训练的基线统计等效（p=1.0）。在MNIST、CIFAR-100、TinyImageNet上比从头训练基线高出最多8个百分点。Hessian ESD和2-D损失景观分析显示收敛到更宽的最小值，Hessian迹和谱半径显著更小。

Conclusion: SPRKD通过将蒸馏从复制范式转变为利用教师作为优化曲率代理，使轻量学生模型能够超越传统KD方法甚至从头训练基线，收敛到更平滑、更宽、噪声鲁棒性更强的局部极小值，为低计算场景下的模型部署提供了有效方案。

Original Abstract: Modern deep neural networks are potent catalysts for scientific and industrial impact, yet excessive parameter counts impede deployment in low-compute settings such as hospital equipment and energy infrastructure. Predominant knowledge distillation (KD) methods favor replication: smaller students mimic teacher output logits, yet empirically yield low task performance, hamper convergence, and act merely as regularization rather than substantive knowledge transfer. We propose Saddle Point Recruitment for Knowledge Distillation (SPRKD), reframing distillation from replication to employing teachers as optimization-curvature and domain proxies, characterizing saddle points as regions of strong further-descent potential via embedding and basin-fractal properties. Using Hessian eigenvalue spectral density (ESD), SPRKD identifies low-loss saddle regions for student re-exploration; weak-teacher ensembles are aggregated into an Approximated Saddle Region (ASR), re-parameterized into the student via Transfer Learning by Injection, and approached with exponentially decaying Euclidean transformations, Negative Hessian Eigensteps, and Gaussian perturbations. On malaria blood smear classification with a 6,430-parameter CNN distilled from a weak 25,546-parameter teacher, SPRKD reaches 94.8% validation accuracy, outperforming Response KD by 24.70 percentage points (McNemar p = 6.3e-87) and matching scratch-trained baselines of the same architecture to statistical equivalence (p = 1.0). Across MNIST, CIFAR-100, and TinyImageNet, SPRKD exceeds scratch-trained baselines by up to 8 percentage points on preliminary benchmarks. Hessian ESD and 2-D loss landscape analysis show convergence to wider minima with substantially smaller Hessian trace and spectral radius than Response KD and control students, indicating smoother descent and greater noise robustness.

中文摘要翻译: 现代深度神经网络是科学和工业应用的强大催化剂，但过多的参数量阻碍了其在医院设备和能源基础设施等低计算场景中的部署。主流的知识蒸馏（KD）方法倾向于复制：较小的学生模仿教师输出的logits，但实验结果表现为任务性能低下、收敛困难，且仅起到正则化作用而非实质性的知识迁移。我们提出鞍点招募知识蒸馏（SPRKD），将蒸馏从复制重构为利用教师作为优化曲率和领域代理，通过嵌入和盆地分形特性将鞍点表征为具有强大进一步下降潜力的区域。利用Hessian特征值谱密度（ESD），SPRKD识别出低损失鞍区域供学生重新探索；弱教师集成被聚合为一个近似的鞍区域（ASR），通过注入式迁移学习重新参数化到学生网络中，并采用指数衰减欧几里得变换、负Hessian特征步长和高斯扰动来逼近该区域。在疟疾血涂片分类任务中，一个6,430参数的CNN从25,546参数的弱教师中蒸馏得到，SPRKD达到94.8%的验证准确率，比Response KD高出24.70个百分点（McNemar p=6.3e-87），并且与同架构从头训练的基线达到统计等价（p=1.0）。在MNIST、CIFAR-100和TinyImageNet上，SPRKD在初步基准测试中比从头训练的基线高出最多8个百分点。Hessian ESD和二维损失景观分析表明，与Response KD和对照学生相比，模型收敛到更宽的最小值，具有显著更小的Hessian迹和谱半径，表明更平滑的下降和更强的噪声鲁棒性。

</details>


### [254] [Exploration of the generative capabilities of Boltzmann machines applied to social systems under the majority rule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23349)
*Mauricio A. Valle,Gonzalo A. Ruz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻尔兹曼机（通过深度信念网络实现）在临界条件下恢复多数规则主导系统的生成能力。实验表明，即使存在输入噪声，DBN仍能生成保持临界状态的样本，但物理可观测量会逐渐退化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索深度信念网络（DBN）在临界条件下恢复由多数规则主导的复杂系统的生成能力，验证非二值可见单元对生成性能的影响。

Method: 训练不同配置的深度信念网络（第一层使用高斯可见单元，支持非二值多状态），通过固定可见单元让DBN"梦想"生成样本，并利用基于卷积网络的离散温度计验证生成样本的临界状态。

Result: 尽管问题复杂，DBN仍能恢复保持临界状态的样本；即使在输入噪声下，生成样本仍能保持临界性，但物理可观测量随噪声逐渐退化。

Conclusion: 深度信念网络能够在临界条件下有效恢复多数规则系统的生成分布，对输入噪声具有一定鲁棒性。

Original Abstract: We study the generative capabilities of Boltzmann machines to recover systems governed by the majority rule under critical conditions. To this end, we train deep belief networks (DBNs) with different configurations, where the first layer can use Gaussian visible units with more than two states (i.e., non-binary units). We then allow the DBN to "dream" samples conditioned on visible units that we keep fixed, and we measure the deviation of this dreamed system from the real one. We also corroborate, using a discrete thermometer based on a convolutional network, that the reconstructions remain in a critical state. Across several training sessions with different architectures, we show that, despite the complexity of the problem, the DBN can recover samples that remain critical even under input noise, with a gradual degradation of physical observables relative to the original sample.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了玻尔兹曼机在临界条件下恢复由多数规则主导的系统的生成能力。为此，我们训练了具有不同配置的深度信念网络（DBN），其中第一层可以使用具有两个以上状态的高斯可见单元（即非二值单元）。然后，我们让DBN在固定可见单元的条件下"梦想"生成样本，并测量该梦想系统与真实系统之间的偏差。我们还利用基于卷积网络的离散温度计验证了重建样本仍处于临界状态。在多个不同架构的训练过程中，我们发现，尽管问题具有复杂性，DBN仍能恢复即使在输入噪声下也保持临界状态的样本，且物理可观测量相对于原始样本逐渐退化。

</details>


### [255] [On the Impossibility of Unbiased and Length-Invariant Policy Optimization with Outcome Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23364)
*Fei Ding,Yongkang Zhang,Yuhao Liao,Zijian Zeng,Huiming Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了GRPO和Dr. GRPO在梯度无偏性与长度不变性之间存在不可调和的权衡：GRPO近似满足长度不变性但梯度有偏，Dr. GRPO满足梯度无偏性但存在长度偏差，两者均非"正确"方案，而是处于根本性权衡的两端。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有工作Dr. GRPO声称通过去除GRPO中的逐轨迹长度归一化可以得到"无偏"优化器，但作者认为这一论断不完整，旨在揭示两种算法在梯度无偏性与长度不变性之间的根本性矛盾。

Method: 作者建立了一个不可能定理，证明在标准结果奖励+GRPO设定下，不存在任何基于长度的加权方案能同时满足梯度无偏性（P1）和长度不变性（P2）。通过参数化族f_α(L)=L^{α-1}刻画完整权衡谱系，其中α=0对应GRPO，α=1对应Dr. GRPO。

Result: 理论分析表明GRPO近似满足P2但违反P1，Dr. GRPO满足P1但违反P2。定量分析显示Dr. GRPO的长度偏差会导致长轨迹对梯度更新的贡献与长度比成正比，即长度偏差依然存在。

Conclusion: 两种算法均非普适意义上的"正确"方案，它们处于一个根本且不可避免的权衡的两端。研究者应根据具体应用场景在梯度无偏性和长度不变性之间做出选择。

Original Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is the dominant reinforcement learning algorithm for training reasoning capabilities in large language models, notably adopted by DeepSeek-R1. The recent improvement Dr. GRPO (COLM 2025) identifies the response-level length bias caused by per-trajectory length normalization in GRPO and proposes removing this normalization, claiming the resulting optimizer is "unbiased." We show that this claim is incomplete. Specifically, we establish an impossibility theorem: under the standard outcome reward + GRPO setting, no length-based weighting scheme can simultaneously achieve the following two properties. (P1) Gradient unbiasedness: the gradient estimator is an unbiased estimate of the true policy gradient. (P2) Length invariance: each trajectory's effective contribution to the gradient is independent of its token length. GRPO approximately satisfies P2 but violates P1; Dr. GRPO satisfies P1 but violates P2. We characterize the complete tradeoff spectrum via the parametric family f_alpha(L) = L^{alpha - 1}, where alpha = 0 recovers GRPO, alpha = 1 recovers Dr. GRPO, and provide quantitative analysis showing that Dr. GRPO's length bias can cause longer trajectories to dominate gradient updates by a factor proportional to the length ratio. Our results reveal that neither algorithm is universally "done right"; they occupy opposite ends of a fundamental and unavoidable tradeoff.

中文摘要翻译: 群体相对策略优化（GRPO）是训练大型语言模型推理能力的主流强化学习算法，已被DeepSeek-R1广泛采用。最近的改进工作Dr. GRPO（COLM 2025）指出了GRPO中逐轨迹长度归一化导致的响应级长度偏差，并提出去除该归一化，声称所得优化器是"无偏的"。我们发现这一论断是不完整的。具体而言，我们建立了一个不可能定理：在标准的结果奖励+GRPO设定下，不存在任何基于长度的加权方案能同时实现以下两个性质：（P1）梯度无偏性——梯度估计是真实策略梯度的无偏估计；（P2）长度不变性——每条轨迹对梯度的有效贡献与其token长度无关。GRPO近似满足P2但违反P1；Dr. GRPO满足P1但违反P2。我们通过参数化族f_α(L)=L^{α-1}刻画了完整的权衡谱系，其中α=0对应GRPO，α=1对应Dr. GRPO，并提供定量分析表明Dr. GRPO的长度偏差会导致较长轨迹对梯度更新的贡献与长度比成正比。我们的结果表明，两种算法均非普适意义上的"正确"方案，它们处于一个根本且不可避免的权衡的两端。

</details>


### [256] [Bitcoin Price Direction Prediction via Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Fusion of Social Sentiment and Technical Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23370)
*Muhammad Abdullah Haroon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于市场状态感知的自适应融合框架（RAML），根据市场波动状态动态调整情感特征与价格特征的融合权重，在比特币子日价格预测任务中优于静态拼接方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法使用静态拼接融合OHLCV技术特征与情感特征，忽略了行为金融学中“零售情感在波动期最具预测性、在平静期噪声大”的发现，无法根据市场状态动态调整融合权重。

Method: RAML框架通过滚动24小时波动率将观测数据划分为稳定和波动两种二元市场状态；利用可学习的sigmoid门控机制，根据检测到的市场状态动态调整情感嵌入相对于价格嵌入的权重——在波动期更信任情感特征，在稳定期更依赖价格特征。

Result: 在3491小时观测数据上，RAML在3小时和6小时预测窗口的macro-F1分别为0.5474和0.5513，3小时AUC最高为0.5084。消融实验证实每个组件均不可或缺，将自适应加权替换为静态拼接导致6小时召回率崩溃（F1: 0.14）。

Conclusion: 状态条件自适应融合是多模态金融预测中必要的设计原则。

Original Abstract: Bitcoin price prediction on sub-daily timescales is a hard open problem in computational finance. Bitcoin exhibits fat-tailed returns, non-stationary dynamics, and a price discovery process influenced by social discourse on Reddit and Twitter. Conventional approaches fuse OHLCV technical features with sentiment via static concatenation, applying identical fusion weights regardless of market state. This is inconsistent with the behavioural finance literature, which shows that retail sentiment is most predictive during volatile periods and noisy during calm ones. This paper proposes Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Learning (RAML), which conditions fusion of sentiment and price features on a dynamically detected binary market regime. Rolling 24-hour volatility partitions observations into stable and volatile regimes; a learnable sigmoid gate adjusts the weight of the sentiment embedding relative to the price embedding, trusting sentiment more during volatility and price dynamics more during stable phases. The system is evaluated on 3,491 hourly observations (July 2024-September 2025), combining Bitcoin OHLCV data with Reddit /r/Bitcoin FinBERT sentiment. Four models are compared - price-only BiLSTM, sentiment-only classifier, static-concatenation BiLSTM, and RAML - across 3-hour and 6-hour horizons, with an ablation study isolating the sentiment branch, regime detection, and adaptive fusion. RAML achieves macro-F1 of 0.5474 (3h) and 0.5513 (6h), with the highest AUC at 3 hours (0.5084), indicating better calibration. Ablation confirms every component is necessary, and replacing adaptive weighting with concatenation causes recall collapse at 6 hours (F1: 0.14). These results establish regime-conditioned adaptive fusion as a necessary design principle for multi-modal financial forecasting.

中文摘要翻译: 比特币在子日时间尺度上的价格预测是计算金融领域的一个困难开放问题。比特币具有厚尾收益率、非平稳动态以及受Reddit和Twitter社交话语影响的价格发现过程。传统方法通过静态拼接将OHLCV技术特征与情感特征融合，无论市场状态如何都施加相同的融合权重。这与行为金融学文献不一致，该文献表明零售情感在波动期最具预测性，在平静期则是噪声。本文提出了状态感知多模态学习（RAML），该方法将情感和价格特征的融合条件化于动态检测到的二元市场状态上。滚动24小时波动率将观测划分为稳定状态和波动状态；一个可学习的sigmoid门控机制调整情感嵌入相对于价格嵌入的权重，在波动期间更信任情感，在稳定阶段更依赖价格动态。该系统在3491小时观测数据（2024年7月至2025年9月）上进行了评估，结合了比特币OHLCV数据与Reddit /r/Bitcoin的FinBERT情感。比较了四种模型——仅价格BiLSTM、仅情感分类器、静态拼接BiLSTM和RAML——涵盖3小时和6小时预测窗口，并进行了隔离情感分支、状态检测和自适应融合的消融研究。RAML在3小时和6小时窗口的macro-F1分别达到0.5474和0.5513，3小时AUC最高（0.5084），表明更好的校准性能。消融实验证实每个组件都是必要的，将自适应加权替换为拼接导致6小时召回率崩溃（F1: 0.14）。这些结果确立了状态条件自适应融合作为多模态金融预测的必要设计原则。

</details>


### [257] [Directional Influence Function: Estimating Training Data Influence in Constrained Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23388)
*Xin Wang,R. Tyrrell Rockafellar,Xuegang,Ban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对约束学习场景下经典影响函数（IF）因忽略可行性边界而导致估计不可靠的问题，提出方向影响函数（DIF），通过将约束学习的最优性条件建模为变分不等式（VI）并分析数据扰动对VI的影响，实现了对约束学习中样本贡献的准确估计。实验表明，DIF在约束线性回归中恢复留一法再训练结果，在公平约束CNN中准确预测数据移除后的测试损失变化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在约束学习（如公平性、安全性、鲁棒性、正则化及物理/逻辑约束）日益普及的背景下，理解训练样本如何影响模型解（如学习参数）对可解释性和鲁棒性至关重要。经典影响函数（IF）通过局部敏感性分析估计样本贡献，但在约束设置中不可靠：数据扰动可能同时改变目标函数和可行域，导致估计结果违反可行性约束。

Method: 提出方向影响函数（DIF），将约束学习的最优性条件建模为变分不等式（VI），通过分析训练数据扰动如何影响该VI来估计样本贡献，从而显式地将约束纳入影响估计中。

Result: 在约束线性回归上，DIF恢复留一法再训练结果，而IF和基于惩罚的IF存在显著偏差；在公平约束CNN上，DIF准确预测数据移除后的测试损失变化，与实际再训练结果高度一致。

Conclusion: DIF是一种高效且可靠的约束学习数据归因工具。

Original Abstract: As constrained learning becomes increasingly common, models are trained under explicit feasibility requirements to enforce fairness, safety, robustness, regulariza- tion, and physics or logic constraints. Understanding how training samples in- fluence the model solution (e.g., learned parameters) is crucial for interpretability and robustness. The classical influence function (IF) estimates sample contribu- tions via local sensitivity analysis, measuring how the solution changes when a specific training sample is perturbed or removed. However, IF becomes unreli- able in constrained settings: data perturbations can reshape both the objective and the feasible region, leading to estimates that violate feasibility. In response, we propose the Directional Influence Function (DIF), a novel estimator that explicitly incorporates these constraints into influence estimation. DIF formulates the opti- mality conditions of constrained learning as a variational inequality (VI) and ana- lyzes how perturbing training data affects this VI. We validate DIF on constrained linear regression and demonstrate that it recovers leave-one-out retraining results, whereas IF and penalty-based IF exhibit significant bias. We further apply DIF to fairness-constrained CNNs, where DIF accurately predicts test loss changes under data removal and aligns closely with actual retraining. Our results establish DIF as an efficient and reliable tool for data attribution in constrained learning.

中文摘要翻译: 随着约束学习日益普及，模型在显式的可行性要求下进行训练，以强制执行公平性、安全性、鲁棒性、正则化以及物理或逻辑约束。理解训练样本如何影响模型解（如学习参数）对于可解释性和鲁棒性至关重要。经典影响函数（IF）通过局部敏感性分析估计样本贡献，衡量特定训练样本被扰动或移除时解的变化方式。然而，IF在约束设置中变得不可靠：数据扰动可能同时改变目标函数和可行域，导致估计结果违反可行性。为此，我们提出方向影响函数（DIF），一种新颖的估计器，它将这些约束显式纳入影响估计中。DIF将约束学习的最优性条件建模为变分不等式（VI），并分析训练数据扰动如何影响该VI。我们在约束线性回归上验证了DIF，证明它能恢复留一法再训练结果，而IF和基于惩罚的IF表现出显著偏差。我们进一步将DIF应用于公平约束CNN，其中DIF准确预测了数据移除后的测试损失变化，并与实际再训练结果高度一致。我们的结果确立了DIF作为约束学习中高效且可靠的数据归因工具。

</details>


### [258] [When Can Depth Replace Precision? A Resource Theory of Quantized Neural Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23390)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究了低比特残差计算替代缺失数值精度的条件，通过松弛控制刻画了量化残差系统的无限深度极限，证明了纯调度逼近松弛类的收敛速率，揭示了全状态写回与增量误差反馈的算术差异，并给出了深度与精度之间的权衡关系。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究在固定输入-输出映射下，何时以及如何用额外的低比特残差计算来替代缺失的数值精度，从而为深度神经网络中的量化训练和推理提供理论基础。

Method: 将量化残差系统建模为从低比特运算库中选择场调度的纯调度问题，利用松弛控制理论刻画无限深度极限；分析纯调度逼近松弛类的收敛速率（有界变差和Hölder依赖情形）；对比全状态写回与增量误差反馈两种执行算术的差异；通过固定教师反证法证明速率下界；建立原对偶边界用于训练前决策。

Result: 1) 纯调度逼近松弛类的速率：有界变差时间依赖下为O(D^{-1})，Hölder依赖下为O(D^{-ϑ}+D^{-1})；2) 全状态写回引入Dρ_z惩罚并可能冻结残差更新，增量误差反馈将增长替换为有界进位项并满足精确的共同格点守恒律；3) 深度与精度权衡：对于相干深度-L高阶高精度比较器，精度匹配需要D=Θ(L)；4) 训练前可通过原对偶边界判定可行、不可行或未定情形。

Conclusion: 深度替代精度只有在明确的运算库、时间范围、执行语义和路由模型下才有意义，并非普适成立。

Original Abstract: When can additional low-bit residual computation replace missing numerical precision for a fixed input-output map? We model a quantized residual system over a fixed horizon as a pure schedule selecting fields from a declared low-bit operation library, and use relaxed controls to characterize its infinite-depth limit. The distance from the target to the closed relaxed reachable set is the exact structural floor: no increase in depth can remove it for that library. Pure schedules approach the relaxed class at rate $O(D^{-1})$ under bounded-variation time dependence and $O(D^{-\vartheta}+D^{-1})$ under Holder dependence of exponent $\vartheta$. Execution arithmetic can reverse this conclusion: full-state write-back introduces a $Dρ_z$ penalty and can freeze residual updates, whereas increment error feedback replaces this growth by a bounded carry term and obeys an exact common-lattice conservation law. A fixed-teacher converse makes this rate sharp: for coherent depth-$L$ first-order high-precision comparators, accuracy matching requires $D=Θ(L)$. Learned codebooks add a metadata resource, while state-dependent routing introduces hybrid event conditions. Verified primal and dual bounds yield feasible, impossible, or unresolved decisions before training. Companion software implements the workflow, and Lean 4 machine-checks the exact discrete core. Depth replaces precision only relative to a declared library, horizon, execution semantics, and routing model.

中文摘要翻译: 对于固定输入-输出映射，何时可以用额外的低比特残差计算替代缺失的数值精度？我们将固定时间范围内的量化残差系统建模为一个纯调度问题，从声明的低比特运算库中选择场，并利用松弛控制来刻画其无限深度极限。目标与闭松弛可达集之间的距离是精确的结构下限：对于该运算库，增加深度也无法消除该距离。在有界变差时间依赖下，纯调度以O(D^{-1})的速率逼近松弛类；在Hölder指数为ϑ的依赖下，逼近速率为O(D^{-ϑ}+D^{-1})。执行算术可以逆转这一结论：全状态写回引入Dρ_z惩罚并可能冻结残差更新，而增量误差反馈将这种增长替换为有界进位项，并遵循精确的共同格点守恒律。基于固定教师的反证法使该速率达到下界：对于相干的深度-L高阶高精度比较器，精度匹配需要D=Θ(L)。学习得到的码本引入元数据资源，而状态相关路由引入混合事件条件。验证后的原对偶边界可在训练前得出可行、不可行或未定的决策。配套软件实现了该工作流，Lean 4对精确离散核心进行了机器验证。深度替代精度仅相对于声明的运算库、时间范围、执行语义和路由模型成立。

</details>


### [259] [A Statistical Difference between Single-Layer Learning and Hierarchical Learning in Wide Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23397)
*Sumio Watanabe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了三层宽神经网络在训练输入到隐藏层权重时的泛化性能，发现训练这些权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差，并且固定权重的设置会在参数空间中出现奇异性，而训练权重则不会。这表明奇异性在宽神经网络中也起着重要作用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无限宽神经网络的理论框架存在两种对立观点：一种将深度学习简化为固定核的核回归，另一种则允许核本身被优化。本文旨在通过有限但大量隐藏单元的三层神经网络，厘清这两种框架的适用性，并探究奇异性在宽网络中的作用。

Method: 研究一个三层神经网络，隐藏单元数量有限但足够大。通过对比训练输入到隐藏层权重与固定这些权重两种设置，分析泛化误差差异以及参数空间中的奇异性现象。

Result: 1) 训练输入到隐藏层权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差。2) 固定权重的设置会在参数空间中出现奇异性，而训练权重则不会。

Conclusion: 奇异性即使在宽神经网络中也扮演着重要角色，这暗示着无限宽网络的两种理论框架在有限宽情况下存在本质差异，训练权重以优化核函数可能比固定核的回归方法更优。

Original Abstract: Hierarchical neural networks are widely used in artificial intelligence, yet their mathematical properties remain incompletely understood. In the infinite-width limit, two different theoretical frameworks have been proposed. One reduces deep learning to kernel regression with a fixed kernel by assuming that the parameters remain close to their initialization, whereas the other allows the parameters to move away from their initialization, requiring the kernel itself to be optimized.
  In this paper, we study a three-layer neural network with a finite but large number of hidden units. We show that training the input-to-hidden weights yields a smaller generalization error than keeping them fixed. Furthermore, the latter setting exhibits singularities in the parameter space, whereas the former does not. These findings indicate that singularities play an essential role even in wide neural networks.

中文摘要翻译: 层次神经网络在人工智能中被广泛使用，然而其数学特性尚未被完全理解。在无限宽极限下，已有两种不同的理论框架被提出。一种通过假设参数始终接近其初始化值，将深度学习简化为具有固定核的核回归；而另一种则允许参数偏离其初始化值，要求核本身得到优化。在本文中，我们研究了一个具有有限但大量隐藏单元的三层神经网络。我们证明，训练输入到隐藏层的权重比固定它们能获得更小的泛化误差。此外，后一种设置在参数空间中表现出奇异性，而前一种则没有。这些发现表明，即使在宽神经网络中，奇异性也扮演着重要角色。

</details>


### [260] [Transfer Learning Architectures for Scalable Multi-Fidelity Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23404)
*Jaewook Lee,Ethan Errington,Christian D. Lorenz,Miao Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在分子与材料的多保真贝叶斯优化（MFBO）中，迁移学习替代模型在非平滑高维空间上显著优于高斯过程（GP），且贪心利用迁移学习均值是更鲁棒的默认策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 高斯过程作为MFBO默认替代模型存在两个核心缺陷：数据累积时扩展性差，且假设平滑性，而这在分子与材料搜索空间中常不成立。迁移学习虽已在属性预测中成功应用，但尚未被测试为闭环优化的引擎。

Method: 在相同的选择规则、保真度预算和模型规模下，系统比较了11种迁移学习替代模型与4种高斯过程方法，涵盖从合成函数到真实化学与材料问题的9个任务。

Result: GP在平滑低维函数上表现最佳，但在分子和材料问题上表现最差；迁移学习替代模型以更少的计算量实现了显著更优的解。不确定性驱动的探索并非可靠有益，校准不能预测优化性能，贪心利用迁移学习均值是更鲁棒的默认策略。

Conclusion: 在分子与材料的多保真贝叶斯优化中，迁移学习是替代模型的更优选择。

Original Abstract: Self-driving laboratories increasingly rely on multi-fidelity Bayesian optimization (MFBO) to balance cheap, approximate evaluations against scarce, expensive ones, with a predictive surrogate at its core.
