Result: 在dSprites数据集上，EC使聚合潜在变量激活分数提升43-62%，FactorVAE分数达到0.891（对比β-VAE变体的0.847），重构误差降低最多38%。在MNIST数据集上，权重过滤将下游分类器的潜在维度从10降至2，同时保持准确率高于90%，且收敛所需的epoch数比无EC的相同流程减少37%。还发现低熵离散因子倾向于合并到单个潜在变量中，而高熵连续因子则分散在多个变量上。

Conclusion: 所提出的熵约束和权重过滤方法能够有效兼顾VAE潜在空间的高编码容量与解耦表示两个目标，在多个基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能，并且揭示了潜在变量熵与生成因子类型（离散/连续）之间的结构关系。

Original Abstract: The usefulness of a variational autoencoder (VAE) depends on two properties of its latent space that are hard to obtain together: high encoding capacity in the individual latent variables, and a low-dimensional, disentangled organization of those variables. Weakening the Kullback-Leibler regularization raises capacity but degrades disentanglement, while strengthening it prunes latent variables away entirely. We formulate VAE training as a soft-constrained optimization problem that addresses both. First, we impose an entropy-based constraint (EC) on individual latent variables, showing that the entropy of a latent code upper-bounds the mutual information it carries about the generative factors of the data. Second, we propose a weight-filter method that exploits the slack of the soft constraint to prune low-entropy dimensions during downstream training. On dSprites, the EC raises the aggregate latent-variable activation score by 43-62% over a vanilla VAE, attains the highest FactorVAE score among the \b{eta} \b{eta}-VAE variants (0.891 vs 0.847), and lowers reconstruction error by up to 38%. On MNIST, the weight filter reduces the latent dimensionality supplied to a downstream classifier from ten to two while holding accuracy above 90%, converging in 37% fewer epochs than the same procedure without the EC. We also find that low-entropy discrete factors tend to merge into a single latent variable, whereas high-entropy continuous factors are distributed across several.

中文摘要翻译: 变分自编码器（VAE）的实用性取决于其潜在空间的两个难以兼得的特性：单个潜在变量的高编码容量，以及变量的低维解耦组织。减弱Kullback-Leibler正则化会提高编码容量但降低解耦度，而增强正则化则会完全剪除潜在变量。本文将VAE训练形式化为一个软约束优化问题，以同时解决这两个问题。首先，我们对单个潜在变量施加基于熵的约束（EC），证明潜在编码的熵上界限制了其与数据生成因子之间的互信息量。其次，我们提出一种权重过滤方法，利用软约束的松弛量在下游训练过程中剪枝低熵维度。在dSprites数据集上，EC使聚合潜在变量激活分数比标准VAE提升43-62%，在β-VAE变体中取得了最高的FactorVAE分数（0.891对比0.847），并将重构误差降低最多38%。在MNIST数据集上，权重过滤将提供给下游分类器的潜在维度从10降至2，同时保持准确率高于90%，比没有EC的相同流程减少37%的收敛轮数。我们还发现，低熵离散因子倾向于合并到单个潜在变量中，而高熵连续因子则分散在多个变量上。

</details>


### [291] [On the post-hoc Evaluation of PDE Discovery: A Multifaceted Challenge of Scientific Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23753)
*Baptiste Mathevon,Farah Cherfaoui,Amaury Habrard,Marc Sebban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了偏微分方程发现领域评估指标的分类法，系统分析了现有指标在多维度评估上的优缺点，并给出了标准化评估实践的建议与未来研究方向。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 偏微分方程发现中，对已发现方程的事后评估是一个多维度难题，需同时考虑预测精度、物理一致性、可解释性和分布外泛化能力，而现有评估指标仅能部分解决该问题，可能导致对物理理论有效性的过度解读。

Method: 通过对机器学习、数值分析、信息论和符号回归等领域的丰富文献进行系统梳理，首次提出了PDE评估指标的分类法，并深入讨论了各指标的优缺点。

Result: 构建了首个PDE评估指标分类体系，指出当前评估往往基于个案进行，缺乏普遍公认的方法论，并据此给出了促进标准化和可靠性评估的建议。

Conclusion: 本文既面向设计新PDE发现算法的机器学习专家，也面向在真实应用中旨在发现和验证可靠科学定律的用户，旨在推动该领域评估实践的标准化与可信化。

Original Abstract: Partial differential equation (PDE) discovery aims to identify from data the governing law of a physical system. Constituting a cornerstone of scientific advancement, it has become during the past decade a major line of research in the rapidly evolving field of Physics-informed Machine Learning (PiML). Among the remaining open problems to address in this domain, the post-hoc evaluation of discovered PDEs raises the particular difficulty of being multifaceted. Indeed, it requires jointly considering predictive accuracy, physical consistency, interpretability, and out-of-distribution generalization capacity. Given that some of these properties are conflicting, it is worth noting that the wide range of existing evaluation metrics only partially address the overall problem, potentially leading to overly interpreted conclusions about the validity of a presumed new physical theory. From an abundant literature spanning machine learning, numerical analysis, information theory or symbolic regression, we propose, to our knowledge, the first taxonomy of PDE evaluation metrics, and discuss their advantages and limitations in depth. Based on the observation that evaluation is often achieved on a case-by-case basis and that a universally accepted methodology remains elusive, we further provide recommendations with the aim of promoting standardized and reliable practices, before sketching promising future lines of research in this field. We argue that this paper is intended both for ML experts who design new PDE discovery algorithms and for users of these methods aiming, in real applications, to discover and validate well-founded scientific laws.

中文摘要翻译: 偏微分方程发现旨在从数据中识别物理系统的控制方程。作为科学进步的基石，在过去十年中，它已成为快速发展的物理信息机器学习领域的一个重要研究方向。在该领域尚待解决的开放问题中，对已发现PDE的事后评估因具有多面性而尤为困难。事实上，它需要同时考虑预测精度、物理一致性、可解释性和分布外泛化能力。鉴于其中一些属性相互冲突，值得注意的是，现有广泛的评估指标仅能部分解决整体问题，可能导致对假设的新物理理论有效性的过度解读。通过梳理涵盖机器学习、数值分析、信息论和符号回归的丰富文献，我们首次提出了PDE评估指标的分类法，并深入讨论了其优点和局限性。基于评估往往在个案基础上进行且缺乏普遍公认方法论的观察，我们进一步提出了旨在促进标准化和可靠实践的建议，并勾勒了该领域有前景的未来研究方向。我们认为，本文既面向设计新型PDE发现算法的机器学习专家，也面向在真实应用中旨在发现和验证可靠科学定律的这些方法的使用者。

</details>


### [292] [WISERouter: LLM Routing with Workload Budget Constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23765)
*Yifei Li,Zihui Gao,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WISERouter框架，将LLM路由建模为带约束的上下文多臂老虎机问题，支持离线和在线学习，在固定预算下优于现有方法且更严格遵循预算约束，在线学习具有亚线性遗憾界。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有LLM路由方法存在两个局限：(i)要么使用启发式方法无法保证预算约束，要么施加固定的每查询预算导致次优性能；(ii)需要监督学习在密集数据集上训练，收集每个查询-模型对的统计信息成本高昂。

Method: 将LLM路由形式化为带约束的上下文多臂老虎机问题，提出WISERouter框架，同时支持基于历史交互的离线学习（WR-Offline）和带探索的在线学习（WR-Online），并证明WR-Online在时间范围T上具有O(√T)的次线性遗憾界。

Result: 在RouterBench和SWE-Bench上：(i) WR-Offline在固定预算下性能超越现有基线，且更严格地遵循预算约束；(ii) WR-Online在性能与基线相当的情况下，显著减少了探索数据的使用量。

Conclusion: WISERouter有效解决了LLM路由中预算约束与数据效率的双重挑战，兼顾了离线和在线场景的需求。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve impressive performance across multiple domains, but using the most capable model for every query is prohibitive at scale. LLM routing exploits diversity in model capability and cost by assigning each query to a suitable model to balance utility and budget. Current methods have two limitations: (i) they either use heuristics that do not always enforce the budget constraint or impose a fixed per-query budget that cannot adapt across the workload and leads to suboptimal performance; (ii) they require supervised learning on a dense dataset with statistics for every query-model pair, which is expensive to collect. To address these challenges, we formulate LLM routing as a constrained contextual multi-armed bandit problem and introduce WISERouter (WR for short), a framework that supports offline learning from historical interactions as well as online learning with exploration. We further prove that WR-Online achieves a sublinear regret bound of $O(\sqrt{T})$ over a time horizon $T$. Empirical results on RouterBench and SWE-Bench demonstrate that (i) WR-Offline surpasses existing baselines in performance under a fixed budget and adheres more closely to budget constraints, and (ii) WR-Online achieves comparable performance to the baselines, while using substantially less exploration data.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型（LLM）在多个领域取得了令人瞩目的性能，但在大规模部署中对每个查询都使用最强模型是成本高昂且不可行的。LLM路由通过利用模型能力和成本的多样性，为每个查询分配合适的模型，以平衡效用和预算。现有方法存在两个局限：(i) 要么使用启发式方法，无法始终保证预算约束，要么施加固定的每查询预算，无法适应工作负载的变化，导致性能次优；(ii) 需要监督学习在包含每个查询-模型对统计信息的密集数据集上进行训练，收集成本高昂。为解决这些挑战，我们将LLM路由形式化为带约束的上下文多臂老虎机问题，并提出WISERouter（简称WR）框架，该框架支持基于历史交互的离线学习以及带探索的在线学习。我们进一步证明WR-Online在时间范围T上具有O(√T)的次线性遗憾界。在RouterBench和SWE-Bench上的实验结果表明：(i) WR-Offline在固定预算下性能超越现有基线，且更严格地遵循预算约束；(ii) WR-Online在性能与基线相当的同时，使用的探索数据显著减少。

</details>


### [293] [A Comparison of Data Augmentation Methods for Training Deep Neural Networks on Synthetic Aperture Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23770)
*C. J. Moore,Gregory D. Vetaw,Jordan Malof*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了多种数据增强策略（包括传统图像增强和基于物理的增强）在合成孔径声纳自动目标识别中训练深度神经网络的效果，并探究了与现代架构（如Transformer）的结合，发现增强能提升识别准确率但并非所有增强都有益。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: SAS ATR训练DNN面临标注目标样本稀缺的问题，因为采集真实SAS数据成本高、耗时长。数据增强是缓解该问题的有效策略，但已有研究提出了多种增强方法，缺乏系统性比较。

Method: 系统比较了多种现有的数据增强策略（包括传统图像增强如对比度变化、裁剪，以及基于SAS物理特性的增强），并在现代DNN架构（如Transformer）上评估增强的效果。

Result: 数据增强能够提升目标识别准确率，但不同增强方法的收益存在差异，并非所有增强都有益。

Conclusion: 数据增强对SAS ATR有促进作用，但效果因方法而异，需要谨慎选择增强策略。

Original Abstract: In this work we study Automatic Target Recognition (ATR) for Synthetic Aperture Sonar (SAS) data with a focus on deep neural networks (DNNs). The main challenge in training DNNs for SAS ATR arises from the limited quantity of labeled target examples, which arises due to the significant costs and time required to collect real-world SAS data. One successful general strategy for mitigating the problem of limited training data is augmentation, which generates additional synthetic training data by introducing realistic variations to available real-world data. Prior research has investigated a variety of augmentation strategies for SAS ATR, including conventional image augmentations (e.g., contrast changes, cropping) as well as physics-based augmentations, which are motivated the specific physics of SAS data. Building on prior work, we systematically compare many of these existing augmentation strategies for training DNNs for SAS ATR. We also investigate the impact of augmentation when combined with modern DNN architectures such as transformers. The results indicate that augmentation can improve target recognition accuracy, although benefits vary, and not all augmentations are beneficial.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究面向合成孔径声纳（SAS）数据的自动目标识别（ATR）问题，重点关注深度神经网络（DNN）。训练DNN用于SAS ATR的主要挑战在于标注目标样本数量有限，这是由于采集真实SAS数据需要高昂的成本和大量时间。缓解训练数据不足问题的有效通用策略之一是数据增强，即通过对现有真实数据引入逼真的变化来生成额外的合成训练数据。已有研究探索了多种用于SAS ATR的增强策略，包括传统图像增强（如对比度变化、裁剪）以及基于物理的增强，后者受SAS数据特定物理原理的启发。在已有工作的基础上，我们系统比较了多种现有的增强策略在训练DNN用于SAS ATR时的效果。我们还研究了将增强与现代DNN架构（如Transformer）结合时的影响。结果表明，数据增强可以提高目标识别准确率，但收益因方法而异，且并非所有增强都有益。

</details>


### [294] [Scale Weight Decay and Train Better](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23777)
*Anuj Apte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种缩放权重衰减（scaled weight decay）方法，根据峰值学习率比例调整权重衰减强度，在不改变渐进优化目标的前提下保持权重衰减的稳定性优势，在混合专家模型训练中以相同超参数实现30%的验证损失下降加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准解耦权重衰减在训练过程中会导致网络权重持续收缩，偏离未正则化方法的渐进优化目标。作者希望设计一种权重衰减策略，既保留其稳定性优势，又避免引入额外的渐近偏差。

Method: 受Robbins-Monro条件启发，提出将权重衰减系数缩放为峰值学习率的比例η/η_max。理论证明了该方法对于SGD和非欧几里得谱优化器Muon均能保持未正则化方法的渐近平稳性保证。通过稳态分析解释了标准权重衰减下权重范数持续收缩而缩放权重衰减下趋于常数的原因。

Result: 在72M-930M参数的混合专家模型（约600 tokens/active parameter）训练中，Muon-SW相比相同超参数的Muon达到相同验证损失加速30%。若趋势保持，该方法可大幅加速前沿模型的预训练。

Conclusion: 缩放权重衰减是一种简单有效的方法，仅需几行代码实现，可在不改变渐进优化目标的情况下加速模型训练。

Original Abstract: The discovery of scaling laws has motivated training neural networks on ever increasing quantities of data. This is typically done with a constant decoupled weight decay which causes the network weights to shrink steadily over the course of training. Taking inspiration from the Robbins--Monro conditions, we propose to scale weight decay by the fraction of the peak learning rate $η/η_{\max}$. We prove that this scaled weight decay preserves the asymptotic stationarity guarantees of the corresponding unregularized methods for both stochastic gradient descent and the non-Euclidean spectral optimizer Muon, thereby avoiding the additional asymptotic bias introduced by constant decoupled weight decay. This retains the stability benefits of weight decay without changing the asymptotic optimization target. Using a steady-state analysis, we explain why under standard weight decay the weight norm shrinks steadily as training proceeds, whereas under scaled weight decay it settles to a roughly constant value. When applied to the training of mixture-of-experts models, Muon with scaled weight decay (Muon-SW) consistently outpaces Muon with identical hyperparameters, reaching the same validation loss $\mathbf{30\%}$ faster at our largest scale across models from $72 - 930$ million parameters trained at $\sim 600$ tokens per active parameter. If this trend continues to hold, the method promises to substantially accelerate the pre-training of frontier models while requiring only a few lines of code to implement.

中文摘要翻译: 缩放定律的发现促使人们用不断增长的数据量来训练神经网络。通常这采用恒定的解耦权重衰减，导致网络权重在训练过程中持续收缩。受Robbins-Monro条件的启发，我们提出将权重衰减按峰值学习率的比例η/η_max进行缩放。我们证明，对于随机梯度下降和非欧几里得谱优化器Muon，这种缩放权重衰减保留了相应未正则化方法的渐近平稳性保证，从而避免了恒定解耦权重衰减引入的额外渐近偏差。这既保留了权重衰减的稳定性优势，又不改变渐近优化目标。通过稳态分析，我们解释了为什么在标准权重衰减下权重范数随训练持续收缩，而在缩放权重衰减下则稳定在近似常数值。当应用于混合专家模型的训练时，采用缩放权重衰减的Muon（Muon-SW）在相同超参数下持续优于标准Muon，在我们最大规模的实验中（模型参数从72M到930M，每个活跃参数约600个tokens），达到相同验证损失的速度加快了30%。如果这一趋势持续保持，该方法有望在仅需几行代码实现的前提下，大幅加速前沿模型的预训练。

</details>


### [295] [SCTA: An Agentic Framework for Stable and Interpretable Target Gene Discovery from Single-Cell RNA Sequencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23821)
*Shuyu Chen,Chen Zhu,Ye Zhang,Yang Li,Qiqi Xie,Haohan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种面向单细胞RNA测序数据的决策中心化智能体框架SCTA，通过将靶点发现任务分解为多个专业智能体并约束结构化生物学证据，实现更稳定、可解释的靶点基因发现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 单细胞RNA测序数据中的异质性使得靶点发现对分析流程中的选择高度敏感，现有工作流和通用分析智能体常产生不稳定或难以解释的靶点假设，限制了其在疾病研究中的可靠性。

Method: SCTA（Single-Cell Target Agent）是一个决策中心化的智能体框架，将靶点发现任务分解为多个专业智能体，每个智能体对应单细胞分析流程中的关键决策点，并通过结构化生物学证据约束下游推理。

Result: 在遗传性慢性胰腺炎的消融研究中，完整证据整合的SCTA在独立运行中产生最稳定的靶点选择，并恢复了先前研究验证过的生物学一致、疾病相关的机制。

Conclusion: 面向单细胞分析结构量身定制的决策感知智能体编排能够提高精准医学中靶点发现的鲁棒性、可解释性和实用性。

Original Abstract: Identifying therapeutic target genes from single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) data remains a fundamental challenge in translational biology. Unlike bulk assays, scRNA-seq captures heterogeneous cellular states and rare subpopulations, but this same heterogeneity makes target discovery highly sensitive to analytical choices throughout the pipeline, including preprocessing, cell population selection, differential expression analysis, and downstream biological interpretation. As a result, existing workflows and general-purpose analysis agents often produce unstable or difficult-to-interpret target hypotheses, limiting their reliability for disease-focused discovery. We present SCTA (Single-Cell Target Agent), a decision-centric agentic framework for stable and interpretable target gene discovery from scRNA-seq data. Rather than treating analysis as a single general-purpose reasoning task, SCTA decomposes target discovery into specialized agents aligned with key decision points in the single-cell pipeline and constrains downstream reasoning with structured biological evidence. In a representative ablation study on hereditary chronic pancreatitis, we demonstrate that SCTA's full evidence integration yields the most stable target selection across independent runs among the tested configurations, while recovering biologically coherent, disease-relevant mechanisms validated in prior studies. These results suggest that decision-aware agent orchestration tailored to the structure of single-cell analysis can improve the robustness, interpretability, and practical utility of target discovery in precision medicine.

中文摘要翻译: 从单细胞RNA测序（scRNA-seq）数据中识别治疗靶点基因仍然是转化生物学中的一个基本挑战。与批量检测不同，scRNA-seq能够捕获异质性细胞状态和稀有亚群，但这种异质性也使得靶点发现对分析流程中的选择高度敏感，包括预处理、细胞群选择、差异表达分析以及下游生物学解释。因此，现有工作流和通用分析智能体常常产生不稳定或难以解释的靶点假设，限制了其在疾病导向发现中的可靠性。我们提出了SCTA（单细胞靶点智能体），一个面向稳定且可解释的scRNA-seq靶点基因发现的决策中心化智能体框架。SCTA并非将分析视为单一通用推理任务，而是将靶点发现分解为与单细胞分析流程中关键决策点对齐的专业智能体，并通过结构化生物学证据约束下游推理。在一项关于遗传性慢性胰腺炎的代表性消融研究中，我们证明了SCTA的完整证据整合在测试配置中能在独立运行中产生最稳定的靶点选择，同时恢复了先前研究中验证过的生物学一致且与疾病相关的机制。这些结果表明，针对单细胞分析结构量身定制的决策感知智能体编排能够提高精准医学中靶点发现的鲁棒性、可解释性和实用性。

</details>


### [296] [DriveDNA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Naturalistic Driving Dataset and Benchmark for Driving Style Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23822)
*Yuhang Wang,Lingyao Li,Hao Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了DriveDNA，一个大规模自然驾驶数据集和基准，用于个性化驾驶风格建模。通过465名驾驶员在115种车型上的4121次驾驶记录（975小时），该基准定义了在相似条件下驾驶员一致的行为模式。实验表明，学习到的表征显著优于传统描述符（AUROC .935 vs .707），但纯视频模型存在严重的路线泄露问题，强调可靠评估必须考虑行为价值和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自然驾驶数据中，驾驶风格信号难以与车辆、道路和环境特定模式分离，现有模型可能将情境规律误认为是驾驶员风格。因此需要一个大规模、可控的数据集和评估基准来正确隔离和评估驾驶风格。

Method: 构建DriveDNA数据集（465名驾驶员，115种车型，4121次驾驶，975小时，10Hz频率，含前视视频）；定义驾驶风格为相似条件下的驾驶员一致行为模式；设计三个核心任务：少样本驾驶员重识别、个性化行为预测、条件匹配对比；提供行为标注和276,248个规则生成的动作事件；评估多种基线（经典描述符、监督/自监督时序编码器、多模态融合、概率预测、零样本基础模型），采用固定多种子协议。

Result: 学习表征在未见驾驶员上显著优于经典描述符（AUROC .935 vs .707）；在匹配驾驶条件下学习表征保留驾驶员特定信息，而描述符性能接近随机；纯视频模型重识别精度高但存在严重路线泄露（依赖情境捷径而非驾驶行为）。

Conclusion: 可靠的驾驶风格评估必须同时考察学习表征的行为价值和其对车辆、驾驶、条件混杂因素的鲁棒性，避免被情境捷径误导。

Original Abstract: Driving style captures stable, driver-specific patterns in how a vehicle is driven. In naturalistic data, however, this signal is hard to isolate because drivers are observed in different vehicles, on different roads, and under different conditions, so models may mistake vehicle- or situation-specific regularities for driver-specific style. We introduce DriveDNA, a large-scale naturalistic dataset and benchmark for personalized driving-style modeling, comprising 4,121 drives from 465 drivers across 115 vehicle models and totaling 975 hours of human-controlled driving at 10 Hz with forward video, collected from community drivers in everyday use. DriveDNA defines driving style as a consistent, driver-specific behavioral pattern in how a vehicle moves under similar conditions. The benchmark evaluates this signal through three core tasks: few-shot driver re-identification, personalized behavior prediction, and condition-matched comparison, and provides behavioral annotations plus 276,248 rule-generated maneuver events across six classes with large-scale human auditing. We evaluate baselines spanning classical descriptors, supervised and self-supervised time-series encoders, multimodal fusion, probabilistic prediction, and zero-shot foundation models under a fixed multi-seed protocol. Learned representations substantially outperform classical descriptors on unseen drivers (AUROC .935 vs. .707) and retain driver-specific information under matched driving conditions, while descriptor performance approaches chance. Video-only models achieve comparable re-identification accuracy but exhibit severe route leakage, showing that strong recognition may arise from contextual shortcuts rather than driving behavior. These findings show that reliable driving-style evaluation must assess both the behavioral value of learned representations and their robustness to vehicle, drive, and condition confounds.

中文摘要翻译: 驾驶风格捕捉了车辆驾驶中稳定且具有驾驶员特异性的模式。然而，在自然驾驶数据中，这一信号难以分离，因为驾驶员是在不同车辆、不同道路和不同条件下被观测的，因此模型可能将车辆或情境特定的规律性误认为是驾驶员特有的风格。我们提出了DriveDNA，一个用于个性化驾驶风格建模的大规模自然驾驶数据集和基准，包含来自465名驾驶员跨越115种车型的4,121次驾驶记录，总计975小时以10 Hz采样的人类控制驾驶数据及前视视频，由社区驾驶员在日常使用中收集。DriveDNA将驾驶风格定义为在相似条件下车辆运动方式中一致的、驾驶员特异的行为模式。该基准通过三个核心任务评估这一信号：少样本驾驶员重识别、个性化行为预测和条件匹配对比，并提供行为标注以及经大规模人工审计的276,248个涵盖六类规则生成的动作事件。我们在固定多种子协议下评估了涵盖经典描述符、监督和自监督时序编码器、多模态融合、概率预测以及零样本基础模型的基线方法。学习得到的表征在未见过的驾驶员上显著优于经典描述符（AUROC .935 vs .707），并在匹配驾驶条件下保留驾驶员特定信息，而描述符性能接近随机。纯视频模型达到了相当的重识别精度，但表现出严重的路线泄露，表明强识别能力可能源于情境捷径而非驾驶行为。这些发现表明，可靠的驾驶风格评估必须同时考察学习表征的行为价值及其对车辆、驾驶和条件混杂因素的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [297] [Latent-LoRA: Compact Latent-Space Adapters with Gradient-Free Routing for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23837)
*Reza Rahimi Azghan,Gautham Krishna Gudur,Giulia Pedrielli,Pavan Turaga,Hassan Ghasemzadeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent-LoRA，一种无需重放、无需训练路由模块的持续学习方法。利用冻结LLM嵌入层的池化token嵌入拟合高斯混合模型实现任务无关的适配器选择，并通过SVD约束任务参数到预训练权重的主子空间，结合正交正则化控制任务间干扰，在五个模型规模和两个基准上实现SOTA性能与近乎零遗忘。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的基于LoRA的持续学习方法要么在推理时需要任务身份信息，要么不加区分地累加所有适配器导致输出失真，而基于门控的方法引入了额外的可训练参数，这些参数本身也需要防止遗忘。本文旨在解决这些问题，实现无需可训练路由组件且无遗忘的持续学习。

Method: 1) 利用冻结的LLM嵌入层输出的池化token嵌入，拟合高斯混合模型(GMM)进行任务无关的适配器选择，无需梯度训练；2) 通过SVD将每个任务的LoRA参数约束到预训练权重的主子空间，获得紧凑的潜在空间参数化；3) 在该子空间内施加正交正则化直接控制任务间干扰。

Result: 在五个模型规模和两个持续学习基准上，Latent-LoRA取得了最先进的性能，且实现了近乎零的灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: Latent-LoRA通过无训练的高斯混合模型路由和主子空间参数约束，有效解决了持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，无需重放数据、无需可训练路由模块，且每个任务使用的参数更少。

Original Abstract: Large language models generalize well to individual tasks but lack an inherent mechanism for learning them sequentially, leading to catastrophic forgetting. To mitigate this, LoRA-based continual learning methods allocate a separate low-rank adapter per task, yet existing approaches either require task identity at inference or sum all adapters indiscriminately, letting irrelevant branches distort the output. Recent gating-based solutions route inputs to the correct adapter but introduce trainable parameters that themselves need protection against forgetting. In this work, we observe that pooled token embeddings from a frozen LLM embedding layer already separate task distributions throughout the learning sequence. A Gaussian mixture model fitted on these embeddings, without any gradient-based training, is sufficient for task-agnostic adapter selection at test time. This eliminates the need for a learned gating module. On the adapter side, constraining each task's parameters to the principal subspace of the pretrained weights via SVD yields a compact latent-space parameterization. Within this subspace, orthogonal regularization directly controls inter-task interference. The resulting system, Latent-LoRA, is replay-free, requires no trainable routing component, and uses substantially fewer parameters per task. Experiments across five model scales and two established continual learning benchmarks show state-of-the-art performance with near-zero forgetting.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型在单个任务上具有良好的泛化能力，但缺乏顺序学习多个任务的内在机制，导致灾难性遗忘。为缓解这一问题，基于LoRA的持续学习方法为每个任务分配一个独立的低秩适配器，但现有方法要么在推理时需要任务身份信息，要么不加区分地对所有适配器求和，使不相关分支扭曲输出。近期基于门控的解决方案将输入路由到正确的适配器，但引入了本身也需要防止遗忘的可训练参数。在本工作中，我们观察到来自冻结LLM嵌入层的池化token嵌入在整个学习序列中已经能够分离任务分布。基于这些嵌入拟合的高斯混合模型，无需任何基于梯度的训练，就足以在测试时进行任务无关的适配器选择。这消除了对学习门控模块的需求。在适配器方面，通过SVD将每个任务的参数约束到预训练权重的主子空间，得到紧凑的潜在空间参数化。在该子空间内，正交正则化直接控制任务间的干扰。最终系统Latent-LoRA无需重放、无需可训练路由组件，且每个任务使用的参数更少。在五个模型规模和两个已建立的持续学习基准上的实验表明，该方法取得了最先进的性能，且近乎零遗忘。

</details>


### [298] [Covariance Last-Layer Ensembles: Function-Space Diversity for Efficient Uncertainty Quantification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23856)
*H. Martin Gillis,Isaac Xu,Gabriel Spadon,Thomas Trappenberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出协方差最后一层集成（cov-LLE），通过直接对成员激活施加协方差惩罚来恢复最后一层集成的函数空间多样性，解决了权重正交化无法缓解的成员预测趋同问题，在匹配成员数K时以单倍骨干网络成本恢复了深度集成的大部分多样性和校准性能，并基于此视角构建了检测器分类体系，提出了尺度不变、无标签的方向性评分来修复近OOD检测失败。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 最后一层集成（LLE）的成员共享骨干网络梯度，容易收敛到相同函数，导致成员间多样性崩溃，而现有的权重正交化（Orthonormal Certificates）仅去相关成员权重而非预测，无法从根本上恢复函数空间的多样性。因此需要一种直接在函数空间层面阻止多样性崩溃的方法。

Method: 提出协方差最后一层集成（cov-LLE），在共享冻结特征图上部署K个线性单元，并对成员激活施加直接的协方差惩罚（covariance penalty），从而在函数空间上强制成员预测保持多样性，而非仅在权重空间进行正交化约束。

Result: 在匹配K时，cov-LLE以单倍骨干网络成本（1× backbone cost）恢复了深度集成的大部分多样性和校准性能：分布内预测方差从0.05提升至9.3（深度集成为22.1，单位×10⁻³），ECE从0.135降至0.090（深度集成为0.035），且不损失精度。此外，将正交证书（OC）视为最后一层集成的特例后，构建了双轴检测器分类体系，并提出尺度不变、无标签的方向性评分，在多个骨干网络上将近OOD的ROC AUC提升了+0.16到+0.18。

Conclusion: 直接对函数空间施加多样性约束比间接的权重正交化更有效，cov-LLE能够以极低的计算成本逼近深度集成的性能，同时所提出的检测器分类体系和方向性评分为OOD检测提供了新的理论视角和实践改进。

Original Abstract: A Last-Layer Ensemble (LLE), $K$ linear units on one shared frozen feature map, is an efficient single-pass approach to the disagreement-based epistemic uncertainty for out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. Its weakness is that members share the backbone gradient and can converge toward the same function, collapsing the inter-member diversity the signal depends on. Whether last-layer diversity can be restored, and what mitigates the collapse, is an open question. The weight-orthonormality defining Orthonormal Certificates (OC), the weight-orthonormal special case of the LLE, is only an indirect correction; it decorrelates the weights of the members, not their predictions. Here, we instead target the collapse directly in function space, with a Covariance Last-Layer Ensemble (cov-LLE) that places a direct covariance penalty on member activations. Cov-LLE restores the function-space diversity that weight-orthonormality cannot, and at matched $K$ recovers much of the diversity and calibration of a deep ensemble at $1\times$ backbone cost (in-distribution prediction variance $0.05\!\to\!9.3$ vs.\ $22.1$ ($\times10^{-3}$), and ECE $0.135\!\to\!0.090$ vs.\ $0.035$, for a $K\times$-cost deep ensemble), at no cost to accuracy. Viewing OC as a last-layer ensemble also organizes detectors into a two-axis taxonomy (by how their units are trained and how their outputs are scored) and exposes the OC score as a magnitude, motivating a scale-invariant, label-free direction score that repairs its near-OOD failure, adding $+0.16$ to $+0.18$ ROC AUC on every backbone.

中文摘要翻译: 最后一层集成（LLE）是一种高效的单次前向传播方法，它在单个共享冻结特征图上部署K个线性单元，利用基于分歧的认知不确定性进行分布外（OOD）检测。其弱点在于成员共享骨干网络梯度，可能收敛至相同函数，导致信号赖以存在的成员间多样性崩溃。最后一层的多样性能否恢复，以及什么因素能够缓解这种崩溃，仍然是一个悬而未决的问题。定义正交证书（OC）的权重正交性——即LLE的权重正交特例——仅是一种间接修正；它对成员的权重进行去相关，而非对其预测进行去相关。本文直接在函数空间上针对这种崩溃加以解决，提出协方差最后一层集成（cov-LLE），对成员激活施加直接的协方差惩罚。Cov-LLE恢复了权重正交性所无法实现的函数空间多样性，在匹配的K取值下，以单倍骨干网络成本恢复了深度集成的大部分多样性和校准性能（分布内预测方差从0.05提升至9.3，深度集成为22.1（×10⁻³）；ECE从0.135降至0.090，深度集成为0.035），且不损失精度。将OC视为最后一层集成的视角还将检测器组织成一个双轴分类体系（按单元的训练方式及其输出的评分方式分类），并揭示OC评分实质上是一种幅值，这启发了一种尺度不变、无标签的方向性评分，修复了其近OOD检测失败，在每一个骨干网络上使ROC AUC提升了+0.16到+0.18。

</details>


### [299] [Controllable Diversity in Normalization-Based Implicit Ensembles via Softmax-Temperature Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23860)
*Mihai Suteu,Ovidiu Serban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出σN-Ens，一种基于归一化的隐式集成方法，通过sigmoid有界缩放器将每个成员视为多任务架构中的一个任务，并引入softmax温度正则化器来调控成员间的共享平衡，以较低的参数成本达到或超越深度集成的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 深度集成虽然能提供最可靠的不确定性估计，但其成本随成员数量线性增长。隐式集成通过共享骨干网络降低了成本，但成员多样性是集成质量的主要决定因素，现有方法无法在训练过程中塑造多样性，只能在初始化时固定或通过架构内置，缺乏对成员间共享程度的灵活调控。

Method: 提出σN-Ens，一种基于归一化的隐式集成方法：(1) 将每个成员视为多任务架构中的一个任务，通过sigmoid有界缩放器（sigmoid-bounded scalers）调制共享骨干网络；(2) 引入softmax温度正则化器，调控成员间的共享均衡水平，刻画精度-校准前沿；(3) 仅复制归一化层，可包裹卷积和Transformer骨干网络，支持预训练模型微调适配；(4) 将隐式集成表达的认知不确定性框架化为调制不确定性（modulation uncertainty）。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet和SST-2上对ResNet和Transformer进行评测，σN-Ens以远低于深度集成的参数成本匹配或超越其性能；随集成规模扩展效果好，而分区方法会崩溃；在分布偏移下保持校准能力。

Conclusion: σN-Ens通过归一化层的轻量复制和温度正则化，以极低的参数开销实现了与深度集成相当甚至更优的性能，在分布偏移下保持良好的校准能力，但OOD检测能力相对较弱。该方法支持预训练模型微调，具有良好的通用性。

Original Abstract: Deep ensembles provide the most reliable uncertainty estimates in deep learning, but their cost grows linearly with the number of members. Implicit ensembles lower this cost by sharing a single backbone across members. Member diversity is a primary determinant of ensemble quality, yet no implicit ensemble can shape it during training; existing methods fix it at initialisation or build it into the architecture. We introduce $σ$N-Ens, a normalisation-based implicit ensemble that treats each member as a task in a multi-task architecture and modulates the shared backbone through sigmoid-bounded scalers. We also introduce a softmax-temperature regulariser, which shapes the equilibrium level of sharing between members and traces the accuracy-calibration frontier. Because only normalisation layers are replicated, the mechanism can wrap convolutional and transformer backbones alike, also allowing pretrained models to be adapted through a short fine-tune. We frame the epistemic uncertainty such an ensemble expresses as modulation uncertainty, and explain why its calibration holds under input corruption, and why its out-of-distribution detection is weaker. Our method is evaluated across ResNets and transformers on CIFAR-10/100, ImageNet and SST-2. $σ$N-Ens matches or outperforms deep ensembles at a fraction of their parameter cost, scales with ensemble size where partitioning methods collapse, and maintains calibration under distribution shift.

中文摘要翻译: 深度集成在深度学习中提供了最可靠的不确定性估计，但其成本随成员数量线性增长。隐式集成通过共享单个骨干网络来降低这一成本。成员多样性是集成质量的主要决定因素，然而现有的隐式集成方法无法在训练过程中塑造多样性；现有方法要么在初始化时固定多样性，要么将其内置到架构中。我们提出了σN-Ens，一种基于归一化的隐式集成方法，它将每个成员视为多任务架构中的一个任务，并通过sigmoid有界缩放器来调制共享骨干网络。我们还引入了一个softmax温度正则化器，它可以塑造成员之间的共享均衡水平，并刻画精度-校准前沿。由于只需复制归一化层，该机制可以同时包裹卷积和Transformer骨干网络，并且允许通过短期微调来适配预训练模型。我们将这种集成所表达的认知不确定性框架化为调制不确定性，并解释了为什么其校准在输入损坏下仍然保持，以及其分布外检测能力较弱的原因。我们的方法在CIFAR-10/100、ImageNet和SST-2数据集上对ResNet和Transformer进行了评估。σN-Ens以远低于深度集成的参数成本匹配或超越深度集成，其性能随集成规模扩展而提升（而分区方法会崩溃），并在分布偏移下保持良好的校准能力。

</details>


### [300] [XMix: Combating Extremely Noisy Labels via Local Smoothness in Self-Supervised Feature Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23865)
*Chengqi Li,Yangdi Lu,Zhihao Shi,Wenbo He,Chamseddine Talhi,Nadjia Kara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出XMix框架，利用自监督特征空间中的局部平滑性，在不依赖噪声标签的前提下增强样本选择全过程，在极端噪声和类别不平衡场景下显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于记忆效应的样本选择方法在极端噪声、类别不平衡场景下表现不佳，且需要精细调参或先验噪声知识，亟需一种不依赖潜在损坏标签的鲁棒方法。

Method: XMix框架包含三个阶段：1) 利用自监督特征空间中的最大似然近邻估计噪声率；2) 借助特征近邻识别额外干净样本并实现类别平衡选择；3) 在半监督学习阶段利用近邻样本生成更可靠的伪标签。

Result: 在极端噪声环境下大幅超越现有方法，在标准LNL基准测试中保持优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过利用自监督特征空间的局部平滑性，XMix有效克服了传统样本选择方法在极端噪声和类别不平衡下的局限性，为标签噪声学习提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Supervised deep learning models rely on large, accurately labeled datasets, yet noisy annotations are often unavoidable and can severely degrade performance under high noise levels. Recent state-of-the-art methods tackle this by using sample selection strategies that exploit the memorization effect to filter out clean data for semi-supervised learning. However, these methods struggle with extreme noise, class imbalance, and require careful tuning or prior noise knowledge. To address these limitations, we propose XMix, a novel framework that leverages local smoothness in the self-supervised feature space to systematically enhance all stages of the sample selection process, without dependence on potentially corrupted labels. First, XMix estimates the noise rate using maximum likelihood among self-supervised feature neighbors. Second, these neighbors then help identify additional clean samples and ensure balanced selection across classes during sample selection. Finally, in the semi-supervised learning phase, XMix uses neighboring samples to generate more reliable pseudo-labels. Our empirical results show that XMix substantially outperforms existing methods in extremely noisy environments and maintains superior performance in standard LNL benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 监督式深度学习模型依赖大规模、准确标注的数据集，然而噪声标注往往难以避免，且在高噪声水平下会严重降低模型性能。近期最先进的方法通过利用记忆效应进行样本选择，筛选出干净数据用于半监督学习。然而，这些方法在极端噪声、类别不平衡场景下表现不佳，且需要精细调参或先验噪声知识。为解决这些局限，我们提出XMix——一种新颖框架，利用自监督特征空间中的局部平滑性，在不依赖潜在损坏标签的前提下，系统性地增强样本选择的全过程。首先，XMix通过自监督特征近邻的最大似然估计来估算噪声率。其次，这些近邻帮助识别额外的干净样本，并在样本选择过程中确保类别平衡。最后，在半监督学习阶段，XMix利用近邻样本生成更可靠的伪标签。实验结果表明，XMix在极端噪声环境下显著优于现有方法，并在标准LNL基准测试中保持优越性能。

</details>


### [301] [A Coulomb Particle Model for Learning Kernel Attention in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23869)
*Masoud Badiei Khuzani,Sharath Honnaiah,Atiq Islam,Alex Cozzi,Abraham Bagherjeiran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于粒子的方法，通过优化核-目标对齐并加入Riesz/Coulomb排斥势正则化来学习随机特征分布，从而提升核机器性能。在线性化Transformer注意力中实现，能在保持线性推理复杂度的同时提高精度、校准性和鲁棒性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 随机特征为核机器提供了可扩展的近似，但其性能高度依赖于特征分布的选择，现有的固定分布无法自适应任务需求，因此需要一种能根据任务学习最优特征分布的方法。

Method: 提出粒子方法学习随机特征分布：通过优化核-目标对齐目标函数，同时使用Riesz/Coulomb排斥势正则化粒子，形成Hamiltonian动力学系统，其均场描述由McKean-Vlasov方程刻画。在应用层面，先在对齐阶段学习正随机特征映射，然后冻结核，用交叉熵训练其余网络参数。

Result: 在合成分类和句子级基准实验上，所提的核化注意力方法在多种特征映射下均能提升精度、校准性和鲁棒性，同时保持线性注意力的推理复杂度。

Conclusion: 通过任务自适应的学习方式优化随机特征分布，可以显著提升核化线性注意力的性能，且不牺牲计算效率。

Original Abstract: Randomized features provide a scalable approximation to kernel machines, but their performance depends strongly on the choice of feature distribution. We propose a particle-based method that learns this distribution by optimizing kernel-target alignment while regularizing particles with a Riesz/Coulomb repulsive potential. The resulting Hamiltonian yields diverse, task-adaptive random features and admits a mean-field description through a McKean--Vlasov equation. We instantiate the method in linearized Transformer attention by learning positive random-feature maps in a first alignment phase, then freezing the kernel and training the remaining network parameters with cross-entropy. Experiments on synthetic classification and sentence-level benchmarks show that learned kernelized attention can improve accuracy, calibration, and robustness for several feature maps while preserving linear-attention inference complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 随机特征为核机器提供了可扩展的近似方法，但其性能高度依赖于特征分布的选择。我们提出了一种基于粒子的方法，通过优化核-目标对齐同时使用Riesz/Coulomb排斥势对粒子进行正则化来学习该分布。由此产生的Hamiltonian动力学生成了多样化且任务自适应的随机特征，并通过McKean-Vlasov方程实现了均场描述。我们将该方法实例化到线性化Transformer注意力中：在第一个对齐阶段学习正随机特征映射，然后冻结核，用交叉熵训练其余网络参数。在合成分类和句子级基准上的实验表明，学习得到的核化注意力可以在多种特征映射下提高精度、校准性和鲁棒性，同时保持线性注意力推理复杂度。

</details>


### [302] [Flash-CNNCap: Capacitance Extraction via Image Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23877)
*Hector R. Rodriguez,Jiechen Huang,Wenjian Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flash-CNNCap提出了一种基于CNN的电容提取器，将全矩阵电容预测重构为空间贡献图上的图像到图像回归，通过总电容模型和主控耦合模型分别预测空间图，经掩码聚合降至导体级值，将全矩阵重建从O(n²)降至O(n)次前向传播，在CapBench上实现与ResNet基线相当的总电容精度（1.5-3.1% MARE）和最优耦合精度（3.0-4.6% MARE），并在含134个导体的窗口上获得17.5倍全矩阵加速。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于标量CNN的电容提取器需要对包含n个导体的窗口进行O(n²)次前向传播才能恢复所有成对电容，计算效率低下。本文旨在通过引入空间贡献图回归范式，将全矩阵电容重建的计算复杂度从O(n²)降至O(n)，实现显著的加速。

Method: 将全矩阵电容预测重新定义为图像到图像的回归问题，预测空间贡献图而非标量值。具体包括两个模型：总电容模型预测每个导体的总电容空间贡献图，主控耦合模型预测耦合电容空间贡献图。通过掩码聚合（mask aggregation）将空间图降维至导体级数值，再经过对称化处理构建Maxwell-style电容矩阵。使用U-Net架构，从导体级标签学习无需逐像素监督。

Result: 在13种模型配置的消融研究中，U-Net在总电容上达到1.5-3.1% MARE（与ResNet基线相当），在耦合电容上达到3.0-4.6% MARE（所有CapBench子集最优）。在平均含134个导体的窗口上实现17.5倍全矩阵加速。部署管线处理1024个窗口耗时51.23秒，相比OpenRCX获得4.4倍加速。

Conclusion: Flash-CNNCap通过空间贡献图回归范式有效降低了全矩阵电容提取的计算复杂度，在保持高精度的同时实现了显著加速，并提供了从DEF到SPEF的完整部署流程。

Original Abstract: We present Flash-CNNCap, a CNN-based capacitance extractor that reformulates full-matrix capacitance prediction as image-to-image regression over spatial contribution maps. Prior scalar CNN-based extractors require $O(n^2)$ forward passes to recover all pairwise capacitances in a window with $n$ conductors. Flash-CNNCap replaces the scalar target with dense contribution maps: a total-capacitance model and a master-conditioned coupling model each predict a spatial map that is reduced to conductor-level values through mask aggregation, cutting full-matrix reconstruction to $O(n)$ passes. The resulting totals and symmetrized pairwise couplings define the corresponding Maxwell-style capacitance matrix under the standard off-diagonal sign convention. The maps are learned from conductor-level labels without per-pixel supervision. An ablation study over 13 model configurations selects a U-Net that matches ResNet baselines on total capacitance (1.5-3.1% MARE) and achieves the strongest coupling accuracy (3.0-4.6% MARE) across all evaluated CapBench subsets, with a $17.5\times$ full-matrix speedup on windows containing 134 conductors on average. A deployed pipeline reads Design Exchange Format (DEF) geometry and writes Standard Parasitic Exchange Format (SPEF) output, processing 1,024 windows in 51.23 seconds with a $4.4\times$ speedup over OpenRCX on the same benchmark. Code and trained models are available at https://github.com/THU-numbda/flash-cnncap.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出Flash-CNNCap，一种基于CNN的电容提取器，它将全矩阵电容预测重新表述为空间贡献图上的图像到图像回归。先前的标量CNN提取器需要对包含n个导体的窗口进行O(n²)次前向传播才能恢复所有成对电容。Flash-CNNCap用密集贡献图替代标量目标：总电容模型和主控条件耦合模型各自预测一个空间图，通过掩码聚合降维至导体级数值，将全矩阵重建缩减至O(n)次前向传播。所得的总电容和对称化的成对耦合电容在标准非对角线符号约定下定义了相应的Maxwell-style电容矩阵。这些图从导体级标签学习，无需逐像素监督。对13种模型配置的消融研究选出了一个U-Net，它在总电容上与ResNet基线相当（1.5-3.1% MARE），并在所有评估的CapBench子集上实现了最强的耦合精度（3.0-4.6% MARE），在平均含134个导体的窗口上获得了17.5倍的全矩阵加速。部署管线读取Design Exchange Format (DEF)几何信息并写入Standard Parasitic Exchange Format (SPEF)输出，在51.23秒内处理1024个窗口，相比同一基准上的OpenRCX获得4.4倍加速。代码和预训练模型可在https://github.com/THU-numbda/flash-cnncap获取。

</details>


### [303] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Predicting Nitrous Oxide Flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23880)
*Freddy Yu,Jashanjeet Kaur Dhaliwal,Subhadeep Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了物理信息神经网络（PINN）在农业N₂O通量排放预测中的应用，基于DayCent过程模型的机理方程构建物理残差，发现物理约束虽会降低分布内精度，但能提升跨站点泛化的鲁棒性，不过跨站点泛化仍具挑战。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前N₂O通量排放预测主要依赖过程模型（如DayCent、Cycles）和经典AI模型，而物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在该领域的应用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用PINN融合机理知识与数据驱动方法，提升预测性能。

Method: 基于DayCent过程模型家族的机理方程，构建严格推导、文献可溯源的物理残差；在此基础上搭建并训练基于MLP的PINN模型，使用涵盖美国四个地理分布不同的农业站点的多站点数据集进行训练。

Result: 在所有物理损失加权超参数λ取值下，PINN一致且显著优于未校准的Cycles模拟（R²=0.01）；MLP基线在10个随机种子下平均R²=0.411。物理约束在留出验证中持续降低模型性能（低λ时轻微下降，高λ时显著下降），但在留一站点验证中一致提升模型性能并降低性能变异性。然而跨站点泛化仍具挑战，所有种子和λ值下在保留站点上R²均为负值。

Conclusion: 物理约束会牺牲分布内精度以换取分布外鲁棒性，将模型锚定到生物地球化学上合理的行为以适应陌生土壤条件，但模型的地理跨站点泛化能力仍然不足。

Original Abstract: Nitrous oxide (N$_2$O) is the dominant ozone-depleting substance emitted in the 21st century, and the third largest contributor to anthropogenic greenhouse gases due to its high potency and long atmospheric lifetime, with more than 70% of N$_2$O emissions occurring as a result of agricultural processes. Current approaches to predicting N$_2$O flux emissions include process-based models such as DayCent and Cycles, as well as classical AI models, but the application of Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) to predicting N$_2$O flux emissions is largely underexplored. Our paper draws upon the mechanistic equations that underlie the DayCent family of process-based models to construct a rigorously derived, literature-traceable physics residual. We then build and train an MLP-based PINN on a multi-site agricultural dataset spanning four geographically distinct US agricultural sites. Across all tested values of the physics loss weighting hyperparameter $λ$, our PINN consistently and substantially outperformed uncalibrated Cycles simulation (R$^2=0.01$), with our MLP baseline achieving mean R$^2=0.411$ across ten random seeds. Physics constraints consistently degrade model performance in holdout validation, with marginal degradation at low $λ$ and significant degradation at high $λ$, but consistently improve model performance and reduce performance variability in leave-one-site-out validation. This suggests that physics constraints sacrifice in-distribution accuracy for out-of-distribution robustness, anchoring the model toward biogeochemically plausible behavior on unfamiliar soil conditions --- though cross-site generalization remains challenging, with negative R$^2$ across all seeds and $λ$ values on our geographically distinct held-out site.

中文摘要翻译: 一氧化二氮（N₂O）是21世纪排放的主要臭氧消耗物质，由于其高潜力和长大气寿命，也是人为温室气体的第三大贡献者，其中超过70%的N₂O排放源自农业过程。目前预测N₂O通量排放的方法包括基于过程的模型（如DayCent和Cycles）以及经典AI模型，但物理信息神经网络（PINNs）在预测N₂O通量排放方面的应用尚未得到充分探索。本文借鉴DayCent系列过程模型背后的机理方程，构建了一个严格推导、文献可溯源的物理残差。随后，我们在一个涵盖美国四个地理分布不同的农业站点的多站点农业数据集上，构建并训练了一个基于MLP的PINN。在所有测试的物理损失加权超参数λ值下，我们的PINN一致且显著优于未校准的Cycles模拟（R²=0.01），MLP基线在10个随机种子下平均R²达到0.411。在留出验证中，物理约束持续降低模型性能，低λ时性能轻微下降，高λ时显著下降；但在留一站点验证中，物理约束一致提升了模型性能并降低了性能变异性。这表明物理约束牺牲了分布内精度以换取分布外鲁棒性，将模型锚定到生物地球化学上合理的行为以适应陌生的土壤条件——尽管跨站点泛化仍然具有挑战性，在所有种子和λ值下，在地理上不同的保留站点上R²均为负值。

</details>


### [304] [ADVERSARIAL: And-Inverter Graph-Assisted Hardware Trojan Detection At Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23882)
*Yaroslav Popryho,Debjit Pal,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于符号学习与知识图谱嵌入的硬件木马检测方法，通过对大规模SoC的门级网表构建AIG布尔网络，利用KGE生成紧凑节点表示，实现线性复杂度下的高效HT检测。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有硬件木马检测方法无法应对现代SoC中数亿至数百亿门级的巨大规模，存在严重的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 将平铺门级网表建模为以AIG表示的布尔网络，将每条有向连接表示为知识图谱嵌入框架中的三元组，生成紧凑且固定维度的逐节点表示，保留多跳结构上下文信息；利用AIG的有界扇入和统一语义确保训练与推理复杂度与边数呈线性关系。

Result: 在大规模SoC基准测试上的实验表明，木马节点与良性节点之间存在清晰的几何分离，方法具有良好的实际可扩展性。

Conclusion: 符号启发的学习机制能够有效区分正常电路结构与表征木马触发器和负载的罕见且功能不一致的连接，为大规模硬件木马检测提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Modern System-on-Chip (SoCs) often contain hundreds of millions to tens of billions of gates, making existing Hardware Trojan (HT) detection methods impractical due to their immense scale. The proposed approach incorporates symbolically enabled learning by modeling flattened gate-level netlists as Boolean networks represented as And-Inverter Graphs (AIGs), where all internal nodes are 2-input AND gates and inversions reside on the edges. Each directed connection is expressed as a triple within a Knowledge Graph Embedding (KGE) framework, producing compact, constant-size per-node representations that retain multi-hop structural context. The AIG's bounded fan-in and uniform semantics ensure training and inference complexity scale linearly with edge count, addressing major scalability bottlenecks in HT detection. Symbolically enabled learning across deep datapaths enables the model to differentiate circuit structures from rare and functionally inconsistent connections that signify potential Trojan triggers and payloads. Experiments on large-scale SoC benchmarks demonstrate clear geometric separation between Trojan and benign nodes and practical scalability.

中文摘要翻译: 现代片上系统(SoC)通常包含数亿至数百亿个门级单元，这使得现有硬件木马(HT)检测方法因规模过大而难以实用。本文提出一种符号启发的学习方法，将平铺门级网表建模为以与-逆变图(AIG)表示的布尔网络，其中所有内部节点均为2输入与门，反相位于边上。每条有向连接表示为知识图谱嵌入(KGE)框架中的一个三元组，生成紧凑且固定维度的逐节点表示，保留多跳结构上下文。AIG的有界扇入和统一语义确保了训练与推理复杂度与边数呈线性关系，解决了HT检测中的主要可扩展性瓶颈。跨深层次数据路径的符号学习使模型能够区分正常电路结构与那些表征潜在木马触发器和负载的罕见且功能不一致的连接。在大规模SoC基准测试上的实验表明，木马节点与良性节点之间存在清晰的几何分离，且方法具有良好的实际可扩展性。

</details>


### [305] [WorldDiT: A Unified Diffusion Architecture for World and Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23909)
*Sen Wang,R. Gnana Praveen,Bidhan Roy,Marcos Villagra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldDiT是一种统一的扩散Transformer架构，它将动作生成与视觉世界建模耦合，无需大规模预训练VLM骨干网络，即可在LIBERO仿真套件上取得强基线性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 近期许多机器人策略依赖大规模预训练视觉-语言模型作为动作骨干以提升控制能力，但这些模型参数量巨大。本文旨在探索是否可以在不使用大规模预训练VLM动作骨干的前提下，通过耦合动作生成与视觉世界建模来实现强性能。

Method: 提出WorldDiT，一种统一的扩散Transformer架构。在训练时，单个扩散Transformer同时生成连续动作块，并从未来相机帧中预测归一化的RGB块目标，从而实现动作生成与视觉世界建模的统一。

Result: 在四个LIBERO仿真套件上，WorldDiT位于报告的总模型参数与平均成功率之间的Pareto前沿，提供了一个强有效的十亿参数以下基线。

Conclusion: WorldDiT证明，无需大规模预训练VLM动作骨干，通过统一的扩散Transformer架构耦合动作生成与视觉世界建模，即可在机器人控制任务上取得优异性能，为未来的规模扩展研究提供了坚实的亚十亿参数基线。

Original Abstract: Many recent robot policies pursue stronger control by using large pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) as the action backbone. We introduce WorldDiT, a unified diffusion transformer architecture that couples action generation with visual world modeling and achieves strong performance without a large pretrained VLM action backbone. During training, a single diffusion transformer generates continuous action chunks and predicts normalized RGB patch targets from future camera frames. Across four LIBERO simulation suites, WorldDiT lies on the reported Pareto frontier for total model parameters and mean success among methods reporting all four suites. These results provide a strong sub-billion-parameter baseline for future scaling studies.

中文摘要翻译: 近期许多机器人策略通过使用大规模预训练视觉-语言模型作为动作骨干来追求更强的控制能力。我们提出WorldDiT，一种统一的扩散Transformer架构，它将动作生成与视觉世界建模相耦合，无需大规模预训练VLM动作骨干即可实现强性能。在训练过程中，单个扩散Transformer同时生成连续动作块，并从未来相机帧中预测归一化的RGB块目标。在四个LIBERO仿真套件上，WorldDiT位于所有四个套件报告的方法中总模型参数与平均成功率之间的Pareto前沿。这些结果为未来的规模扩展研究提供了一个坚实的亚十亿参数基线。

</details>


### [306] [Greedy dynamical meta-learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23925)
*Aria Yom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种双层元学习算法，内环让智能体在高维空间自我优化，外环用低维零阶方法优化，以解决梯度下降长期不稳定和零阶优化器高维失效的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 梯度下降在大模型上扩展性好但长时程不稳定，零阶优化器可任意时长但在高维下失效，而学习发生在长时间尺度的大模型中，两种方法都难以加速学习过程。

Method: 提出一种双层元学习算法：内环让智能体对自身参数进行高维优化（修改自身权重和偏置），外环对内环进行低维优化；由于外环参数极少，可使用标准零阶方法。

Result: 该算法结合了梯度下降的高维扩展能力和零阶优化的长期稳定性，通过外环的低维处理规避了零阶方法在高维下的局限性。

Conclusion: 通过将高维优化限制在内环、低维优化放在外环，使得零阶方法能够在元学习层面发挥作用，有望在学习加速方面产生有效特性。

Original Abstract: Gradient descent scales well to large models, but becomes unstable over long time horizons. Gradient-free optimizers can scale to arbitrary timespans, but are hobbled by high dimensions. Since learning occurs in large models over long timescales, neither of these approaches is likely to produce traits which can accelerate the learning process. Instead, we propose a meta-learning algorithm in which the agent learns to modify its own weights and biases. Our algorithm consists of an inner loop, wherein the agent performs some high-dimensional optimization upon itself, and an outer loop, wherein we perform some low-dimensional optimization upon the inner loop. Since the outer loop handles very few parameters, standard zeroth-order methods may be used.

中文摘要翻译: 梯度下降在大规模模型上具有良好的扩展性，但在长时间尺度上变得不稳定。无梯度优化器可以扩展到任意时间跨度，却受到高维度的限制。由于学习发生在长时间尺度的大模型中，这两种方法都不太可能产生能够加速学习过程的特性。为此，我们提出一种元学习算法，使智能体学会修改自身的权重和偏置。我们的算法包含一个内环——智能体在其中对自身进行高维优化，以及一个外环——我们在其中对内环进行低维优化。由于外环处理的参数极少，可以使用标准的零阶方法。

</details>


### [307] [DECAF: De-Clustering for Adaptive Representational Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23934)
*Anjie Le,Can Peng,Hongcheng Guo,J. Alison Noble*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现许多机器遗忘方法易受聚类攻击，提出DECAF后处理方法，通过输入噪声、置信度抑制和熵基输出多样化破坏遗忘数据的特征空间结构，在CIFAR-10上达到0.10%遗忘类准确率和79.4%保留准确率，性能优于基线且效率更高。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 许多现有的机器遗忘方法容易受到一种简单的无监督聚类攻击，该攻击可以恢复类别结构，导致遗忘不彻底。这使得这些方法不适合需要可靠处理删除请求的持续部署场景。因此，需要一种能够有效抵抗聚类攻击的遗忘方法。

Method: 提出DECAF（DE-Clustering for Adaptive Forgetting），一种仅操作于遗忘集的后处理方法。该方法结合三种技术：（1）输入噪声注入；（2）置信度抑制；（3）基于熵的输出多样化。通过这三者的组合来破坏与遗忘数据相关的残差特征空间结构，从而打破聚类。

Result: 在CIFAR-10数据集上使用ResNet-18模型，DECAF达到0.10%的遗忘类准确率、79.4%的保留准确率和0.88的AUS（遗忘未学习分数），全面超越所有基线方法。在基于聚类的分析中，DECAF的性能与使用完整训练集的遗忘方法相当，但计算效率显著更高。

Conclusion: DECAF作为一种高效的post-hoc遗忘方法，能够在仅使用遗忘集的情况下有效破坏特征空间中的聚类结构，抵抗聚类攻击，兼顾了遗忘效果与计算效率，适用于需要持续可靠处理删除请求的场景。

Original Abstract: Machine unlearning, which aims to remove the influence of specific training data from a trained model, is a key requirement for privacy, accountability, and adaptive deployment. We argue that many unlearning methods are vulnerable to a simple clustering attack, which can recover class structure in an unsupervised manner, limiting their suitability for continual deployment where removal requests must be handled reliably on demand. To address this, we propose DECAF (DE-Clustering for Adaptive Forgetting), a post-hoc method that operates only on the forget set and is designed to break the cluster. DECAF combines input noise, confidence suppression, and entropy-based output diversification to disrupt the residual feature-space structure associated with forgotten data. On CIFAR-10 with ResNet-18, DECAF attains 0.10% forget-class accuracy, 79.4% retain accuracy, and an AUS of 0.88, surpassing all other baselines. In cluster-based analysis, it attains performance comparable to that of unlearning methods that use the full training set, while being significantly more efficient. Code: https://github.com/ale256/representation_unlearning.

中文摘要翻译: 机器遗忘旨在从已训练模型中移除特定训练数据的影响，是保障隐私、问责和自适应部署的关键需求。我们认为许多遗忘方法容易受到一种简单的聚类攻击，该攻击以无监督方式恢复类别结构，限制了这些方法在需要按需可靠处理删除请求的持续部署场景中的适用性。为解决这一问题，我们提出DECAF（DE-Clustering for Adaptive Forgetting），一种仅操作于遗忘集的后处理方法，旨在打破聚类。DECAF结合输入噪声、置信度抑制和基于熵的输出多样化，以破坏与遗忘数据相关的残差特征空间结构。在CIFAR-10数据集上使用ResNet-18模型，DECAF实现了0.10%的遗忘类准确率、79.4%的保留准确率和0.88的AUS，全面超越所有基线方法。在基于聚类的分析中，DECAF达到了与使用完整训练集的遗忘方法相当的性能，同时效率显著更高。代码：https://github.com/ale256/representation_unlearning。

</details>


### [308] [Variational Boosting for Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23940)
*Pavlos Protopapas,Kaylee Vo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种变分提升框架，通过函数空间中的加性构造和局部正交条件，将全局非线性细化分解为一系列良态子问题，实现稳定的二阶优化，为多阶段PINN提供投影函数梯度下降的几何解释。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统单块PINN存在病态条件、谱偏差和优化不稳定性等问题，亟需更稳定的训练框架。

Method: 引入变分提升框架，在函数空间中逐步添加弱学习器构成解，每个弱学习器满足局部正交条件（等价于在函数流形切空间上的投影函数梯度下降步），通过小规模网络实现完整牛顿或共轭梯度更新。

Result: 将全局非线性细化分解为一系列良态子问题，保留了算子的全变分结构，实现了稳定二阶优化。

Conclusion: 该框架为多阶段PINN提供了投影函数梯度下降的几何解释，并为非线性微分方程实现了稳定的二阶优化。

Original Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) solve differential equations by minimizing the residual of a nonlinear operator over a neural parameterization of the solution. However, monolithic PINNs often suffer from ill-conditioning, spectral bias, and optimization instability.
  We introduce a variational boosting framework in which solutions are constructed additively in function space. Each stage trains a weak learner whose converged correction satisfies a local orthogonality condition, equivalent to a projected functional gradient descent step onto the tangent space of the network's function manifold. Because each correction network is deliberately small, the restricted minimization admits full Newton or conjugate gradient updates, which are typically infeasible in large PINNs. The resulting method separates global nonlinear refinement into a sequence of well-conditioned subproblems while preserving the full variational structure of the operator.
  This framework provides a geometric interpretation of multi-stage PINNs as projected functional gradient descent and enables stable second-order optimization for nonlinear differential equations.

中文摘要翻译: 物理信息神经网络（PINNs）通过最小化解的神经参数化上非线性算子的残差来求解微分方程。然而，单块PINN常常遭受病态条件、谱偏差和优化不稳定的困扰。我们引入了一种变分提升框架，其中解在函数空间中逐步累加构建。每个阶段训练一个弱学习器，其收敛后的校正满足局部正交条件，等价于在网络函数流形切空间上的投影函数梯度下降步。由于每个校正网络被刻意设计得很小，受限的最小化可以应用完整的牛顿或共轭梯度更新，而这在大型PINN中通常是不可行的。所提出的方法将全局非线性细化分解为一系列良态子问题，同时保留了算子的全变分结构。该框架为多阶段PINN提供了投影函数梯度下降的几何解释，并为非线性微分方程实现了稳定的二阶优化。

</details>


### [309] [Joint Flow Matching for Generator-Consistent Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23946)
*Hayden McAlister,Lech Szymanski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Joint Flow Matching (JFM) 是一种用于多变量连续归一化流的训练框架，通过为每个变量在时间端点分配相反角色，实现前向和反向条件推断的一致性，同时支持联合分类与生成，无需事后校准即可获得良好校准的置信度分数和分类器一致的图像生成。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 标准流匹配将所有变量同时从噪声传输到数据，缺乏从共享联合模型中进行前向和反向条件推断的自然机制。JFM 旨在解决这一问题，实现联合分布的一致性。

Method: JFM 为每个变量在时间端点分配相反角色，从而产生一致的联合分布，使得前向或反向积分成为同一联合的条件分布。

Result: 在条件数据集上验证，JFM 具有竞争性的准确率，置信度分数内在良好校准，无需事后校准，并能实现分类器一致的图像生成。

Conclusion: JFM 为判别-生成模型的联合分类与生成提供了可解释性基础，在保持准确性的同时实现了良好的置信度校准和一致的生成。

Original Abstract: We introduce Joint Flow Matching (JFM), a training framework for continuous normalising flows over multiple variables. Standard flow matching transports variables from noise to data simultaneously, offering no natural mechanism for forward and reverse conditional inference from a shared joint model. JFM resolves this by assigning opposite roles to each variable at the temporal endpoints. We prove that JFM produces a consistent joint distribution where that forward or reverse integration are conditionals of the same joint. We explore this consistency in the context of joint classification and generation as the basis for interpretability in discriminative-generative models. We validate JFM on conditional datasets producing competitive accuracy with inherently well-calibrated confidence scores without post-hoc calibration, and classifier-consistent image generation.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了联合流匹配（Joint Flow Matching, JFM），一种用于多变量连续归一化流的训练框架。标准流匹配同时将所有变量从噪声传输到数据，缺乏从共享联合模型中进行前向和反向条件推断的自然机制。JFM 通过为每个变量在时间端点分配相反角色来解决这一问题。我们证明了 JFM 生成一致的联合分布，其中前向或反向积分都是同一联合的条件分布。我们在联合分类和生成的背景下探索这种一致性，作为判别-生成模型可解释性的基础。我们在条件数据集上验证了 JFM，其在无需事后校准的情况下产生了具有竞争性的准确率和内在良好校准的置信度分数，以及分类器一致的图像生成。

</details>


### [310] [Understanding Machine Unlearning Through the Lens of Mode Connectivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23970)
*Jiali Cheng,Hadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从模式连接性（mode connectivity）视角研究机器遗忘，发现遗忘模型通常位于参数空间中平滑低损耗路径连接的盆地，揭示了遗忘过程的非线性进展和不同遗忘方法的机制差异，并基于此提出了集成方法以提升泛化性和抗攻击能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前机器遗忘领域虽然取得进展，但遗忘过程的损失景观和优化几何特性缺乏深入理解。本文旨在通过模式连接性这一新视角揭示遗忘模型的参数空间结构。

Method: 引入\"遗忘中的模式连接性\"（MCU）概念，在课程学习、二阶优化等不同训练设置下，以及在不同遗忘方法之间，系统评估遗忘模型之间的模式连接性。通过分析参数空间中低损耗路径的平滑性，研究遗忘模型之间的关系。

Result: 1）许多遗忘模型位于相连的盆地中，具有平滑的保留/遗忘行为；2）训练动态变化可将解移入不同盆地；3）同一盆地内模型在隐私指标上可存在显著差异；4）遗忘过程从原始模型到遗忘模型呈非线性进展；5）多数近似遗忘方法与重训练在机制上不同（线性连接性揭示）；6）基于MCU的集成可提升泛化性和对重学习攻击的鲁棒性；7）MCU平滑性与遗忘难度相关。

Conclusion: 模式连接性为理解机器遗忘提供了有价值的分析工具，揭示了遗忘模型的结构特性，并为改进遗忘方法（如集成策略）提供了新方向。

Original Abstract: Machine Unlearning aims to remove undesired information from trained models without full retraining from scratch. Despite recent progress, the loss landscape and optimization geometry of unlearning are poorly understood. In this paper, we study machine unlearning through the lens of mode connectivity--the phenomenon that independently trained models can often be connected by smooth low-loss paths in parameter space. We introduce {\em mode connectivity in unlearning} (MCU) and evaluate it across a range of settings, including curriculum learning, second-order optimization, and connectivity across different unlearning methods. We find that many unlearned models lie in connected basins with smooth retain/forget behavior, while changes in training dynamics can move solutions into different basins. MCU also reveals that models within the same basin can differ substantially on privacy metrics, and that unlearning progresses nonlinearly from the original model to the unlearned model. In addition, linear connectivity suggests that most approximate unlearning methods are mechanistically distinct from retraining. Finally, MCU-based ensembling can improve generalization and robustness to relearning attacks, and MCU smoothness correlates with unlearning difficulty. To our knowledge, this is the first study of machine unlearning through the lens of mode connectivity.

中文摘要翻译: 机器遗忘旨在从已训练模型中移除不期望的信息，而无需从头开始完整重训练。尽管近期已有进展，但遗忘过程的损失景观和优化几何特性仍未得到充分理解。本文通过模式连接性——即独立训练的模型通常可以在参数空间中通过平滑的低损耗路径相连的现象——这一视角来研究机器遗忘。我们引入\"遗忘中的模式连接性\"（MCU），并在包括课程学习、二阶优化以及不同遗忘方法之间的连接性等多种设置下对其进行评估。我们发现，许多遗忘模型位于具有平滑保留/遗忘行为的相连盆地中，而训练动态的变化可以将解移入不同的盆地。MCU还揭示，同一盆地内的模型在隐私指标上可能存在显著差异，且遗忘过程从原始模型到遗忘模型呈非线性进展。此外，线性连接性表明，大多数近似遗忘方法与重训练在机制上是不同的。最后，基于MCU的集成可以提升泛化性和对重学习攻击的鲁棒性，并且MCU的平滑性与遗忘难度相关。据我们所知，这是首次通过模式连接性视角对机器遗忘进行的研究。

</details>


### [311] [Adaptive Data Admission and Retention for Streaming Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23987)
*Zhuoyi Zhao,Ben Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究了客户端内存受限的流式联邦学习，提出ACDPP策略在采样成本预算和缓冲区约束下最小化累积超额人口风险，通过有效样本量刻画和在线准入/内存管理机制，实现了亚线性遗憾和成本违规控制。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际联邦学习中，新产生的训练数据带来时变采样成本，且客户端内存有限，需要选择性接纳和保留数据。现有研究缺乏对有限内存下流式数据准入与内存管理的联合优化框架。

Method: 1) 推导基于有效样本量的学习误差界，刻画瞬时训练样本量、不同样本增长和重用不平衡的影响；2) 提出Active-Constraint Drift-Plus-Penalty (ACDPP)策略，结合客户端K步保留规则和服务端在线准入规则及时变矩形准入区域；3) 通过辅助常量准入策略构建比较论证，连接ACDPP学习界与无代价oracle基准。

Result: 理论上证明了亚线性遗憾和采样成本违反保证，并通过离线选择保留视界控制缓冲区占用违反。多数据集实验表明所提策略在满足采样成本和缓冲区约束的同时，性能接近oracle基准。

Conclusion: 提出的ACDPP框架有效解决了客户端内存受限下流式联邦学习中的数据选择性准入与内存管理问题，在理论上提供了严格的性能保证，实验上验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: We study streaming federated learning with limited client memory, where newly generated training data incur time-varying sampling costs and must be selectively admitted and retained over time. We consider a joint server-side admission and client-side memory-management framework with the objective of minimizing the cumulative excess population risk under a sampling-cost budget and buffer constraints. We first derive a learning-error bound that explicitly captures the effects of instantaneous training sample size, distinct-sample growth, and reuse imbalance through a characterization of the effective sample size. Through a surrogate penalty obtained from this bound, we develop an Active-Constraint Drift-Plus-Penalty (ACDPP) policy that combines a structured client-side $K$-step retention rule with a server-side online admission rule and a time-varying rectangular admission region. We further present a sequence of comparison arguments, via an auxiliary constant-admission policy, that connects the ACDPP learning bound to a costless oracle benchmark. This yields explicit guarantees in terms of sublinear regret and sampling-cost violation, while the buffer-occupancy violation is controlled through offline selection of the retention horizon. Experiments on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed policy remains close to the oracle benchmark while satisfying the sampling-cost and buffer constraints.

中文摘要翻译: 我们研究了客户端内存受限的流式联邦学习，其中新生成的训练数据会带来时变的采样成本，并且必须随时间推移有选择地接纳和保留。我们考虑了一个联合的服务端准入和客户端内存管理框架，目标是在采样成本预算和缓冲区约束下最小化累积超额人口风险。我们首先推导了一个学习误差界，通过有效样本量的刻画，明确捕捉了瞬时训练样本量、不同样本增长和重用不平衡的影响。通过该界得到的替代惩罚项，我们开发了一种主动约束漂移加惩罚（ACDPP）策略，该策略将结构化的客户端K步保留规则与服务端在线准入规则及一个时变矩形准入区域相结合。我们进一步通过一个辅助的常量准入策略，提出了一系列比较论证，将ACDPP的学习界与一个无代价的oracle基准联系起来。这给出了亚线性遗憾和采样成本违反方面的明确保证，而缓冲区占用违反则通过离线选择保留视界来控制。在多个数据集上的实验表明，所提出的策略在满足采样成本和缓冲区约束的同时，性能接近oracle基准。

</details>


### [312] [When Should Active RAG Retrieve? A Budget-Aware Evaluation of Utility, Calibration, and Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24010)
*Pin Qian,Su Wang,Chong Peng,Junxian You,Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Yihang Chen,Xiaochong Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对Active RAG系统中预算感知评估不充分的问题，提出了一套多维度的评估框架，包括精确top-k效用前沿、可部署阈值前沿、保守预算前沿、危害审计和成本分解，揭示了现有单点评估的不足，并验证了简单基线方法往往不逊于复杂学习路由器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Active RAG评估方法对操作点（如证据使用预算）的界定不明确，两个系统可能声称相同的预算却实际使用率不同，导致更高准确率可能反映的是更宽松的预算而非更好的检索策略。因此需要建立预算感知的评估框架。

Method: 将主动检索重新建模为效用估计问题——检索的价值仅体现在其对无检索答案的边际正确性变化上。以此为基础提出四类评估工具：精确top-k效用前沿、可部署阈值前沿、保守预算前沿、危害审计与成本分解，分别对应三个分离的评估问题。

Result: 在知识密集型多跳QA数据集和开放指令模型上的实验表明：检索危害不可忽视；路由器排序在不同数据集和预算下发生变化；名义阈值可能达不到目标使用率；简单的不确定性或检索得分基线方法往往不逊于学习的效用路由器。

Conclusion: 预算感知的Active RAG评估应报告前沿曲线、实际使用率、阈值迁移误差、危害率和成本分解，而不能仅报告准确率。

Original Abstract: Active RAG systems decide when to retrieve external knowledge during generation, making them a budget-sensitive case of agentic RAG and self-adaptive retrieval. Yet evaluations often leave the operating point underspecified: two systems may both claim a 50% evidence-usage budget while realizing different held-out usage rates, so higher accuracy can reflect a looser budget rather than a better retrieval policy. We study budget-aware evaluation for Active RAG by recasting active retrieval as utility estimation, where retrieval is valuable only through its marginal correctness change over a no-retrieval answer. This view separates three questions that single-point evaluations conflate: whether trigger scores rank useful retrieval decisions, whether thresholds calibrated on past data meet future budgets, and how trigger-side computation changes deployment cost. We operationalize these questions with exact top-k utility frontiers, deployable threshold frontiers, conservative budget frontiers, harm audits, and cost decompositions. Across knowledge-intensive multi-hop QA datasets and open instruction models, retrieval harm is non-negligible, router rankings change across datasets and budgets, nominal thresholds can miss target usage, and simple uncertainty or retrieval-score baselines often rival learned utility routers. Budget-aware Active RAG evaluations should therefore report frontiers, realized usage, threshold-transfer error, harm rates, and cost decompositions alongside accuracy.

中文摘要翻译: 主动RAG系统在生成过程中决定何时检索外部知识，使其成为智能体RAG和自适应检索中预算敏感的一类情形。然而，现有评估往往对操作点界定不明确：两个系统可能都声称50%的证据使用预算，却实现了不同的实际使用率，因此更高的准确率可能反映的是更宽松的预算而非更优的检索策略。本文通过将主动检索重新建模为效用估计问题来研究Active RAG的预算感知评估——其中检索的价值仅体现在其对无检索答案的边际正确性变化上。这一视角将单点评估混淆的三个问题分离开来：触发分数是否对有用检索决策进行排序、基于历史数据校准的阈值能否满足未来预算、以及触发侧计算如何改变部署成本。我们用精确top-k效用前沿、可部署阈值前沿、保守预算前沿、危害审计和成本分解来操作化这些问题。在知识密集型多跳QA数据集和开放指令模型上，检索危害不可忽略、路由器排序随数据集和预算变化、名义阈值可能达不到目标使用率、简单的不确定性或检索得分基线方法往往不逊于学习的效用路由器。因此，预算感知的Active RAG评估应报告前沿曲线、实际使用率、阈值迁移误差、危害率和成本分解，而不仅仅是准确率。

</details>


### [313] [Capacity-Aware Deep Learning for Generalizable Traffic Volume Estimation Across Links and Cities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24056)
*Léo Hein,Giovanni De Nunzio,Aurélie Pirayre,Laurent Najman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅利用广泛可获取的地域数据（如探头速度、道路拓扑和天气）进行路段级交通流量估计的框架，通过容量感知公式将交通理论约束嵌入学习过程，在空间分布偏移下优于现有基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统网络级交通流量估计依赖固定传感器测量值的传播，性能严重受限于传感器密度，难以部署在传感器稀疏的路网中。本文旨在摆脱对高密度传感器的依赖，仅利用广泛可获取的数据实现准确的交通流量估计。

Method: 提出一个路段级学习框架，从稀疏传感器测量中学习有监督的局部映射。框架将交通流量估计建模为稀疏监督下的空间分布外泛化问题，并引入容量感知公式：将流量分解为路段特定结构容量与小时级的时变利用率之积，将交通理论约束直接嵌入学习过程。

Result: 在两种泛化设置（网络内未见路段和跨网络未见城市）下的广泛实验表明，所提出的结构约束在空间分布偏移下始终优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 将交通理论约束以容量感知形式嵌入学习过程，能够有效提升模型在空间分布偏移下的泛化鲁棒性，为稀疏传感器网络下的交通流量估计提供了一种可行方案。

Original Abstract: Network-wide traffic volume estimation typically relies on propagating measurements from fixed sensors, making performance highly dependent on sensor density and limiting deployment in sparsely instrumented networks. We propose a link-level learning framework that estimates hourly traffic volumes from widely available territorial data only, including probe speed profiles, road and topological descriptors, along with weather observations. A supervised local mapping is learned from sparse sensor measurements and evaluated under two generalization settings: intra-network (unseen links within the training network) and inter-network (unseen city). This formulation frames traffic volume estimation as a spatial out-of-distribution generalization problem under sparse supervision. To enhance spatial robustness, we introduce a capacity-aware formulation that models volume as the product of a link-specific structural capacity and an hourly regime-aware utilization ratio, embedding traffic-theoretic constraints directly into the learning process. Extensive experiments in both generalization settings demonstrate that the proposed structural constraints consistently outperform a state-of-the-art baseline under spatial distribution shift.

中文摘要翻译: 网络级交通流量估计通常依赖于从固定传感器传播测量值，这使得性能高度依赖于传感器密度，并限制了在稀疏传感器网络中的部署。本文提出一个路段级学习框架，仅利用广泛可获取的地域数据（包括探头速度剖面、道路和拓扑描述符以及天气观测）来估计小时级交通流量。该框架从稀疏传感器测量中学习有监督的局部映射，并在两种泛化设置下进行评估：网络内（训练网络中未见的路段）和跨网络（未见城市）。该公式将交通流量估计建模为稀疏监督下的空间分布外泛化问题。为增强空间鲁棒性，我们引入一种容量感知公式，将流量建模为路段特定结构容量与小时级时变利用率之积，从而将交通理论约束直接嵌入学习过程。在两种泛化设置下的大量实验表明，所提出的结构约束在空间分布偏移下始终优于最先进的基线方法。

</details>


### [314] [Constrained Reinforcement Learning Using Successor Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24057)
*Michael Girstl,Alexander Mattick,Christopher Mutschler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Safe Deep Successor Representation (SafeDSR)，通过解耦值函数中的动态、奖励和成本分量，实现高效适应新成本结构的约束强化学习方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有约束强化学习方法难以适应成本函数的变化（如域偏移或障碍物移动），策略缺乏灵活性，无法应对复杂真实世界条件。

Method: SafeDSR扩展了Deep Successor Representation，引入一个可学习的权重矩阵来解耦值函数中的动态、奖励和成本三个分量。当成本结构变化时，只需以监督方式更新该权重矩阵，而无需重新训练整个网络。

Result: 在可配置的二维导航环境中，SafeDSR在简单导航任务上表现具有竞争力，同时在灵活性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: SafeDSR通过解耦学习实现了对成本结构变化的快速适应，为约束强化学习在实际环境中的部署提供了更灵活高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Real-world Reinforcement Learning depends on the ability to formulate safety constraints into a policy. A common way to model such constraints is to introduce an additional cost signal in the Markov Decision Process, which notifies the agent of unwanted behavior independently of the reward signal. Unfortunately, current methods are hard to adapt to changes in the cost function introduced by, e.g., domain shift or obstacles moving over time. The lack of adaptability means that policies are too unflexible to deal with complex real-world conditions. We propose the Safe Deep Successor Representation (SafeDSR), a novel method that allows quick retraining of policies towards new cost structures. SafeDSR extends the Deep Successor Representation (Kulkarni et al., 2016) to Constrained Reinforcement Learning by introducing a single learnable weight matrix to decouple the learned value function across dynamics, rewards, and costs. This matrix can be updated in a supervised manner instead of having to adapt the whole network if the cost structure of the environment changes. We demonstrate this ability in a freely configurable two-dimensional navigation environment and show that our method is competitive on a simple navigation task while being considerably more flexible

中文摘要翻译: 现实世界的强化学习依赖于将安全约束融入策略的能力。建模此类约束的一种常见方式是在马尔可夫决策过程中引入额外的成本信号，该信号独立于奖励信号向智能体通知不当行为。遗憾的是，当前方法难以适应由域偏移或障碍物随时间移动等因素导致的成本函数变化。缺乏适应性意味着策略过于僵化，无法应对复杂的真实世界条件。我们提出了安全深度后继表示（Safe Deep Successor Representation, SafeDSR），一种能够快速重新训练策略以适应新成本结构的新方法。SafeDSR通过引入一个单一的可学习权重矩阵来解耦学习到的值函数中的动态、奖励和成本分量，从而将深度后继表示（Kulkarni et al., 2016）扩展到约束强化学习。当环境成本结构发生变化时，只需以监督方式更新该矩阵，而无需调整整个网络。我们在一个可自由配置的二维导航环境中展示了这一能力，实验表明我们的方法在简单导航任务上具有竞争力，同时灵活性显著更高。

</details>


### [315] [ACRL: Adaptive Control of Training-Inference Discrepancy for Stable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24062)
*Wenwu Fan,Qihong Lin,Zhijie Xia,Zhuo Zheng,Sihao Wang,Qiang Chen,Liangsheng Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对LLM强化学习训练中因训练-推理不一致（架构分离与低精度量化）导致的稳定性问题，提出自适应控制RL方法ACRL，动态维持不一致性在合理范围，同时提升策略熵与探索能力，在FP8量化下稳定训练并达到或超越BF16基线精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: LLM的RL训练常因训练与推理之间的不一致（源于训练/推理引擎架构分离以及推理时低精度量化）而出现训练不稳定问题。

Method: 提出自适应控制强化学习（ACRL），自适应地将训练-推理不一致性控制在合理范围内，从而保证RL训练的稳定性；同时该方法内在增加策略熵，促进探索并提升精度。

Result: 推理引擎使用FP8量化时，ACRL能持续将训练-推理不一致性维持在合理范围内并稳定RL训练；其精度不仅匹配BF16基线，且优于重要性采样（IS）修正方法。

Conclusion: ACRL能有效解决训练-推理不一致导致的RL训练不稳定问题，在低精度量化场景下兼具稳定训练与提升精度的优势。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) training for Large Language Models (LLMs) often suffers from instability due to the discrepancy between training and inference. This training-inference discrepancy stems from two primary factors: an architectural separation between training and inference engines, and the use of low-precision quantization in inference versus higher-precision computation in training. To address training instability issues caused by high training-inference discrepancy, we present the principles and methods for its adaptive control. We propose Adaptive Control Reinforcement Learning (ACRL), which adaptively maintains the training-inference discrepancy within a reasonable range to ensure stable RL training. Beyond stabilization, ACRL inherently increases policy entropy, thereby enhancing exploration and improving accuracy. The experimental results show that when the inference engine utilizes FP8 quantization, ACRL consistently maintains the training-inference discrepancy within a reasonable range and stabilizes RL training. Furthermore, ACRL not only matches the accuracy of the BF16 baseline but also outperforms importance sampling (IS) fixes.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型（LLM）的强化学习（RL）训练常常因训练与推理之间的不一致而出现训练不稳定问题。这种训练-推理不一致主要源于两个因素：训练引擎与推理引擎之间的架构分离，以及推理中采用低精度量化而训练中使用较高精度计算。为了解决由高训练-推理不一致性引发的训练不稳定问题，我们提出了对其自适应控制的原则与方法。我们提出自适应控制强化学习（ACRL），它自适应地将训练-推理不一致性维持在合理范围内，以确保RL训练的稳定性。除了稳定训练外，ACRL还内在增加了策略熵，从而增强了探索能力并提升了精度。实验结果表明，当推理引擎采用FP8量化时，ACRL能够持续将训练-推理不一致性维持在合理范围内，并稳定RL训练。此外，ACRL不仅匹配了BF16基线的精度，还优于重要性采样（IS）修正方法。

</details>


### [316] [Learning Reusable Hybrid Motion Priors for Humanoid Locomotion from Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24083)
*Valerio Belli,Valerio Modugno,Enrico Mingo Hoffman,Fabio Amadio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三阶段流水线，将运动模仿技能蒸馏为可重用的混合运动先验（HMP），用于人形机器人运动控制，无需为每个新任务重新训练完整策略。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 强化学习训练的人形机器人控制器针对每个新任务需要单独设计奖励和训练过程，而运动模仿训练的控制器仅是参考跟踪器，无法直接作为任务策略使用。需要一种可重用的运动先验来桥接模仿学习与任务学习。

Method: 三阶段流水线：(1) 训练专家策略模仿重定向的人体运动捕捉数据；(2) 将专家蒸馏为冻结架构，包含本体感受编码器、残差向量量化（RVQ）码本和动作解码器；(3) 训练任务级策略通过选择离散码本条目解决运动任务，同时保持HMP冻结。

Result: 在速度跟踪、点目标导航和跌倒恢复速度跟踪任务上验证效果，并在真实Unitree G1机器人上部署速度跟踪策略。蒸馏保留了专家跟踪行为，HMP可重用无需重新训练。RVQ码本具有可解释结构，活跃阶段数量调节可用步态模式。旋转技巧改善了潜在组织并减少了跌倒。

Conclusion: 所提出的HMP能够从运动模仿中提取可重用的运动先验，作为不同下游运动策略的动作接口，且码本具有可解释性，旋转技巧提升了学习效果。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning can produce robust humanoid controllers, but each new task is typically trained as a separate policy with its own reward design and training process. Motion imitation provides an alternative source of motor competence by training policies to track retargeted human motions, yet the resulting controllers remain reference trackers and are not directly usable as task policies. We propose a three-stage pipeline that turns motion-imitation skills into a reusable hybrid motion prior (HMP) for humanoid locomotion. First, an expert policy is trained to imitate retargeted human motion-capture clips. Second, the expert is distilled into a frozen architecture composed of a proprioceptive encoder, a residual vector-quantized (RVQ) codebook, and an action decoder. Third, task-level policies are trained to solve locomotion tasks by selecting discrete codebook entries while the HMP remains frozen. We evaluate the method on velocity tracking, point-goal navigation, and fall-recovery velocity tracking in simulation, and deploy the velocity-tracking policy on a real Unitree G1 robot. The distillation process preserves the tracking behavior of the expert, while the resulting HMP can be reused without retraining as the action interface for different downstream locomotion policies. The learned HMP reveals an interpretable codebook structure in which the number of active RVQ stages modulates the available gait patterns. We further show that training the codebook with the rotation trick improves latent organization and reduces downstream falls compared with a standard straight-through estimator.

中文摘要翻译: 强化学习可以生成鲁棒的人形控制器，但每个新任务通常需要单独设计奖励和训练过程来训练独立的策略。运动模仿通过训练策略跟踪重定向的人体运动来提供另一种运动能力来源，但由此产生的控制器仍然是参考跟踪器，不能直接用作任务策略。我们提出一个三阶段流水线，将运动模仿技能转化为可重用的混合运动先验（HMP），用于人形机器人 locomotion。首先，训练一个专家策略来模仿重定向的人体运动捕捉片段。其次，将专家蒸馏为一个冻结架构，该架构由本体感受编码器、残差向量量化（RVQ）码本和动作解码器组成。第三，训练任务级策略通过选择离散码本条目来解决运动任务，同时保持HMP冻结。我们在仿真中评估了该方法在速度跟踪、点目标导航和跌倒恢复速度跟踪任务上的表现，并在真实的Unitree G1机器人上部署了速度跟踪策略。蒸馏过程保留了专家的跟踪行为，而得到的HMP可以作为不同下游运动策略的动作接口直接复用，无需重新训练。学习得到的HMP揭示了可解释的码本结构，其中活跃的RVQ阶段数量调节了可用的步态模式。我们进一步证明，与标准的直通估计器相比，使用旋转技巧训练码本可以改善潜在空间组织并减少下游跌倒。

</details>


### [317] [MAPLE: Efficient and Diverse Multi-Alpha Generation for Portfolio Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24131)
*Yu-Chen Den,Kuan-Yu Chen,Kendro Vincent,Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPLE是一种不依赖特定骨干架构的框架，通过统一预测头、极值排序损失和多样化正则化，在单次训练中生成多个低相关性的股票Alpha信号，在四个股票市场上以更少的参数和训练时间实现了优于九个基线的收益风险表现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统量化alpha挖掘通过组合多个低相关预测信号实现强风险调整收益，但深度学习的股票排序方法通常为每只股票生成单一alpha，依赖日益复杂且收益递减的架构，多样性仅通过分离模型或隐式路由获得，缺乏对alpha间相关性的显式控制。

Method: MAPLE包含三个核心组件：1) 统一、容量可扩展的预测头生成多个alpha；2) 极值权重排序损失（extreme-rank weighted listwise ranking loss）；3) 显式惩罚alpha间成对相关性的多样性正则化项。该框架是骨干架构无关的，可在单次训练中完成。

Result: 在美国、中国和日本四个股票市场上，MAPLE在九个基线方法中取得最佳平均夏普比率和卡尔玛比率，参数最多减少55倍，训练时间减少2.5倍。在五种骨干架构上泛化，夏普比率和卡尔玛比率分别提升10%-23%和17%-43%。

Conclusion: 合理的损失函数设计和容量分配（而非架构复杂性）是驱动多样化且有效的多alpha生成的关键。统一头在应用多样性损失前已降低alpha间相关性，极值排序损失使多样性正则化改善而非侵蚀每个alpha的排序质量，容量扩展则维持了这种平衡。

Original Abstract: Classical alpha mining achieves strong risk-adjusted returns by combining many low-correlated predictive signals, yet deep learning stock-ranking methods typically produce a single alpha per stock, rely on increasingly complex architectures with diminishing gains, and obtain diversity only through separate models or implicit routing, without explicitly controlling inter-alpha correlation. We introduce MAPLE (Multi-Alpha Position-aware Listwise Ensembling), a backbone-agnostic framework that recovers this diversity principle within a single training pass. MAPLE combines a unified, capacity-scaled prediction head with an extreme-rank weighted listwise ranking loss and a diversity regularizer that explicitly penalizes pairwise correlation across alphas. Across four equity markets spanning the US, China, and Japan, MAPLE achieves the best average Sharpe and Calmar ratios among nine baselines, using up to 55x fewer parameters and 2.5x less training time, and generalizes across five backbone architectures with Sharpe and Calmar Ratio gains of 10-23% and 17-43%, respectively. Behavioral analysis further shows why each component works: the unified head already reduces inter-alpha correlation before any diversity loss is applied, and the extreme-rank loss lets diversity regularization improve rather than erode per-alpha ranking quality as capacity scaling sustains this balance at scale. These results show that principled loss design and capacity allocation, rather than architectural complexity, drive diverse and effective multi-alpha generation.

中文摘要翻译: 经典量化alpha挖掘通过组合多个低相关性的预测信号实现强劲的风险调整收益，然而深度学习的股票排序方法通常为每只股票生成单一alpha，依赖日益复杂但收益递减的架构，多样性仅通过独立模型或隐式路由获得，缺乏对alpha间相关性的显式控制。我们提出MAPLE（多Alpha位置感知列表集成），这是一种不依赖特定骨干架构的框架，在单次训练过程中恢复这一多样性原则。MAPLE结合了统一的、容量可扩展的预测头、极值权重排序损失以及显式惩罚各alpha间成对相关性的多样性正则化项。在涵盖美国、中国和日本的四个股票市场中，MAPLE在九个基线方法中取得了最佳的平均夏普比率和卡尔玛比率，参数最多减少55倍，训练时间减少2.5倍，并在五种骨干架构上表现出良好的泛化能力，夏普比率和卡尔玛比率分别提升10%-23%和17%-43%。行为分析进一步揭示了每个组件的作用机理：统一头在施加任何多样性损失之前就已经降低了alpha间的相关性；极值排序损失使得多样性正则化能够改善而非侵蚀每个alpha的排序质量，而容量扩展则在规模上维持了这种平衡。这些结果表明，合理的损失函数设计和容量分配——而非架构复杂性——是驱动多样化且有效的多alpha生成的关键。

</details>


### [318] [An Empirical Study of Feature Selection Granularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24145)
*Muhammad Rajabinasab,Arthur Zimek*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究特征选择中的算法设计视角，通过实验比较全局排序与贪心递归消除两种策略，发现贪心递归方法虽计算成本更高，但几乎一致性地提升了特征选择质量，证实了“维度灾难”会掩盖特征重要性这一假设。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有特征选择研究多集中于开发新算法或基准测试，但忽略了算法设计范式本身的影响。作者质疑：全局一次性排名的方式是否会因存在噪声特征而遮蔽其他重要特征的真实重要性？递归消除策略是否会带来更好的结果？

Method: 选取五种不同的特征选择算法，每种算法分别在两种设计范式下实现：(1) 传统的全局选择设计（一次性计算重要性并排名选特征）；(2) 贪心递归消除设计（每步移除一个特征后重新评估重要性）。在标准特征选择评估指标上进行系统性实验对比。

Result: 贪心递归消除方法在几乎所有的算法和评估指标上一致性地提升了特征选择质量，但代价是更高的计算开销。实验结果支持了“维度灾难会掩盖其缓解方式”的初始预期。

Conclusion: 算法设计范式（全局一次性选择 vs. 贪心递归消除）对特征选择质量有显著影响；贪心递归方法能在多数情况下取得更好的特征子集，尽管计算成本更高，这提示研究者应重视特征选择中的算法设计选择。

Original Abstract: Feature selection aims to identify the most informative and relevant features for a given dataset, either in terms of capturing the underlying data structure and distribution better, or with respect to the performance on a downstream task. Existing research in this area has largely focused on developing novel algorithms (in both supervised and unsupervised settings), proposing new evaluation metrics and frameworks, or benchmarking the performance of existing methods. In this work, we examine feature selection through an algorithmic design perspective. Conventional feature selection algorithms typically compute feature importance scores globally across the entire feature set and then select the top-ranked features in a single step. However, this approach raises a critical question: Can the presence of less informative (or noisy) features mask or obscure the true importance of other, more relevant features? In other words, would a recursive strategy, where features are removed one by one while re-evaluating importance at each step, yield different and potentially better results than the standard global ranking approach? To answer this question, we conduct an extensive empirical study using five diverse feature selection algorithms. We implement each algorithm under both the conventional global selection design and the greedy recursive elimination design. We then analyze the impact of this algorithmic choice, both individually for each method and collectively across all methods, on a range of standard feature selection evaluation metrics. The empirical evaluation results show that the greedy approach improves the overall feature selection quality almost consistently, albeit on the expense of higher computational cost, supporting our initial expectation that the curse of dimensionality also obscures the ways of mitigating it.

中文摘要翻译: 特征选择旨在为给定数据集识别最具信息量和最相关的特征，无论是为了更好地捕捉底层数据结构和分布，还是针对下游任务的性能。该领域的现有研究主要集中在开发新算法（包括监督和无监督设置）、提出新的评估指标和框架，或对现有方法进行基准测试。在这项工作中，我们从算法设计的角度审视特征选择。传统的特征选择算法通常在整个特征集上全局计算特征重要性分数，然后一步选出排名靠前的特征。然而，这种方法提出了一个关键问题：信息量较少（或噪声）特征的存在是否会掩盖或模糊其他更相关特征的真实重要性？换句话说，一种递归策略——即每次移除一个特征并在每一步重新评估重要性——能否产生比标准全局排名方法不同且可能更好的结果？为了回答这个问题，我们使用五种不同的特征选择算法进行了广泛的实证研究。我们在传统的全局选择设计和贪心递归消除设计下分别实现了每种算法。然后，我们分析了这种算法选择的影响——既针对每种方法单独分析，也在所有方法上综合分析——考察其对一系列标准特征选择评估指标的作用。实证评估结果表明，贪心方法几乎一致地提升了整体特征选择质量，尽管代价是更高的计算成本，这支持了我们最初的预期：维度灾难同样掩盖了缓解维度灾难的方法。

</details>


### [319] [Calibrated Tree-Neural Fusion for Fine-Grained Vegetation Community Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24160)
*Dristi Datta,Md Khalid Hasan Sakib,Manoranjan Paul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Calibrated EcoTreeFuseNet-Plus框架，融合树模型概率、神经网络输出和温度缩放校准，在29类植被分类任务上达到80%准确率，校准误差从0.3866降至0.0651，五折评估宏F1为0.7717±0.0112，实现了小样本细粒度生态分类的可靠判别-校准权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有植被群落分类方法依赖单一的树集成或通用神经网络，难以处理细粒度生态类别中光谱、地形和结构特征重叠的问题；同时存在堆叠泄漏防护不足、概率校准不充分、少数类评估薄弱以及跨重复数据分割稳定性缺乏验证等局限。

Method: 提出Calibrated EcoTreeFuseNet-Plus概率融合框架：①从六种LiDAR地形/冠层变量和两种高光谱植被指数中提取栅格值；②融合out-of-fold树概率与EcoFuseNet-V2输出；③验证集选择的元学习；④事后温度缩放校准。经质量控制后保留1,833条完整记录，涵盖29个植被与非植被类别。

Result: 在留出测试集上准确率0.8000，宏F1为0.7768，平衡准确率0.7903，MCC为0.7903；校准误差从0.3866降至0.0651且不改变类别预测；五种子评估宏F1为0.7717±0.0112，展示了稳定的跨分割性能。

Conclusion: 该方法在小样本细粒度生态分类任务中实现了可靠的判别-校准权衡，有效解决了现有方法概率校准不足和稳定性缺乏的问题。

Original Abstract: Accurate vegetation-community classification is essential for ecological monitoring, habitat assessment, and evidence-based environmental management in heterogeneous landscapes. Existing studies often rely on standalone tree ensembles or generic neural networks, although fine-grained ecological classes frequently exhibit overlapping spectral, topographic, and structural characteristics. Many frameworks also provide limited protection against stacking leakage, insufficient probability calibration, weak minority-class evaluation, and little evidence of stability across repeated data splits. To address these limitations, this study proposes Calibrated EcoTreeFuseNet-Plus, a tree-neural probability-fusion framework that combines out-of-fold tree probabilities, EcoFuseNet-V2 outputs, validation-selected meta-learning, and post-hoc temperature scaling. Raster values from six LiDAR-derived terrain and canopy variables and two hyperspectral vegetation indices were extracted at coordinate-based reference locations. Quality control removed 26 samples with missing elevation and one sample with non-finite NDWI, producing 1,833 complete records across 29 vegetation and non-vegetation classes. On the held-out test set, the proposed model achieved an accuracy of 0.8000, a macro F1-score of 0.7768, a balanced accuracy of 0.7903, and an MCC of 0.7903. Calibration reduced the expected calibration error from 0.3866 to 0.0651 without changing class predictions. Five-seed evaluation yielded a macro F1-score of 0.7717 +/- 0.0112, indicating stable performance across repeated splits. The results demonstrate a reliable discrimination-calibration trade-off for small-sample, fine-grained ecological classification.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的植被群落分类对于异质景观中的生态监测、栖息地评估和循证环境管理至关重要。现有研究通常依赖单一的树集成或通用神经网络，然而细粒度生态类别往往表现出重叠的光谱、地形和结构特征。许多框架对堆叠泄漏的防护有限，概率校准不充分，少数类评估薄弱，且缺乏跨重复数据分割的稳定性证据。为解决上述局限，本研究提出Calibrated EcoTreeFuseNet-Plus——一种树-神经概率融合框架，结合了out-of-fold树概率、EcoFuseNet-V2输出、验证集选择的元学习和事后温度缩放校准。在基于坐标的参考位置处提取了六种LiDAR衍生的地形与冠层变量以及两种高光谱植被指数的栅格值。质量控制移除了26个缺失高程的样本和1个NDWI非有限的样本，最终在29个植被与非植被类别中获得1,833条完整记录。在留出测试集上，所提模型取得了0.8000的准确率、0.7768的宏F1、0.7903的平衡准确率和0.7903的MCC。校准将期望校准误差从0.3866降至0.0651，且不改变类别预测。五种子评估得到宏F1为0.7717±0.0112，表明模型在重复分割上性能稳定。结果表明，该方法在小样本细粒度生态分类中实现了可靠的判别-校准权衡。

</details>


### [320] [Forecasting the Emergence and Evolution of Crash Hotspots: A Unified Deep Learning Framework for Proactive Traffic Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24168)
*Jingwen Zhu,Keshu Wu,Pei Li,Steven T. Parker,Bin Ran,David A. Noyce*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HERALD是一个统一的深度学习框架，能够同时检测新兴热点、预测下周风险位置并追踪热点生命周期，将热点管理从"回顾过去"转变为"预见未来"。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统事故热点管理基于历史事故地图指导执法，但热点具有动态涌现和消退的特性，这种滞后方法只能巡逻"昨天的热点"，而无法应对正在形成的新热点。打破这一滞后需要同时具备三种能力：检测刚诞生的热点、预测下周热点位置、追踪热点整个生命周期。

Method: HERALD框架将每个县近期的碰撞历史提炼为每周风险图，使用CNN-Transformer进行下周风险预测，其中混合专家（Mixture-of-Experts）机制使单一模型能够同时服务密集城市核心区和稀疏农村走廊。每个预测锚定在县的长期碰撞地理分布上，通过近期碰撞的自激发效应进行锐化，并配以新热点即将出现的明确预警。

Result: 在威斯康星州六个异质性县上，HERALD在预测准确性、热点定位精度和新兴风险预警能力上均优于五个同等训练设置的基线方法。一个可调节参数允许在部署需要时以精度换取更高的灵敏度。

Conclusion: HERALD将热点管理从映射过去转变为预测未来，实现了对事故热点动态生命周期的主动感知与前瞻性干预。

Original Abstract: Road crashes remain among the gravest threats to public safety, and preventing them is a defining task of transportation systems worldwide. Much of that harm concentrates at hotspots, yet a hotspot is less a place than an episode; it emerges quietly at an intersection or along an arterial, intensifies for weeks, then subsides, only to reappear elsewhere. Enforcement guided by maps of past crashes inevitably trails this cycle, patrolling yesterday's hotspots while tomorrow's form unwatched. Breaking that lag requires three capabilities at once: detecting hotspots as they are born, forecasting where they will sit next week, and following each one through its life. We introduce HERALD (Hotspot Emergence, Risk Anticipation, and Life-cycle Dynamics), a unified deep learning framework that provides all three from a single statewide model. HERALD distills each county's recent crash history into weekly risk maps and forecasts the next with a CNN--Transformer, whose mixture-of-experts lets one model serve dense urban cores and sparse rural corridors alike.
